# ng-spotlight 21.0.0 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build and release-prep `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight` 21.0.0: a keyboard-first command palette for Angular 21 with a headless core, a `/primeng` secondary entry point, a JSON Schema for the HTTP format, a GitHub Pages kitchen sink, and hardened CI/release/pages workflows.

**Architecture:** A pure RxJS search engine (`engine.ts`) fans a query out to registered sources, ranks local results, and streams grouped state. `SpotlightService` (provided by `provideSpotlight`) owns state as signals, runs actions and keeps Recent items. `<spotlight-host>` owns the CDK overlay, focus, hotkeys and every ARIA attribute, and renders the visual parts (`input`, `chips`, `groupHeader`, `row`, `badge`) through components named by the `SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER` token. The PrimeNG adapter only swaps those part components.

**Tech Stack:** Angular 21.2.x (standalone, signals, zoneless), @angular/cdk 21.2.x (overlay, a11y, layout), RxJS 7.8, PrimeNG 21.1.x, ng-packagr 21.2.x, vitest 4 + @analogjs/vite-plugin-angular + jsdom, axe-core, ajv, esbuild (size check), marked + prismjs (kitchen sink), `@tocdk/lingui-angular` v0.2.1 (kitchen sink only).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-02-ng-spotlight-design.md` (read it before starting any task).

**Out of scope:** The Tivedo FR-087 integration (spec §10: admin shell wiring, `/api/admin/search`, the `hiddenFromNav` CI rule) gets its own plan in the Tivedo repo after 21.0.0 is published. Registering the repo in the tocDK/team harness (Rex ledger, board licenses panel, Ada review, self-hosted runner, spec §8 last bullet) is team-repo work and is not planned here. No task publishes to npm: the release workflow runs only when the operator pushes a `v21.0.0` tag.

**Reference project:** `/home/toc/git/team/.runtime/rex/src/tocDK__lingui-angular` (sibling `@tocdk/lingui-angular`). Its `main` has since moved to Angular 22, so this plan uses the Angular 21.2 equivalents of its tooling (the same versions the Tivedo consumer app runs) and keeps its conventions: vitest projects with a `.js` setup file, ng-packagr partial compilation, `content/*.content.ts` docs modules checked by `scripts/check-docs.mjs`, example sources generated by `scripts/generate-example-sources.mjs`, and `--base-href "/ng-spotlight/"` for GitHub Pages.

**Branch:** Do all tasks on a feature branch `toc/ng-spotlight-v21` in a worktree (superpowers:using-git-worktrees), one commit per task, then one PR into `main`.

## Global Constraints

- Package `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight` (MIT), repo `https://github.com/tocDK/ng-spotlight`.
- Entry points: `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight` (headless core; peers `@angular/core`, `@angular/common`, `@angular/router`, `@angular/cdk`) and `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/primeng` (PrimeNG renderer adapter; `primeng` is an optional peer). "Apps that don't use PrimeNG never resolve it."
- "Versions follow Angular majors": `21.x` supports Angular 21. First release: `21.0.0`.
- Toolchain: Angular `^21.2.25`, @angular/cdk `^21.2.14`, @angular/build and @angular/cli `^21.2.29`, ng-packagr `^21.2.7`, primeng `^21.1.10`, TypeScript `~5.9.3`, Node 22, vitest `^4.1.11`.
- "Size target: core at or under 15 kB gzipped." Measured by `scripts/check-size.mjs`: the core FESM bundle minified by esbuild with `@angular/*`, `rxjs` and `tslib` external, then gzipped, must be at most 15360 bytes.
- Default hotkeys `['ctrl.space', 'mod.k']`; "'mod' = Cmd on macOS, Ctrl elsewhere". Hotkeys "ignore IME composition".
- Source defaults: `minLength` "default 0 for local sources, 2 for http"; `debounceMs` "default 0 for local sources, 150 for http"; `timeoutMs` "default 5000"; `priority` "lower comes first; default = registration order".
- Default HTTP request: "`GET {url}?q={query}`, plus `chip={groupId}` when a chip other than All is active". Per-source `request`/`map` win over global `http` options, which win over the defaults.
- Route label precedence: "route `title` (string), then `data.spotlight.label`, then `data.breadcrumb`". "Routes with a `:param` segment are skipped." Opt-out: `data: { spotlight: false }`.
- Ranking: "exact match, then a match at the start of the title, then a match at the start of a word, then subsequence order. Keywords count at a lower weight, and items in Recent get a small boost. HTTP results keep the server's order."
- `children` on `SpotlightItem` is "reserved for nested pages (v1.x); must be absent in v1".
- Text keys: `placeholder`, `all`, `recent`, `noResults`, `loading`, `error`, `retry`, `resultsAnnouncement` (a function of count), `hintNavigate`, `hintOpen`, `hintClose`. "The library has no i18n dependency."
- "Phone width (at most 600 px): a full-screen sheet."
- Theme: "one stylesheet using CSS variables only (`--sp-bg`, `--sp-fg`, `--sp-muted`, `--sp-accent`, `--sp-radius`, `--sp-font`, `--sp-shadow`, …), with a dark-mode variant. `theme: 'none'` ships no CSS."
- Accessibility: "WAI-ARIA combobox pattern, works fully from the keyboard, and passes axe with zero violations." The PrimeNG adapter "must produce the same accessibility tree as the core, and a test enforces this."
- "Core coverage must be at least 90%." (vitest thresholds: lines/functions/statements 90, branches 85, as in lingui-angular.)
- Workflow hygiene: "every action pinned to a commit SHA, `persist-credentials: false`, least-privilege `permissions:` per job, and no untrusted `${{ }}` expansion in `run:`."
- Pinned actions (looked up 2026-11-02 with `gh api repos/<owner>/<action>/git/ref/tags/<tag>`; all lightweight tags): `actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1`, `actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0`, `actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2`, `actions/upload-pages-artifact@fc324d3547104276b827a68afc52ff2a11cc49c9 # v5.0.0`, `actions/deploy-pages@368f82528645a54fb793d4d04e342629a3f51346 # v5.0.1`.
- Kitchen sink "Published at `https://tocdk.github.io/ng-spotlight/`. Every page shows its own source, generated from the real example files".
- Code style: standalone components, `ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush` in the library, signals (`input()`, `computed()`, `contentChildren()`), no `NgModule`s, no zone.js at runtime. Selector prefixes: components `spotlight-` (library) and `app-` (kitchen sink), directives `spotlight` camelCase.
- Every commit message ends with the two lines:
  `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`
  `Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01KPPoJVjcRbL7ZMXrfPVNwo`

## Review Focus

1. **Unsafe URL actions from server data** (`{ url: 'javascript:…' }`, `'java\nscript:…'`, `'data:text/html,…'`): the item must never navigate or open a tab; `onError` is called, the palette closes, and the item is not stored in Recent. Test: Task 7 "blocks unsafe url schemes instead of navigating".
2. **Malformed source payloads** (`{ groups: null }`, items without `id`/`title`/`action`, items that set `children`, groups without `id`, `search()` throwing synchronously): only that source's group shows the error row (or the invalid items are dropped); every other group still renders. Tests: Task 2 "drops invalid items and groups", Task 6 "isolates malformed payloads to their own group".
3. **Broken Recent storage** (storage blocked, `load()` rejects, `save()` throws synchronously, stored data from an older version or another app): the palette opens and works with an empty Recent list and never throws. Tests: Task 3 "localStorageRecent survives blocked storage and foreign data", Task 7 "survives a recent storage that rejects, throws or returns garbage".
4. **Hotkey repeat and re-open while open** (holding Ctrl+Space, calling `open()` twice, open/close/open in one tick): exactly one overlay exists, and focus returns to the element that had it before the first open. Test: Task 9 "keeps a single overlay under key repeat and repeated open()".
5. **A `run()` action that rejects**: `onError(err, source)` is called, the palette closes, `selected` does not emit, and the item is not added to Recent. Test: Task 7 "reports a rejecting run() and keeps it out of Recent".

---

## Decisions this plan makes where the spec is silent or self-contradictory

Each is reflected in the task that owns it. The operator can veto any of them before execution.

- **D-P1 PrimeNG dialog (spec §7.1 vs §7.3).** §7.1 makes the core own the CDK overlay (backdrop, Esc, focus trap, focus restore); §7.3 maps the `dialog` part to `p-dialog`, which brings its own mask, focus trap and positioning. Running both would double the overlay and break "same accessibility tree". The plan keeps the CDK overlay and makes the `dialog` part a pair of classes (`{ panelClass, backdropClass? }`). The PrimeNG adapter's classes remap the `--sp-*` variables to PrimeNG design tokens (`--p-content-background`, `--p-primary-color`, …), so white-label themes still apply, and the other parts use real PrimeNG components (`p-iconfield` + `pInputText`, `p-chip`, `p-tag`, `pi` icons).
- **D-P2 `SpotlightItem.group` is required, but `SpotlightSource.group` is "default group for items that do not set one".** `staticSource` takes `SpotlightItemInput` (`group` optional); the engine fills a missing `group` from the source for every source.
- **D-P3 Recent storage cannot serialize `run()`.** `SpotlightRecentStorage` stores `SpotlightRecentEntry` (id, title, group, sourceId, subtitle, icon, badges, and `action` only for `route`/`url`), not `SpotlightItem`. `run()` entries have no `action` and are resolved through their source by id.
- **D-P4 Texts as Signals need an injection context.** `provideSpotlight()` also accepts a factory `() => SpotlightConfig`, run in the environment injector, so `texts` can come from `inject(LinguiService).t$(…)`.
- **D-P5 Several independent palettes on one page (kitchen sink).** `SpotlightConfig.isolated?: boolean` makes `provideSpotlight` create its own `SpotlightService` instead of adding sources to the parent's.
- **D-P6 `resultsAnnouncement` is a function, not a text value.** It is called inside a `computed`, so it is reactive when it reads signals.
- **D-P7 `SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER` is called "internal" but the "starter renderer adapter" needs it.** It is exported, together with the part context types and the default part components.
- **D-P8 "axe checks on the open overlay in both themes".** Read as: the default theme in light and dark (`sp-dark`), plus the PrimeNG adapter. jsdom cannot compute colour contrast, so contrast is not machine-checked; the CSS uses ≥ 4.5:1 pairs.
- **D-P9 Unsafe URL schemes.** `url` actions only allow `http`, `https`, `mailto`, `tel` and relative URLs; anything else is blocked and reported (Review Focus 1).
- **D-P10 Hotkey toggles.** Pressing the hotkey while the palette is open closes it; auto-repeat events are ignored.
- **D-P11 `rxjs` is a peer dependency** (`^7.8.0`); the spec's table lists only the Angular packages, but every Angular library needs it.
- **D-P12 Lazy routes.** `routeSource` reads lazily loaded children through Angular's internal `Route._loadedRoutes`, the only place the Router keeps them. A test pins the behaviour so an Angular upgrade that changes it fails loudly.
- **D-P13 `routeSource` extras.** Each item gets `subtitle` = its URL; `data.spotlight.suggested: true` marks a route as suggested on the empty query.
- **D-P14 Chips.** The chip list is captured from the groups shown while "All" is active and stays fixed while a chip is active, so the chips don't collapse to one.
- **D-P15 Kitchen-sink shell.** A plain-CSS shell (no Angular Material, no SSR) with the same docs mechanics as lingui-angular, plus an "API reference" page so `check:docs` can require every export to be documented.

### Task 1: Workspace scaffold and toolchain

**Files:**
- Create: `package.json`, `angular.json`, `tsconfig.base.json`, `tsconfig.json`, `tsconfig.spec.json`, `vitest.config.ts`, `vitest.setup.js`, `eslint.config.js`, `.prettierrc`, `.editorconfig`, `.nvmrc`, `.gitignore`
- Create: `projects/ng-spotlight/package.json`, `projects/ng-spotlight/ng-package.json`, `projects/ng-spotlight/tsconfig.lib.json`, `projects/ng-spotlight/tsconfig.lib.prod.json`
- Create: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/public-api.ts`, `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/version.ts`
- Create: `projects/kitchen-sink/tsconfig.app.json`, `projects/kitchen-sink/src/index.html`, `projects/kitchen-sink/src/main.ts`, `projects/kitchen-sink/src/styles.css`, `projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/app.ts`
- Test: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/version.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces: `VERSION: Version` (from `@angular/core`) exported from `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight`; path aliases `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight` → `projects/ng-spotlight/src/public-api.ts` and `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/primeng` → `projects/ng-spotlight/primeng/src/public-api.ts` in both `tsconfig.base.json` and `vitest.config.ts`; vitest projects `lib` and `demo`; npm scripts `test`, `test:lib`, `test:demo`, `test:coverage`, `lint`, `build:lib`, `build:demo`, `start`.

`vitest.config.ts`:

````ts
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import angular from '@analogjs/vite-plugin-angular';
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';

const fromRoot = (path: string) => fileURLToPath(new URL(path, import.meta.url));

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [angular({ tsconfig: './tsconfig.spec.json' })],
  resolve: {
    // Vite does not read tsconfig "paths"; mirror them here. The more specific alias goes first.
    alias: [
      {
        find: /^@tocdk-oss\/ng-spotlight\/primeng$/,
        replacement: fromRoot('./projects/ng-spotlight/primeng/src/public-api.ts'),
      },
      { find: /^@tocdk-oss\/ng-spotlight$/, replacement: fromRoot('./projects/ng-spotlight/src/public-api.ts') },
    ],
  },
  test: {
    globals: true,
    passWithNoTests: true,
    coverage: {
      provider: 'v8',
      include: ['projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/**/*.ts', 'projects/ng-spotlight/primeng/src/**/*.ts'],
      exclude: ['**/*.spec.ts', '**/*.spec-util.ts', '**/public-api.ts', '**/index.ts'],
      thresholds: { lines: 90, branches: 85, functions: 90, statements: 90 },
    },
    projects: [
      {
        extends: true,
        test: {
          name: 'lib',
          environment: 'jsdom',
          include: ['projects/ng-spotlight/**/*.spec.ts'],
          // setupFiles must be explicit per project (not reliably inherited with the Angular
          // plugin), and must be .js so the Angular transform does not swallow it.
          setupFiles: ['./vitest.setup.js'],
        },
      },
      {
        extends: true,
        test: {
          name: 'demo',
          environment: 'jsdom',
          include: ['projects/kitchen-sink/src/**/*.spec.ts'],
          setupFiles: ['./vitest.setup.js'],
        },
      },
    ],
  },
});
````

`vitest.setup.js`:

````js
// Plain JS setup: initializes the Angular TestBed. Every TestBed in this repo also adds
// provideZonelessChangeDetection() to its providers.
````

````html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <title>@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight kitchen sink</title>
    <base href="/" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
    <script>
      // Apply the persisted theme before Angular boots so dark-mode visitors see no flash.
      (function () {
        try {
          var saved = localStorage.getItem('kitchen-sink:theme');
          var prefersDark = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
          if (saved ? saved === 'dark' : prefersDark) document.documentElement.classList.add('sp-dark');
          else document.documentElement.classList.add('sp-light');
        } catch (_) {
          /* localStorage may throw in private mode */
        }
      })();
    </script>
  </head>
  <body>
    <app-root></app-root>
  </body>
</html>
````

### Task 2: Public types, result normalisation, JSON Schema

**Files:**
- Create: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/types.ts`
- Create: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/normalize.ts`
- Create: `projects/ng-spotlight/schema/spotlight-response.schema.json`
- Modify: `projects/ng-spotlight/ng-package.json` (ship the schema), `projects/ng-spotlight/package.json` (export it), `projects/ng-spotlight/src/public-api.ts`
- Test: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/normalize.spec.ts`, `projects/ng-spotlight/schema/schema.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces (all exported from `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight` except where marked internal):
  - `type SpotlightBadgeTone = 'info' | 'success' | 'warn' | 'danger'`; `interface SpotlightBadge { label: string; tone?: SpotlightBadgeTone }`
  - `type SpotlightAction = { route: unknown[] | string } | { url: string; newTab?: boolean } | { run: () => void | Promise<void> }`
  - `interface SpotlightItem { id; title; subtitle?; icon?; group: string; keywords?; badges?; suggested?; data?: unknown; action: SpotlightAction; children?: never }`
  - `type SpotlightItemInput = Omit<SpotlightItem, 'group'> & { group?: string }`
  - `interface SpotlightGroup { id: string; label: string; total?: number; items: SpotlightItem[] }`, `interface SpotlightResponse { groups: SpotlightGroup[] }`, `type SpotlightResult = SpotlightItem[] | SpotlightResponse`
  - `interface SpotlightContext { signal: AbortSignal; chip: string | null; injector: Injector }`
  - `interface SpotlightSource { id; group?; prefix?; minLength?; debounceMs?; timeoutMs?; priority?; local?; search(query: string, ctx: SpotlightContext): Observable<SpotlightResult> | Promise<SpotlightResult> | SpotlightResult }`
  - `type SpotlightTextValue = string | Signal<string> | Observable<string>`; `interface SpotlightTexts` (keys from Global Constraints; `resultsAnnouncement: (count: number) => string`)
  - `interface SpotlightHttpRequest { method?; params?; headers?; body? }`, `interface SpotlightHttpOptions { request?: (query, ctx) => SpotlightHttpRequest; map?: (response: unknown, query: string) => SpotlightResult }`
  - `type SpotlightStoredAction`, `interface SpotlightRecentEntry { id; title; group; sourceId; subtitle?; icon?; badges?; action?: SpotlightStoredAction }`, `interface SpotlightRecentStorage { load(): Promise<SpotlightRecentEntry[]>; save(items: SpotlightRecentEntry[]): Promise<void> }`
  - `interface SpotlightConfig { hotkeys?; texts?: Partial<SpotlightTexts>; recent?: { max?; storage? }; http?: SpotlightHttpOptions; theme?: 'default' | 'none'; onError?: (error: unknown, source: SpotlightSource) => void; sources?: SpotlightSource[]; isolated?: boolean }`
  - Internal (`normalize.ts`): `interface NormalizedGroup { id: string; label: string; total?: number; items: SpotlightItem[] }`, `class SpotlightResultError extends Error`, `isSpotlightResponse(value: unknown): value is SpotlightResponse`, `isValidItem(value: unknown): value is SpotlightItem`, `normalizeResult(result: unknown, fallbackGroup: string): NormalizedGroup[]`
  - Package export `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/schema/spotlight-response.schema.json`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the types**

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/types.ts`:

````ts
import type { Injector, Signal } from '@angular/core';
import type { Observable } from 'rxjs';

/** Visual tone of a badge. The renderer maps it to its own colours. */
export type SpotlightBadgeTone = 'info' | 'success' | 'warn' | 'danger';

export interface SpotlightBadge {
  label: string;
  tone?: SpotlightBadgeTone;
}

/** What happens when an item is selected. */
export type SpotlightAction =
  | { route: unknown[] | string }
  | { url: string; newTab?: boolean }
  | { run: () => void | Promise<void> };

export interface SpotlightItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  /** The renderer decides the meaning (CSS class name, image URL, …). */
  icon?: string;
  group: string;
  keywords?: string[];
  badges?: SpotlightBadge[];
  /** Shown on an empty query. */
  suggested?: boolean;
  /** Free payload for custom templates. */
  data?: unknown;
  action: SpotlightAction;
  /** Reserved for nested pages (v1.x). Must be absent in v1: items that set it are dropped. */
  children?: never;
}

/** An item as written for staticSource(): `group` falls back to the source's group. */
export type SpotlightItemInput = Omit<SpotlightItem, 'group'> & { group?: string };

export interface SpotlightGroup {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  total?: number;
  items: SpotlightItem[];
}

export interface SpotlightResponse {
  groups: SpotlightGroup[];
}

export type SpotlightResult = SpotlightItem[] | SpotlightResponse;

export interface SpotlightContext {
  /** Aborted when the next keystroke supersedes this search. */
  signal: AbortSignal;
  /** Group id of the active chip, or null for "All". */
  chip: string | null;
  /** Environment injector of the provideSpotlight() call that registered the source. */
  injector: Injector;
}

export interface SpotlightSource {
  id: string;
  /** Default group for items that do not set one. */
  group?: string;
  /** e.g. '#', '@'. When the query starts with it, only this source runs. */
  prefix?: string;
  /** Default 0 (httpSource: 2). */
  minLength?: number;
  /** Default 0 (httpSource: 150). */
  debounceMs?: number;
  /** Default 5000. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
  /** Group order; lower comes first. Default: registration order. */
  priority?: number;
  /** true: the library ranks and filters the results. false (default): keep the source's order. */
  local?: boolean;
  search(
    query: string,
    ctx: SpotlightContext,
  ): Observable<SpotlightResult> | Promise<SpotlightResult> | SpotlightResult;
}

export type SpotlightTextValue = string | Signal<string> | Observable<string>;

export interface SpotlightTexts {
  placeholder: SpotlightTextValue;
  all: SpotlightTextValue;
  recent: SpotlightTextValue;
  noResults: SpotlightTextValue;
  loading: SpotlightTextValue;
  error: SpotlightTextValue;
  retry: SpotlightTextValue;
  /** Called inside a computed: reading signals here makes the announcement reactive. */
  resultsAnnouncement: (count: number) => string;
  hintNavigate: SpotlightTextValue;
  hintOpen: SpotlightTextValue;
  hintClose: SpotlightTextValue;
}

export interface SpotlightHttpRequest {
  method?: string;
  params?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | ReadonlyArray<string | number | boolean>>;
  headers?: Record<string, string | string[]>;
  body?: unknown;
}

export interface SpotlightHttpOptions {
  request?: (query: string, ctx: SpotlightContext) => SpotlightHttpRequest;
  map?: (response: unknown, query: string) => SpotlightResult;
}

/** The serialisable subset of SpotlightAction. */
export type SpotlightStoredAction = { route: unknown[] | string } | { url: string; newTab?: boolean };

export interface SpotlightRecentEntry {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  group: string;
  sourceId: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  icon?: string;
  badges?: SpotlightBadge[];
  /** Absent for run() items: those are resolved through their source by id. */
  action?: SpotlightStoredAction;
}

export interface SpotlightRecentStorage {
  load(): Promise<SpotlightRecentEntry[]>;
  save(items: SpotlightRecentEntry[]): Promise<void>;
}

export interface SpotlightConfig {
  /** Default ['ctrl.space', 'mod.k']. 'mod' = Cmd on macOS, Ctrl elsewhere. */
  hotkeys?: string[];
  texts?: Partial<SpotlightTexts>;
  recent?: { max?: number; storage?: SpotlightRecentStorage };
  /** Global defaults for every httpSource registered by this call (inherited by child calls). */
  http?: SpotlightHttpOptions;
  theme?: 'default' | 'none';
  onError?: (error: unknown, source: SpotlightSource) => void;
  sources?: SpotlightSource[];
  /** true: create a separate palette instead of adding sources to the parent injector's one. */
  isolated?: boolean;
}
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing normalisation test**

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/normalize.spec.ts`:

````ts
import { isSpotlightResponse, normalizeResult, SpotlightResultError } from './normalize';
import type { SpotlightItem } from './types';

const item = (id: string, group: string, extra: Partial<SpotlightItem> = {}): SpotlightItem => ({
  id,
  title: `Title ${id}`,
  group,
  action: { route: `/${id}` },
  ...extra,
});

describe('normalizeResult', () => {
  it('groups a flat array by item.group in order of first appearance, label = group id', () => {
    const groups = normalizeResult([item('1', 'Pages'), item('2', 'Commands'), item('3', 'Pages')], 'fallback');
    expect(groups.map((g) => [g.id, g.label, g.items.map((i) => i.id)])).toEqual([
      ['Pages', 'Pages', ['1', '3']],
      ['Commands', 'Commands', ['2']],
    ]);
  });

  it('puts items without a group into the fallback group', () => {
    const raw = [{ id: 'x', title: 'X', action: { route: '/x' } }];
    expect(normalizeResult(raw, 'Fallback')).toEqual([
      { id: 'Fallback', label: 'Fallback', items: [{ id: 'x', title: 'X', group: 'Fallback', action: { route: '/x' } }] },
    ]);
  });

  it('passes a grouped response through and forces item.group to the enclosing group id', () => {
    const groups = normalizeResult(
      { groups: [{ id: 'customers', label: 'Customers', total: 42, items: [item('c1', 'other')] }] },
      'fallback',
    );
    expect(groups).toEqual([{ id: 'customers', label: 'Customers', total: 42, items: [item('c1', 'customers')] }]);
  });

  it('uses the group id as label when the label is missing', () => {
    const groups = normalizeResult({ groups: [{ id: 'orders', items: [item('o1', 'orders')] }] }, 'f');
    expect(groups[0].label).toBe('orders');
  });

  it('drops invalid items and groups', () => {
    const groups = normalizeResult(
      {
        groups: [
          {
            id: 'g',
            label: 'G',
            items: [
              null,
              'text',
              { id: '', title: 'empty id', action: { route: '/' } },
              { id: 'no-title', action: { route: '/' } },
              { id: 'no-action', title: 'No action' },
              { id: 'bad-action', title: 'Bad action', action: { foo: 1 } },
              { id: 'kid', title: 'Has children', action: { route: '/' }, children: [] },
              { id: 'ok', title: 'Fine', action: { url: 'https://example.com' } },
            ],
          },
          { label: 'no id', items: [] },
          null,
          { id: 'no-items', label: 'No items', items: 'nope' },
        ],
      },
      'f',
    );
    expect(groups.map((g) => [g.id, g.items.map((i) => i.id)])).toEqual([
      ['g', ['ok']],
      ['no-items', []],
    ]);
  });

  it('throws SpotlightResultError for anything that is not a result', () => {
    for (const bad of [null, undefined, 'x', 42, { foo: 1 }, { groups: null }]) {
      expect(() => normalizeResult(bad, 'f')).toThrow(SpotlightResultError);
    }
  });
});

describe('isSpotlightResponse', () => {
  it('detects the grouped format', () => {
    expect(isSpotlightResponse({ groups: [] })).toBe(true);
    expect(isSpotlightResponse([])).toBe(false);
    expect(isSpotlightResponse({ groups: 'x' })).toBe(false);
  });
});
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the implementation**

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/normalize.ts`:

````ts
import type { SpotlightItem, SpotlightResponse } from './types';

/** A group after validation. Every item's `group` equals the group id. */
export interface NormalizedGroup {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  total?: number;
  items: SpotlightItem[];
}

export class SpotlightResultError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'SpotlightResultError';
  }
}

export function isSpotlightResponse(value: unknown): value is SpotlightResponse {
  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && Array.isArray((value as { groups?: unknown }).groups);
}

function isValidAction(value: unknown): boolean {
  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return false;
  const action = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  return (
    typeof action['route'] === 'string' ||
    Array.isArray(action['route']) ||
    typeof action['url'] === 'string' ||
    typeof action['run'] === 'function'
  );
}

/** id and title are non-empty strings, the action is usable and `children` (reserved) is absent. */
export function isValidItem(value: unknown): value is SpotlightItem {
  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return false;
  const item = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  return (
    typeof item['id'] === 'string' &&
    item['id'] !== '' &&
    typeof item['title'] === 'string' &&
    isValidAction(item['action']) &&
    item['children'] === undefined
  );
}

/**
 * Turns whatever a source returned into validated groups. Invalid items and groups are
 * dropped; a value that is not a result at all throws SpotlightResultError, which the
 * engine turns into an error row for that source only.
 */
export function normalizeResult(result: unknown, fallbackGroup: string): NormalizedGroup[] {
  if (Array.isArray(result)) {
    const byGroup = new Map<string, SpotlightItem[]>();
    for (const raw of result as unknown[]) {
      if (!isValidItem(raw)) continue;
      const group = typeof raw.group === 'string' && raw.group !== '' ? raw.group : fallbackGroup;
      const list = byGroup.get(group) ?? [];
      list.push({ ...raw, group });
      byGroup.set(group, list);
    }
    return [...byGroup].map(([id, items]) => ({ id, label: id, items }));
  }
  if (isSpotlightResponse(result)) {
    const groups: NormalizedGroup[] = [];
    for (const rawGroup of result.groups as unknown[]) {
      if (typeof rawGroup !== 'object' || rawGroup === null) continue;
      const g = rawGroup as Record<string, unknown>;
      const id = g['id'];
      if (typeof id !== 'string' || id === '') continue;
      const rawItems: unknown[] = Array.isArray(g['items']) ? (g['items'] as unknown[]) : [];
      const group: NormalizedGroup = {
        id,
        label: typeof g['label'] === 'string' ? g['label'] : id,
        items: rawItems.filter(isValidItem).map((item) => ({ ...item, group: id })),
      };
      const total = g['total'];
      if (typeof total === 'number') group.total = total;
      groups.push(group);
    }
    return groups;
  }
  throw new SpotlightResultError(
    '[ng-spotlight] a source returned something that is neither SpotlightItem[] nor { groups: SpotlightGroup[] }',
  );
}
````

````ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';

import type { SpotlightItem, SpotlightResponse } from '../src/lib/types';

const schema: object = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(resolve(process.cwd(), 'projects/ng-spotlight/schema/spotlight-response.schema.json'), 'utf8'),
) as object;
const validate = new Ajv2020({ allErrors: true }).compile(schema);

// Typed fixtures: if the TS types and the schema drift apart, one of these stops compiling or validating.
const grouped: SpotlightResponse = {
  groups: [
    {
      id: 'customers',
      label: 'Customers',

      items: [
        {
          id: 'c-1',
          title: 'Acme A/S',

          icon: 'pi pi-building',
          group: 'customers',

          badges: [{ label: 'VIP', tone: 'success' }],
          suggested: false,

        },

    },
    { id: 'orders', label: 'Orders', items: [{ id: 'o-1', title: 'Order 1', group: 'orders', action: { url: 'https://x.test/o/1', newTab: true } }] },
  ],
};
const flat: SpotlightItem[] = [{ id: 'p', title: 'Page', group: 'Pages', action: { route: '/page' } }];

describe('spotlight-response.schema.json', () => {
  it('accepts the grouped SpotlightResponse format', () => {
    expect(validate(grouped)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('accepts a flat SpotlightItem array', () => {

  });

  it('rejects an item that sets the reserved children field', () => {

  });

  it('rejects an unknown badge tone and a run() action (functions are not JSON)', () => {

    expect(validate([{ id: 'x', title: 'X', action: { run: 'alert(1)' } }])).toBe(false);
````

````json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "https://tocdk.github.io/ng-spotlight/schema/spotlight-response.schema.json",
  "title": "SpotlightResult",
  "description": "Response format for @tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight httpSource: a grouped SpotlightResponse or a flat SpotlightItem array (grouped by item.group).",
  "oneOf": [
    { "$ref": "#/$defs/SpotlightResponse" },
    { "type": "array", "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/SpotlightItem" } }
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "SpotlightResponse": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["groups"],
      "properties": {
        "groups": { "type": "array", "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/SpotlightGroup" } }
      }
    },
    "SpotlightGroup": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["id", "label", "items"],
      "properties": {
        "id": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1 },
        "label": { "type": "string" },
        "total": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0 },
        "items": { "type": "array", "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/SpotlightItem" } }
      }
    },
    "SpotlightItem": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["id", "title", "action"],
      "properties": {
        "id": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1 },
        "title": { "type": "string" },
        "subtitle": { "type": "string" },
        "icon": { "type": "string" },
        "group": { "type": "string", "description": "Optional inside a group (the group id wins); in a flat array it defaults to the source's group." },
        "keywords": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
        "badges": { "type": "array", "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/SpotlightBadge" } },
        "suggested": { "type": "boolean" },
        "data": {},
        "action": { "$ref": "#/$defs/SpotlightAction" },
        "children": false
      }
    },
    "SpotlightBadge": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["label"],
      "properties": {
        "label": { "type": "string" },
        "tone": { "enum": ["info", "success", "warn", "danger"] }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "SpotlightAction": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "required": ["route"],
          "properties": { "route": { "oneOf": [{ "type": "string" }, { "type": "array" }] } },
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "required": ["url"],
          "properties": { "url": { "type": "string" }, "newTab": { "type": "boolean" } },
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
````

````ts
import { rankItems, RECENT_BOOST, scoreItem, scoreText } from './rank';
import type { SpotlightItem } from './types';

const item = (id: string, title: string, keywords?: string[]): SpotlightItem => ({
  id,
  title,
  group: 'g',
  keywords,
  action: { route: `/${id}` },
});

describe('scoreText', () => {
  it('orders exact > title start > word start > subsequence, case-insensitively', () => {
    const exact = scoreText('Users', 'users');
    const start = scoreText('Users list', 'users');
    const word = scoreText('Super users', 'users');
    const subsequence = scoreText('Unused rows', 'users');
    expect(exact).toBeGreaterThan(start);
    expect(start).toBeGreaterThan(word);
    expect(word).toBeGreaterThan(subsequence);
    expect(subsequence).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('prefers tighter subsequences', () => {
    expect(scoreText('customers', 'cstm')).toBeGreaterThan(scoreText('c-s-t-m', 'cstm'));
  });

  it('returns -1 when the query is not a subsequence', () => {
    expect(scoreText('Orders', 'xyz')).toBe(-1);
  });

  it('treats word boundaries like space, dash, slash and dot', () => {
    expect(scoreText('admin/users', 'users')).toBe(scoreText('admin users', 'users'));
    expect(scoreText('admin-users', 'users')).toBe(scoreText('admin.users', 'users'));
  });
});

describe('rankItems', () => {
  it('ranks keyword matches below title matches of the same kind', () => {
    const ranked = rankItems([item('team', 'Team', ['users']), item('super', 'Super users'), item('users', 'Users')], 'users');
    expect(ranked.map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['users', 'super', 'team']);
  });

  it('drops items that do not match', () => {
    expect(rankItems([item('a', 'Alpha'), item('b', 'Beta')], 'alp').map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['a']);
  });

  it('keeps the original order for equal scores and for the empty query', () => {
    const items = [item('1', 'Order list'), item('2', 'Order lines')];
    expect(rankItems(items, 'order').map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['1', '2']);
    expect(rankItems(items, '').map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['1', '2']);
  });

  it('gives Recent items a small boost that breaks ties but not tiers', () => {
    const items = [item('1', 'Order list'), item('2', 'Order lines'), item('3', 'Orders')];
    // 'order': all three start with it (800 each), so source order holds until the boost.
    expect(rankItems(items, 'order').map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['1', '2', '3']);
    expect(rankItems(items, 'order', new Set(['2'])).map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['2', '1', '3']);
    // 'orders': exact (1000) stays first; the boost lifts '2' (subsequence 295) over '1' (297).
    expect(rankItems(items, 'orders').map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['3', '1', '2']);
    expect(rankItems(items, 'orders', new Set(['2'])).map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['3', '2', '1']);
    expect(scoreItem(items[1], 'order', new Set(['2']))).toBe((scoreItem(items[1], 'order', new Set()) ?? 0) + RECENT_BOOST);
  });
});
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/hotkeys.spec.ts`:

````ts
import { DEFAULT_HOTKEYS, matchesHotkey, parseHotkey } from './hotkeys';

const down = (init: KeyboardEventInit) => new KeyboardEvent('keydown', init);

describe('parseHotkey', () => {
  it('parses ctrl.space', () => {
    expect(parseHotkey('ctrl.space', false)).toEqual({ ctrl: true, alt: false, shift: false, meta: false, key: ' ' });
  });

  it('maps mod to meta on macOS and ctrl elsewhere', () => {
    expect(parseHotkey('mod.k', true)).toMatchObject({ meta: true, ctrl: false, key: 'k' });
    expect(parseHotkey('mod.k', false)).toMatchObject({ meta: false, ctrl: true, key: 'k' });
  });

  it('is case-insensitive and supports key aliases', () => {
    expect(parseHotkey('Alt.Shift.Slash', false)).toEqual({ ctrl: false, alt: true, shift: true, meta: false, key: '/' });
  });

  it('rejects unknown modifiers and empty specs', () => {
    expect(() => parseHotkey('hyper.k', false)).toThrow('[ng-spotlight] unknown modifier "hyper" in hotkey "hyper.k"');
    expect(() => parseHotkey('', false)).toThrow('[ng-spotlight] invalid hotkey ""');
  });

  it('defaults to ctrl.space and mod.k', () => {
    expect(DEFAULT_HOTKEYS).toEqual(['ctrl.space', 'mod.k']);
  });
});

describe('matchesHotkey', () => {
  const ctrlSpace = parseHotkey('ctrl.space', false);

  it('matches the exact modifier set', () => {
    expect(matchesHotkey(down({ key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true }), ctrlSpace)).toBe(true);
    expect(matchesHotkey(down({ key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: true }), ctrlSpace)).toBe(false);
    expect(matchesHotkey(down({ key: ' ', code: 'Space' }), ctrlSpace)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('ignores IME composition', () => {
    expect(matchesHotkey(down({ key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true, isComposing: true }), ctrlSpace)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('falls back to event.code when Alt changes event.key (macOS)', () => {
    const altP = parseHotkey('alt.p', true);
    expect(matchesHotkey(down({ key: 'π', code: 'KeyP', altKey: true }), altP)).toBe(true);
    const alt1 = parseHotkey('alt.1', true);
    expect(matchesHotkey(down({ key: '¡', code: 'Digit1', altKey: true }), alt1)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('matches letters case-insensitively', () => {
    expect(matchesHotkey(down({ key: 'K', code: 'KeyK', ctrlKey: true }), parseHotkey('mod.k', false))).toBe(true);
  });
});
````

````ts
import { entryToItem, isRecentEntry, localStorageRecent, pushRecent, toRecentEntry } from './recent';
import type { SpotlightItem, SpotlightRecentEntry } from './types';

const entry = (id: string, sourceId = 's'): SpotlightRecentEntry => ({ id, title: id, group: 'g', sourceId, action: { route: `/${id}` } });

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  localStorage.clear();
});

describe('localStorageRecent', () => {
  it('round-trips entries under a namespaced key', async () => {
    const storage = localStorageRecent('app');
    await storage.save([entry('a'), entry('b')]);
    expect(localStorage.getItem('ng-spotlight:recent:app')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(await storage.load()).toEqual([entry('a'), entry('b')]);
  });

  it('returns [] when nothing is stored', async () => {
    expect(await localStorageRecent('empty').load()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('localStorageRecent survives blocked storage and foreign data', async () => {
    const storage = localStorageRecent('app');
    localStorage.setItem('ng-spotlight:recent:app', '{not json');
    expect(await storage.load()).toEqual([]);
    localStorage.setItem('ng-spotlight:recent:app', JSON.stringify({ items: [] }));
    expect(await storage.load()).toEqual([]);
    localStorage.setItem('ng-spotlight:recent:app', JSON.stringify([entry('ok'), { id: 1 }, null, 'x', { id: 'no-source', title: 't', group: 'g' }]));
    expect(await storage.load()).toEqual([entry('ok')]);

    vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'getItem').mockImplementation(() => {
      throw new DOMException('blocked', 'SecurityError');
    });
    vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'setItem').mockImplementation(() => {
      throw new DOMException('full', 'QuotaExceededError');
    });
    expect(await storage.load()).toEqual([]);
    await expect(storage.save([entry('a')])).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('recent helpers', () => {
  const run = vi.fn();
  const routeItem: SpotlightItem = {
    id: 'cust',
    title: 'Customers',
    subtitle: '/customers',
    icon: 'pi pi-users',
    group: 'Pages',
    keywords: ['clients'],
    badges: [{ label: 'new' }],
    suggested: true,
    data: { big: 'payload' },
    action: { route: '/customers' },
  };

  it('stores only what is needed to re-render and re-run route and url items', () => {
    expect(toRecentEntry(routeItem, 'routes')).toEqual({
      id: 'cust',
      title: 'Customers',
      group: 'Pages',
      sourceId: 'routes',
      subtitle: '/customers',
      icon: 'pi pi-users',
      badges: [{ label: 'new' }],
      action: { route: '/customers' },
    });
    expect(toRecentEntry({ id: 'u', title: 'U', group: 'g', action: { url: 'https://x.dk', newTab: true } }, 's').action).toEqual({
      url: 'https://x.dk',
      newTab: true,
    });
  });

  it('stores run() items without an action', () => {
    const stored = toRecentEntry({ id: 'logout', title: 'Log out', group: 'Commands', action: { run } }, 'cmd');
    expect(stored).toEqual({ id: 'logout', title: 'Log out', group: 'Commands', sourceId: 'cmd' });
    expect(entryToItem(stored)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('turns a stored route entry back into an item', () => {
    expect(entryToItem(entry('a'))).toEqual({ id: 'a', title: 'a', group: 'g', action: { route: '/a' } });
  });

  it('pushes most-recent-first, de-duplicates by source and id, and caps the list', () => {
    let list: SpotlightRecentEntry[] = [];
    list = pushRecent(list, entry('a'), 3);
    list = pushRecent(list, entry('b'), 3);
    list = pushRecent(list, entry('a'), 3);
    list = pushRecent(list, entry('a', 'other'), 3);
    list = pushRecent(list, entry('c'), 3);
    expect(list.map((e) => `${e.sourceId}:${e.id}`)).toEqual(['s:c', 'other:a', 's:a']);
  });

  it('validates entries', () => {
    expect(isRecentEntry(entry('a'))).toBe(true);
    expect(isRecentEntry({ ...entry('a'), action: { run: 'x' } })).toBe(false);
    expect(isRecentEntry({ id: 'a', title: 'a', group: 'g' })).toBe(false);
  });
});
````

````ts
import { EnvironmentInjector, provideZonelessChangeDetection, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { BehaviorSubject } from 'rxjs';
import { DEFAULT_SPOTLIGHT_TEXTS, resolveTexts, SPOTLIGHT_TEXT_KEYS } from './texts';

describe('resolveTexts', () => {
  let injector: EnvironmentInjector;
  beforeEach(() => {
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({ providers: [provideZonelessChangeDetection()] });
    injector = TestBed.inject(EnvironmentInjector);
  });

  it('falls back to the English defaults for every key', () => {
    const texts = resolveTexts(undefined, injector);
    for (const key of SPOTLIGHT_TEXT_KEYS) expect(texts[key]()).toBe(DEFAULT_SPOTLIGHT_TEXTS[key]);
    expect(texts.placeholder()).toBe('Search…');
    expect(texts.resultsAnnouncement(0)).toBe('No results');
    expect(texts.resultsAnnouncement(1)).toBe('1 result');
    expect(texts.resultsAnnouncement(7)).toBe('7 results');
  });

  it('accepts plain strings', () => {
    expect(resolveTexts({ placeholder: 'Find…' }, injector).placeholder()).toBe('Find…');
  });

  it('passes signals through so changes show live', () => {
    const placeholder = signal('Search…');
    const texts = resolveTexts({ placeholder }, injector);
    placeholder.set('Søg…');
    expect(texts.placeholder()).toBe('Søg…');
  });

  it('subscribes to observables', () => {
    const recent = new BehaviorSubject('Recent');
    const texts = resolveTexts({ recent }, injector);
    expect(texts.recent()).toBe('Recent');
    recent.next('Seneste');
    expect(texts.recent()).toBe('Seneste');
  });

  it('uses a custom results announcement', () => {
    expect(resolveTexts({ resultsAnnouncement: (n) => `${n} hits` }, injector).resultsAnnouncement(3)).toBe('3 hits');
  });
});
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/rank.ts`:

````ts
import type { SpotlightItem } from './types';

const WORD_BOUNDARY = /[\s\-_/.:,()[\]]/;

/** Score bonus for items that are in Recent. Smaller than the gap between match tiers. */
export const RECENT_BOOST = 50;

/**
 * 1000 exact, 800 start of text, 600 start of a word, 200–300 subsequence (tighter is higher),
 * -1 no match, 0 for the empty query.
 */
export function scoreText(text: string, query: string): number {
  if (query === '') return 0;
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const q = query.toLowerCase();
  if (t === q) return 1000;
  if (t.startsWith(q)) return 800;
  for (let i = t.indexOf(q, 1); i > 0; i = t.indexOf(q, i + 1)) {
    if (WORD_BOUNDARY.test(t[i - 1])) return 600;
  }
  let pos = -1;
  let first = -1;
  for (const ch of q) {
    pos = t.indexOf(ch, pos + 1);
    if (pos === -1) return -1;
    if (first === -1) first = pos;
  }
  const gaps = pos - first + 1 - q.length;
  return 200 + Math.max(0, 100 - gaps);
}

/** Title score, or half the best keyword score, plus the Recent boost. null = no match. */
export function scoreItem(item: SpotlightItem, query: string, recentIds: ReadonlySet<string>): number | null {
  let best = scoreText(item.title, query);
  for (const keyword of item.keywords ?? []) {
    const score = scoreText(keyword, query);
    if (score >= 0) best = Math.max(best, score / 2);
  }
  if (best < 0) return null;
  return best + (recentIds.has(item.id) ? RECENT_BOOST : 0);
}

/** Filters and sorts local results. Stable: equal scores keep the source's order. */
export function rankItems(
  items: readonly SpotlightItem[],
  query: string,
  recentIds: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(),
): SpotlightItem[] {
  if (query === '') return [...items];
  return items
    .map((item, index) => ({ item, index, score: scoreItem(item, query, recentIds) }))
    .filter((entry): entry is { item: SpotlightItem; index: number; score: number } => entry.score !== null)
    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || a.index - b.index)
    .map((entry) => entry.item);
}
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/hotkeys.ts`:

````ts
export interface ParsedHotkey {
  ctrl: boolean;
  alt: boolean;
  shift: boolean;
  meta: boolean;
  /** Lower-case KeyboardEvent.key value (' ' for space). */
  key: string;
}

export const DEFAULT_HOTKEYS: readonly string[] = ['ctrl.space', 'mod.k'];

const MODIFIERS = new Set(['ctrl', 'alt', 'shift', 'meta', 'mod']);

const KEY_ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  space: ' ',
  slash: '/',
  period: '.',
  comma: ',',
  dash: '-',
  esc: 'escape',
  escape: 'escape',
  enter: 'enter',
};

/** Parses 'ctrl.space', 'mod.k', 'alt.shift.slash', … 'mod' = Cmd on macOS, Ctrl elsewhere. */
export function parseHotkey(spec: string, isMac: boolean): ParsedHotkey {
  const parts = spec.toLowerCase().split('.').filter((p) => p !== '');
  const key = parts.pop();
  if (key === undefined) throw new Error(`[ng-spotlight] invalid hotkey "${spec}"`);
  for (const modifier of parts) {
    if (!MODIFIERS.has(modifier)) throw new Error(`[ng-spotlight] unknown modifier "${modifier}" in hotkey "${spec}"`);
  }
  const mods = new Set(parts);
  return {
    ctrl: mods.has('ctrl') || (mods.has('mod') && !isMac),
    alt: mods.has('alt'),
    shift: mods.has('shift'),
    meta: mods.has('meta') || (mods.has('mod') && isMac),
    key: KEY_ALIASES[key] ?? key,
  };
}

export function matchesHotkey(event: KeyboardEvent, hotkey: ParsedHotkey): boolean {
  if (event.isComposing) return false;
  if (
    event.ctrlKey !== hotkey.ctrl ||
    event.altKey !== hotkey.alt ||
    event.shiftKey !== hotkey.shift ||
    event.metaKey !== hotkey.meta
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
  if (hotkey.key === ' ') return key === ' ' || event.code === 'Space';
  if (key === hotkey.key) return true;
  // On macOS, Alt changes event.key (Alt+P gives 'π'); compare the physical key instead.
  if (/^[a-z]$/.test(hotkey.key)) return event.code === `Key${hotkey.key.toUpperCase()}`;
  if (/^[0-9]$/.test(hotkey.key)) return event.code === `Digit${hotkey.key}`;
  return false;
}

export function detectMac(): boolean {
  return typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && /Mac|iPhone|iPad|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent);
}
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/url.ts`:

````ts
const ALLOWED_SCHEMES = new Set(['http', 'https', 'mailto', 'tel']);

/**
 * true for http(s), mailto, tel and relative URLs. Browsers drop ASCII tab/newline and
 * leading control characters before reading the scheme ("java\nscript:" runs script), so
 * those are removed before the check.
 */
export function isSafeUrl(url: string): boolean {
  const compact = url.replace(/[\u0000- \u007f]/g, '');
  const match = /^([a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*):/i.exec(compact);
  return match === null || ALLOWED_SCHEMES.has(match[1].toLowerCase());
}
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/recent.ts`:

````ts
import type { SpotlightItem, SpotlightRecentEntry, SpotlightRecentStorage, SpotlightStoredAction } from './types';

function isStoredAction(value: unknown): value is SpotlightStoredAction {
  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return false;
  const action = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  return typeof action['route'] === 'string' || Array.isArray(action['route']) || typeof action['url'] === 'string';
}

export function isRecentEntry(value: unknown): value is SpotlightRecentEntry {
  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return false;
  const entry = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  return (
    typeof entry['id'] === 'string' &&
    typeof entry['title'] === 'string' &&
    typeof entry['group'] === 'string' &&
    typeof entry['sourceId'] === 'string' &&
    (entry['action'] === undefined || isStoredAction(entry['action']))
  );
}

/**
 * Recent items in localStorage under `ng-spotlight:recent:<key>`. Every access is wrapped
 * in try/catch, so the palette still works when storage is blocked, full or corrupted.
 */
export function localStorageRecent(key: string): SpotlightRecentStorage {
  const storageKey = `ng-spotlight:recent:${key}`;
  return {
    load(): Promise<SpotlightRecentEntry[]> {
      try {
        const raw = globalThis.localStorage?.getItem(storageKey);
        if (!raw) return Promise.resolve([]);
        const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
        return Promise.resolve(Array.isArray(parsed) ? (parsed as unknown[]).filter(isRecentEntry) : []);
      } catch {
        return Promise.resolve([]);
      }
    },
    save(items: SpotlightRecentEntry[]): Promise<void> {
      try {
        globalThis.localStorage?.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(items));
      } catch {
        // Blocked or full storage: Recent simply isn't persisted.
      }
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
  };
}

/** Keeps only the fields needed to re-render the item and re-run a route or url action. */
export function toRecentEntry(item: SpotlightItem, sourceId: string): SpotlightRecentEntry {
  const entry: SpotlightRecentEntry = { id: item.id, title: item.title, group: item.group, sourceId };
  if (item.subtitle !== undefined) entry.subtitle = item.subtitle;
  if (item.icon !== undefined) entry.icon = item.icon;
  if (item.badges !== undefined) entry.badges = item.badges;
  const action = item.action;
  if ('route' in action) entry.action = { route: action.route };
  else if ('url' in action) entry.action = action.newTab ? { url: action.url, newTab: true } : { url: action.url };
  return entry;
}

/** A stored route/url entry as an item; null for run() entries (resolve those through the source). */
export function entryToItem(entry: SpotlightRecentEntry): SpotlightItem | null {
  if (entry.action === undefined) return null;
  const item: SpotlightItem = { id: entry.id, title: entry.title, group: entry.group, action: entry.action };
  if (entry.subtitle !== undefined) item.subtitle = entry.subtitle;
  if (entry.icon !== undefined) item.icon = entry.icon;
  if (entry.badges !== undefined) item.badges = entry.badges;
  return item;
}

/** Most recent first, unique per source + id, at most `max` entries. */
export function pushRecent(
  list: readonly SpotlightRecentEntry[],
  entry: SpotlightRecentEntry,
  max: number,
): SpotlightRecentEntry[] {
  const rest = list.filter((e) => !(e.id === entry.id && e.sourceId === entry.sourceId));
  return [entry, ...rest].slice(0, Math.max(0, max));
}
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/texts.ts`:

````ts
import { Injector, isSignal, signal, type Signal } from '@angular/core';
import { toSignal } from '@angular/core/rxjs-interop';
import { isObservable, type Observable } from 'rxjs';
import type { SpotlightTexts, SpotlightTextValue } from './types';

export type SpotlightTextKey = Exclude<keyof SpotlightTexts, 'resultsAnnouncement'>;

export const SPOTLIGHT_TEXT_KEYS: readonly SpotlightTextKey[] = [
  'placeholder',
  'all',
  'recent',
  'noResults',
  'loading',
  'error',
  'retry',
  'hintNavigate',
  'hintOpen',
  'hintClose',
];

export const DEFAULT_SPOTLIGHT_TEXTS: { readonly [K in SpotlightTextKey]: string } & {
  readonly resultsAnnouncement: (count: number) => string;
} = {
  placeholder: 'Search…',
  all: 'All',
  recent: 'Recent',
  noResults: 'No results',
  loading: 'Loading…',
  error: 'Something went wrong',
  retry: 'Retry',
  hintNavigate: 'to navigate',
  hintOpen: 'to open',
  hintClose: 'to close',
  resultsAnnouncement: (count) => (count === 0 ? 'No results' : count === 1 ? '1 result' : `${count} results`),
};

export type ResolvedSpotlightTexts = { readonly [K in SpotlightTextKey]: Signal<string> } & {
  readonly resultsAnnouncement: (count: number) => string;
};

function toTextSignal(value: SpotlightTextValue | undefined, fallback: string, injector: Injector): Signal<string> {
  if (value === undefined) return signal(fallback).asReadonly();
  if (isSignal(value)) return value as Signal<string>;
  if (isObservable(value)) return toSignal(value as Observable<string>, { initialValue: fallback, injector });
  return signal(value).asReadonly();
}

/** Every text becomes a Signal<string>; Observables are subscribed for the injector's lifetime. */
export function resolveTexts(texts: Partial<SpotlightTexts> | undefined, injector: Injector): ResolvedSpotlightTexts {
  const resolved = {} as Record<SpotlightTextKey, Signal<string>>;
  for (const key of SPOTLIGHT_TEXT_KEYS) {
    resolved[key] = toTextSignal(texts?.[key], DEFAULT_SPOTLIGHT_TEXTS[key], injector);
  }
  return {
    ...resolved,
    resultsAnnouncement: texts?.resultsAnnouncement ?? DEFAULT_SPOTLIGHT_TEXTS.resultsAnnouncement,
  };
}
````

````ts
import { Injector } from '@angular/core';
import { staticSource } from './static-source';

const ctx: SpotlightContext = { signal: new AbortController().signal, chip: null, injector: Injector.NULL };

describe('staticSource', () => {

    const source = staticSource({
      group: 'Commands',
      items: [
        { id: 'logout', title: 'Log out', action: { run: () => undefined } },
        { id: 'docs', title: 'Docs', group: 'Links', action: { url: 'https://example.com' } },
      ],
    });
````

````ts
import { Component, Injector, provideZonelessChangeDetection } from '@angular/core';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { provideRouter, Router, type Routes } from '@angular/router';
import { routeSource } from './route-source';
import type { SpotlightItem } from '../types';

@Component({ template: '' })
class Dummy {}

const lazyChildren: Routes = [{ path: 'page', component: Dummy, title: 'Lazy page' }];

const routes: Routes = [
  { path: '', pathMatch: 'full', redirectTo: 'dashboard' },
  { path: 'dashboard', component: Dummy, title: 'Dashboard', data: { spotlight: { keywords: ['home'], suggested: true } } },
  { path: 'labelled', component: Dummy, data: { spotlight: { label: 'From data' }, breadcrumb: 'Crumb' } },
  { path: 'crumb', component: Dummy, data: { breadcrumb: 'From breadcrumb' } },
  { path: 'resolved-title', component: Dummy, title: () => 'dynamic', data: { spotlight: { label: 'Resolved fallback' } } },
  { path: 'customers/:id', component: Dummy, title: 'Customer' },
  { path: 'orders/:id', children: [{ path: 'lines', component: Dummy, title: 'Order lines' }] },
  { path: 'hidden', component: Dummy, title: 'Hidden', data: { spotlight: false } },
  { path: 'admin', title: 'Admin', data: { spotlight: false }, children: [{ path: 'users', component: Dummy, title: 'Users' }] },
  {
    path: 'reports',
    title: 'Reports',
    children: [
      { path: '', component: Dummy, title: 'Report overview' },
      { path: 'unlabelled', component: Dummy },
    ],
  },
  { path: 'lazy', loadChildren: () => Promise.resolve(lazyChildren) },
  { matcher: () => null, component: Dummy, title: 'Matcher' },
  { path: '**', component: Dummy, title: 'Not found' },
];

function search(source = routeSource()): SpotlightItem[] {
  return source.search('', { signal: new AbortController().signal, chip: null, injector: TestBed.inject(Injector) }) as SpotlightItem[];
}

describe('routeSource', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({ providers: [provideZonelessChangeDetection(), provideRouter(routes)] });
  });

  it('lists labelled static routes using title, then data.spotlight.label, then data.breadcrumb', () => {
    expect(search().map((i) => [i.subtitle, i.title])).toEqual([
      ['/dashboard', 'Dashboard'],
      ['/labelled', 'From data'],
      ['/crumb', 'From breadcrumb'],
      ['/resolved-title', 'Resolved fallback'],
      ['/admin/users', 'Users'],
      ['/reports', 'Report overview'],
    ]);
  });

  it('builds route items with keywords, suggestion flag and a route action', () => {
    expect(search()[0]).toEqual({
      id: 'route:/dashboard',
      title: 'Dashboard',
      subtitle: '/dashboard',
      group: 'Pages',
      keywords: ['home'],
      suggested: true,
      action: { route: '/dashboard' },
    });
  });

  it('includes lazy children once they are loaded', async () => {
    expect(search().some((i) => i.title === 'Lazy page')).toBe(false);
    await TestBed.inject(Router).navigateByUrl('/lazy/page');
    expect(search().at(-1)).toMatchObject({ title: 'Lazy page', subtitle: '/lazy/page' });
  });

  it('applies the app filter', () => {
    const source = routeSource({ filter: (_route, url) => !url.startsWith('/admin') });
    expect(search(source).some((i) => i.subtitle === '/admin/users')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('is a local source with configurable id and group', () => {
    expect(routeSource()).toMatchObject({ id: 'routes', group: 'Pages', local: true });
    const custom = routeSource({ id: 'nav', group: 'Navigation', prefix: '/', priority: 1 });
    expect(custom).toMatchObject({ id: 'nav', group: 'Navigation', prefix: '/', priority: 1 });
    expect(search(custom)[0].group).toBe('Navigation');
  });
});
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/sources/static-source.ts`:

````ts
import type { SpotlightItem, SpotlightItemInput, SpotlightSource } from '../types';

export interface StaticSourceOptions {
  /** Default `static:<group>`. */
  id?: string;
  group: string;
  items: SpotlightItemInput[];
  prefix?: string;
  priority?: number;
}

/** Fixed commands. Results are local: the library ranks and filters them. */
export function staticSource(options: StaticSourceOptions): SpotlightSource {
  const items: SpotlightItem[] = options.items.map((item) => ({ ...item, group: item.group ?? options.group }));
  return {
    id: options.id ?? `static:${options.group}`,
    group: options.group,
    prefix: options.prefix,
    priority: options.priority,
    local: true,
    search: () => items,
  };
}
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/sources/route-walk.ts`:

````ts
import type { Route, Routes } from '@angular/router';

/** The shape of `route.data.spotlight` (or `false` to opt the route out). */
export interface SpotlightRouteData {
  label?: string;
  keywords?: string[];
  suggested?: boolean;
  /** Not used by the library; apps use it in their own nav-coverage tests. */
  hiddenFromNav?: boolean;
}

export interface WalkedRoute {
  route: Route;
  url: string;
  label: string | null;
  keywords: string[];
  suggested: boolean;
  optedOut: boolean;
  /** This route or an ancestor has a `:param` segment. */
  hasParam: boolean;
  /** Renders a component and has no children of its own. */
  isPage: boolean;
}

/** Angular keeps lazily loaded children here (internal API; pinned by route-source.spec.ts). */
type RouteWithLoadedChildren = Route & { _loadedRoutes?: Routes };

/** Depth-first walk of a Router config. Skips redirects, `**` and custom matchers. */
export function walkRoutes(
  routes: Routes,
  includeLazy: boolean,
  parentSegments: readonly string[] = [],
  parentHasParam = false,
): WalkedRoute[] {
  const out: WalkedRoute[] = [];
  for (const route of routes) {
    if (route.path === '**' || route.redirectTo !== undefined || route.matcher !== undefined) continue;
    const segments = [...parentSegments, ...(route.path ?? '').split('/').filter((s) => s !== '')];
    const hasParam = parentHasParam || segments.some((s) => s.startsWith(':'));
    const data: Record<string, unknown> = route.data ?? {};
    const spotlight = data['spotlight'];
    const routeData: SpotlightRouteData =
      typeof spotlight === 'object' && spotlight !== null ? (spotlight as SpotlightRouteData) : {};
    const breadcrumb = data['breadcrumb'];
    const rawLabel =
      typeof route.title === 'string'
        ? route.title
        : typeof routeData.label === 'string'
          ? routeData.label
          : typeof breadcrumb === 'string'
            ? breadcrumb
            : null;
    out.push({
      route,
      url: '/' + segments.join('/'),
      label: rawLabel !== null && rawLabel.trim() !== '' ? rawLabel : null,
      keywords: Array.isArray(routeData.keywords)
        ? routeData.keywords.filter((k): k is string => typeof k === 'string')
        : [],
      suggested: routeData.suggested === true,
      optedOut: spotlight === false,
      hasParam,
      isPage:
        (route.component !== undefined || route.loadComponent !== undefined) &&
        route.children === undefined &&
        route.loadChildren === undefined,
    });
    const children = route.children ?? (includeLazy ? (route as RouteWithLoadedChildren)._loadedRoutes : undefined);
    if (children) out.push(...walkRoutes(children, includeLazy, segments, hasParam));
  }
  return out;
}
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/sources/route-source.ts`:

````ts
import { Router, type Route } from '@angular/router';
import type { SpotlightItem, SpotlightSource } from '../types';
import { walkRoutes } from './route-walk';

export interface RouteSourceOptions {
  /** Default 'routes'. */
  id?: string;
  /** Default 'Pages'. */
  group?: string;
  prefix?: string;
  priority?: number;
  /** Return false to hide a route, e.g. to apply the app's role gates. */
  filter?: (route: Route, url: string) => boolean;
}

/**
 * Every labelled static route, including lazy children once loaded. A deeper route with the
 * same URL (an empty-path child) wins over its parent. Results are local.
 */
export function routeSource(options: RouteSourceOptions = {}): SpotlightSource {
  const group = options.group ?? 'Pages';
  return {
    id: options.id ?? 'routes',
    group,
    prefix: options.prefix,
    priority: options.priority,
    local: true,
    search: (_query, ctx) => {
      const router = ctx.injector.get(Router);
      const byUrl = new Map<string, SpotlightItem>();
      for (const entry of walkRoutes(router.config, true)) {
        if (entry.optedOut || entry.hasParam || entry.label === null) continue;
        if (options.filter && !options.filter(entry.route, entry.url)) continue;
        byUrl.set(entry.url, {
          id: `route:${entry.url}`,
          title: entry.label,
          subtitle: entry.url,
          group,
          keywords: entry.keywords,
          suggested: entry.suggested,
          action: { route: entry.url },
        });
      }
      return [...byUrl.values()];
    },
  };
}
````

````ts
import type { Route, Routes } from '@angular/router';
import { walkRoutes } from './route-walk';

export interface SpotlightRouteAuditEntry {
  path: string;
  reason: 'missing-label';
}

export interface SpotlightRouteAuditOptions {
  /** Return true to skip a route. */
  ignore?: (route: Route, url: string) => boolean;
}

/**
 * Static pages that have no label (title, data.spotlight.label or data.breadcrumb) and are not
 * opted out with data.spotlight: false. Synchronous: `loadChildren` is not followed, so pass
 * lazy route arrays in directly. A page whose URL a labelled parent already covers passes.
 *
 *     expect(spotlightRouteAudit(adminRoutes)).toEqual([]);
 */
export function spotlightRouteAudit(
  routes: Routes,
  options: SpotlightRouteAuditOptions = {},
): SpotlightRouteAuditEntry[] {
  const walked = walkRoutes(routes, false);
  const labelledUrls = new Set(walked.filter((w) => w.label !== null).map((w) => w.url));
  return walked
    .filter((w) => w.isPage && !w.hasParam && !w.optedOut && w.label === null && !labelledUrls.has(w.url))
    .filter((w) => !(options.ignore?.(w.route, w.url) ?? false))
    .map((w) => ({ path: w.url, reason: 'missing-label' as const }));
}
````

````ts
import { HttpTestingController, provideHttpClientTesting } from '@angular/common/http/testing';
import { Injector, provideZonelessChangeDetection } from '@angular/core';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { firstValueFrom, type Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { SPOTLIGHT_HTTP_DEFAULTS } from '../tokens';
import type { SpotlightContext, SpotlightHttpOptions, SpotlightResult } from '../types';
import { httpSource } from './http-source';

function setup(globalDefaults?: SpotlightHttpOptions) {
  TestBed.configureTestingModule({
    providers: [
      provideZonelessChangeDetection(),
      provideHttpClient(),
      provideHttpClientTesting(),
      ...(globalDefaults ? [{ provide: SPOTLIGHT_HTTP_DEFAULTS, useValue: globalDefaults }] : []),
    ],
  });
  const http = TestBed.inject(HttpTestingController);
  const ctx = (chip: string | null = null): SpotlightContext => ({
    signal: new AbortController().signal,
    chip,
    injector: TestBed.inject(Injector),
  });
  return { http, ctx };
}

const response = { groups: [{ id: 'customers', label: 'Customers', items: [] }] };

describe('httpSource', () => {
  afterEach(() => TestBed.inject(HttpTestingController).verify());

  it('has remote defaults', () => {
    setup();
    expect(httpSource({ url: '/api/search' })).toMatchObject({ id: 'http:/api/search', minLength: 2, debounceMs: 150, local: false });
    expect(httpSource({ url: '/x', id: 'x', minLength: 3, debounceMs: 0, group: 'X', priority: 2 })).toMatchObject({
      id: 'x',
      minLength: 3,
      debounceMs: 0,
      group: 'X',
      priority: 2,
    });
  });

  it('sends GET {url}?q= and passes the response through', async () => {
    const { http, ctx } = setup();
    const result = firstValueFrom(httpSource({ url: '/api/search' }).search('acme', ctx()) as Observable<SpotlightResult>);
    const req = http.expectOne((r) => r.url === '/api/search');
    expect(req.request.method).toBe('GET');
    expect(req.request.params.get('q')).toBe('acme');
    expect(req.request.params.has('chip')).toBe(false);
    req.flush(response);
    expect(await result).toEqual(response);
  });

  it('adds chip={groupId} when a chip is active', () => {
    const { http, ctx } = setup();
    void firstValueFrom(httpSource({ url: '/api/search' }).search('acme', ctx('orders')) as Observable<SpotlightResult>);
    const req = http.expectOne((r) => r.url === '/api/search');
    expect(req.request.params.get('chip')).toBe('orders');
    req.flush([]);
  });

  it('errors when the response is not a SpotlightResult', async () => {
    const { http, ctx } = setup();
    const result = firstValueFrom(httpSource({ url: '/api/search' }).search('acme', ctx()) as Observable<SpotlightResult>);
    http.expectOne('/api/search?q=acme').flush({ hits: [] });
    await expect(result).rejects.toThrow('[ng-spotlight] httpSource /api/search: response is neither');
  });

  it('uses the global request/map when the source sets none', async () => {
    const { http, ctx } = setup({
      request: (query) => ({ method: 'POST', body: { term: query }, headers: { 'x-global': '1' } }),
      map: (res) => (res as { data: SpotlightResult }).data,
    });
    const result = firstValueFrom(httpSource({ url: '/api/search' }).search('acme', ctx()) as Observable<SpotlightResult>);
    const req = http.expectOne('/api/search');
    expect(req.request.method).toBe('POST');
    expect(req.request.body).toEqual({ term: 'acme' });
    expect(req.request.headers.get('x-global')).toBe('1');
    req.flush({ data: response });
    expect(await result).toEqual(response);
  });

  it('lets per-source request/map win over the global ones', async () => {
    const { http, ctx } = setup({ request: () => ({ params: { global: 'yes' } }), map: () => [] });
    const source = httpSource({
      url: '/api/legacy',
      request: (query) => ({ params: { term: query } }),
      map: (res, query) => [{ id: query, title: String((res as { name: string }).name), group: 'Legacy', action: { route: '/l' } }],
    });
    const result = firstValueFrom(source.search('abc', ctx()) as Observable<SpotlightResult>);
    const req = http.expectOne((r) => r.url === '/api/legacy');
    expect(req.request.params.get('term')).toBe('abc');
    expect(req.request.params.has('global')).toBe(false);

  it('cancels the request when the subscriber goes away (next keystroke)', () => {
    const { http, ctx } = setup();

    const req = http.expectOne('/api/search?q=acme');
    sub.unsubscribe();
    expect(req.cancelled).toBe(true);

});
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/tokens.ts`:

````ts
import { InjectionToken } from '@angular/core';
import type { SpotlightHttpOptions } from './types';

/** Global request/map defaults for httpSource. Provided by provideSpotlight({ http }). */
export const SPOTLIGHT_HTTP_DEFAULTS = new InjectionToken<SpotlightHttpOptions>('SPOTLIGHT_HTTP_DEFAULTS');
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/sources/http-source.ts`:

````ts
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { map as mapResponse } from 'rxjs';
import { isSpotlightResponse } from '../normalize';
import { SPOTLIGHT_HTTP_DEFAULTS } from '../tokens';
import type { SpotlightContext, SpotlightHttpOptions, SpotlightHttpRequest, SpotlightResult, SpotlightSource } from '../types';

export interface HttpSourceOptions extends Partial<Omit<SpotlightSource, 'search'>>, SpotlightHttpOptions {
  url: string;
}

function defaultRequest(query: string, ctx: SpotlightContext): SpotlightHttpRequest {
  return { method: 'GET', params: ctx.chip !== null ? { q: query, chip: ctx.chip } : { q: query } };
}

/**
 * Remote results in the server's order. Default request: GET {url}?q={query}[&chip={groupId}].
 * Default response: SpotlightResponse or SpotlightItem[]. Per-source request/map win over
 * provideSpotlight({ http }), which wins over the defaults.
 */
export function httpSource(options: HttpSourceOptions): SpotlightSource {
  const { url, request, map, ...rest } = options;
  const defaultMap = (response: unknown): SpotlightResult => {
    if (Array.isArray(response) || isSpotlightResponse(response)) return response as SpotlightResult;
    throw new Error(`[ng-spotlight] httpSource ${url}: response is neither SpotlightResponse nor SpotlightItem[]`);
  };
  return {
    minLength: 2,
    debounceMs: 150,
    local: false,
    ...rest,
    id: rest.id ?? `http:${url}`,
    search: (query, ctx) => {
      const globals = ctx.injector.get(SPOTLIGHT_HTTP_DEFAULTS, {});
      const req = (request ?? globals.request ?? defaultRequest)(query, ctx);
      const toResult = map ?? globals.map ?? defaultMap;
      return ctx.injector
        .get(HttpClient)
        .request(req.method ?? 'GET', url, {
          params: req.params,
          headers: req.headers,
          body: req.body,
          responseType: 'json',
        })
        .pipe(mapResponse((response: unknown) => toResult(response, query)));
    },
  };
}
````

### Task 6: Search engine (fan-out, debounce, abort, timeout, chips, prefixes)

**Files:**
- Create: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/engine.ts`
- Modify: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/public-api.ts`
- Test: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/engine.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `normalizeResult`, `NormalizedGroup` (Task 2); `rankItems` (Task 3); `SpotlightSource`, `SpotlightItem`, `SpotlightResult` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000`
  - `interface SpotlightRegisteredSource { source: SpotlightSource; injector: Injector; order: number }`
  - `interface SpotlightQueryState { query: string; chip: string | null }`
  - `interface SpotlightGroupState { key: string; id: string; label: string; total?: number; sourceId: string; items: SpotlightItem[]; loading: boolean; error: unknown }` (`key` = `<sourceId>::<groupId>`; `error` is `null` when the source answered) — exported publicly as a type
  - `interface SpotlightEngineOptions { sources: readonly SpotlightRegisteredSource[]; input$: Observable<SpotlightQueryState>; retry$: Observable<string>; recentIds: () => ReadonlySet<string>; onError: (error: unknown, source: SpotlightSource) => void }`
  - `interface SpotlightQueryPlan { query: string; only: SpotlightSource | null }`; `planQuery(sources: readonly SpotlightRegisteredSource[], raw: string): SpotlightQueryPlan`
  - `sortSources(sources: readonly SpotlightRegisteredSource[]): SpotlightRegisteredSource[]`
  - `toObservableResult(result: ReturnType<SpotlightSource['search']>): Observable<SpotlightResult>`
  - `createSearchEngine(options: SpotlightEngineOptions): Observable<SpotlightGroupState[]>`

Behaviour (spec §5): trim; a registered prefix narrows to its source and is stripped (longest prefix wins; an empty remainder lists everything that source returns); each source runs with its own `minLength`/`debounceMs`/`timeoutMs`; `switchMap` plus `AbortSignal` drop stale answers; a group shows `loading: true` (with its previous items) until its source answers; groups are ordered by `priority ?? registration order`; local sources are ranked; the empty query keeps only `suggested` items; with a chip active only sources known to feed that group run and only that group is kept; a failing or timed-out source yields one `{ items: [], error }` group and an `onError` call, without touching other groups; `retry$.next(sourceId)` re-runs one source with the latest input.
