`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/engine.spec.ts`:

````ts
import { Injector } from '@angular/core';
import { BehaviorSubject, map, NEVER, of, Subject, throwError, timer } from 'rxjs';
import {
  createSearchEngine,
  planQuery,
  sortSources,
  type SpotlightGroupState,
  type SpotlightQueryState,
  type SpotlightRegisteredSource,
} from './engine';
import type { SpotlightItem, SpotlightResult, SpotlightSource } from './types';

const item = (id: string, title: string, group: string, extra: Partial<SpotlightItem> = {}): SpotlightItem => ({
  id,
  title,
  group,
  action: { route: `/${id}` },
  ...extra,
});

const regsOf = (sources: SpotlightSource[]): SpotlightRegisteredSource[] =>
  sources.map((source, order) => ({ source, injector: Injector.NULL, order }));

function harness(sources: SpotlightSource[]) {
  const input = new BehaviorSubject<SpotlightQueryState>({ query: '', chip: null });
  const retry = new Subject<string>();
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const emissions: SpotlightGroupState[][] = [];
  const sub = createSearchEngine({
    sources: regsOf(sources),
    input$: input,
    retry$: retry,
    recentIds: () => new Set<string>(),
    onError: (_error, source) => errors.push(source.id),
  }).subscribe((groups) => emissions.push(groups));
  const latest = () => emissions.at(-1) ?? [];
  const titles = () => latest().flatMap((g) => g.items.map((i) => i.title));
  const set = (query: string, chip: string | null = null) => input.next({ query, chip });
  return { retry, errors, emissions, sub, latest, titles, set };
}

const pages: SpotlightSource = {
  id: 'pages',
  group: 'Pages',
  local: true,
  search: () => [
    item('cust', 'Customers', 'Pages', { suggested: true }),
    item('ord', 'Orders', 'Pages'),
    item('set', 'Settings', 'Pages', { keywords: ['preferences'] }),
  ],
};

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

describe('planQuery', () => {
  const orders: SpotlightSource = { id: 'orders', prefix: '#', search: () => [] };
  const cmd: SpotlightSource = { id: 'cmd', prefix: '>', search: () => [] };
  const cmd2: SpotlightSource = { id: 'cmd2', prefix: '>>', search: () => [] };
  const regs = regsOf([pages, orders, cmd, cmd2]);

  it('trims and strips a registered prefix', () => {
    expect(planQuery(regs, '  #12 ')).toEqual({ query: '12', only: orders });
    expect(planQuery(regs, ' plain ')).toEqual({ query: 'plain', only: null });
  });

  it('lets the longest prefix win', () => {
    expect(planQuery(regs, '>>x').only).toBe(cmd2);
    expect(planQuery(regs, '>x').only).toBe(cmd);
  });
});

describe('sortSources', () => {
  it('orders by priority, then registration order', () => {
    const a: SpotlightSource = { id: 'a', search: () => [] };
    const b: SpotlightSource = { id: 'b', priority: 0, search: () => [] };
    const c: SpotlightSource = { id: 'c', search: () => [] };
    expect(sortSources(regsOf([a, b, c])).map((r) => r.source.id)).toEqual(['a', 'b', 'c']);
    const d: SpotlightSource = { id: 'd', priority: -1, search: () => [] };
    expect(sortSources(regsOf([a, b, c, d])).map((r) => r.source.id)).toEqual(['d', 'a', 'b', 'c']);
  });
});

describe('createSearchEngine', () => {
  it('emits local results in the same tick as the keystroke', () => {
    const h = harness([pages]);
    h.set('ord');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Orders']);
  });

  it('ranks local sources (keywords at lower weight) and keeps the order of other sources', () => {
    const remote: SpotlightSource = { id: 'remote', group: 'Remote', search: () => [item('b', 'Beta', 'Remote'), item('a', 'Alpha', 'Remote')] };
    const h = harness([pages, remote]);
    h.set('s');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Settings', 'Customers', 'Orders', 'Beta', 'Alpha']);
    h.set('pref');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Settings', 'Beta', 'Alpha']);
  });

  it('shows only suggested items on the empty query', () => {
    const h = harness([pages]);
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Customers']);
    h.set('   ');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Customers']);
  });

  it('runs only the prefixed source and strips the prefix; an empty remainder lists everything', () => {
    const seen: string[] = [];
    const orders: SpotlightSource = {
      id: 'orders',
      group: 'Orders',
      prefix: '#',
      local: true,
      search: (query) => {
        seen.push(query);
        return [item('1001', 'Order 1001', 'Orders'), item('1002', 'Order 1002', 'Orders')];
      },
    };
    const h = harness([pages, orders]);
    h.set('#1001');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Order 1001']);
    expect(seen.at(-1)).toBe('1001');
    h.set('#');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Order 1001', 'Order 1002']);
    h.set('ord');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Orders', 'Order 1001', 'Order 1002']);
  });

  it('respects minLength', () => {
    const calls: string[] = [];
    const remote: SpotlightSource = { id: 'r', group: 'R', minLength: 2, search: (q) => (calls.push(q), [item('x', 'X', 'R')]) };
    const h = harness([remote]);
    h.set('a');
    expect(calls).toEqual([]);
    h.set('ab');
    expect(calls).toEqual(['ab']);
  });

  it('debounces per source', () => {
    const calls: string[] = [];
    const remote: SpotlightSource = { id: 'r', group: 'R', debounceMs: 150, search: (q) => (calls.push(q), [item(q, q, 'R')]) };
    const h = harness([remote]);
    h.set('ab');
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(149);
    h.set('abc');
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(149);
    expect(calls).toEqual([]);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
    expect(calls).toEqual(['abc']);
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['abc']);
  });

  it('never renders a stale response and aborts the superseded request', () => {
    const signals: AbortSignal[] = [];
    const slow: SpotlightSource = {
      id: 'slow',
      group: 'Slow',
      minLength: 1,
      search: (q, ctx) => {
        signals.push(ctx.signal);
        return timer(q === 'a' ? 300 : 100).pipe(map(() => [item(q, `result ${q}`, 'Slow')]));
      },
    };
    const h = harness([slow]);
    h.set('a');
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(50);
    h.set('ab');
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(500);
    const everRendered = h.emissions.flatMap((groups) => groups.flatMap((g) => g.items.map((i) => i.title)));
    expect(everRendered).not.toContain('result a');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['result ab']);
    expect(signals[0].aborted).toBe(true);
    expect(signals[1].aborted).toBe(false);
  });

  it('resolves promise results', async () => {
    const promised: SpotlightSource = { id: 'p', group: 'P', search: () => Promise.resolve([item('x', 'From promise', 'P')]) };
    const h = harness([promised]);
    h.set('x');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1);
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['From promise']);
  });

  it('turns a timeout into an error group and reports it', () => {
    const hang: SpotlightSource = { id: 'hang', group: 'Hang', timeoutMs: 1000, search: () => NEVER };
    const h = harness([hang]);
    h.set('x');
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(999);
    expect(h.latest()[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 'Hang', loading: true, error: null });
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
    expect(h.latest()[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 'Hang', loading: false, items: [] });
    expect(h.latest()[0].error).toBeInstanceOf(Error);
    expect(h.errors).toEqual(['hang']);
  });

  it('isolates a throwing source to its own group', () => {
    const broken: SpotlightSource = {
      id: 'broken',
      group: 'Broken',
      search: () => {
        throw new Error('boom');
      },
    };
    const h = harness([pages, broken]);
    h.set('cust');
    expect(h.latest().map((g) => [g.id, g.items.length, g.error !== null])).toEqual([
      ['Pages', 1, false],
      ['Broken', 0, true],
    ]);
    expect(h.errors).toEqual(['broken', 'broken']);
  });

  it('isolates malformed payloads to their own group', () => {
    const bad: SpotlightSource = { id: 'bad', group: 'Bad', search: () => ({ groups: null }) as unknown as SpotlightResult };
    const half: SpotlightSource = {
      id: 'half',
      search: () =>
        ({
          groups: [
            { id: 'g', label: 'G', items: [{ id: 'no-title' }, null, item('fine', 'Fine', 'g'), { ...item('kid', 'Kid', 'g'), children: [] }] },
            { label: 'no id', items: [] },
          ],
        }) as unknown as SpotlightResult,
    };
    const h = harness([bad, half]);
    h.set('q');
    expect(h.latest().map((g) => [g.id, g.items.map((i) => i.title), g.error !== null])).toEqual([
      ['Bad', [], true],
      ['g', ['Fine'], false],
    ]);
  });

  it('re-runs one source on retry', () => {
    let fail = true;
    const flaky: SpotlightSource = {
      id: 'flaky',
      group: 'Flaky',
      search: () => (fail ? throwError(() => new Error('down')) : of([item('ok', 'Back online', 'Flaky')])),
    };
    const h = harness([flaky]);
    h.set('b');
    expect(h.latest()[0].error).not.toBeNull();
    fail = false;
    h.retry.next('other');
    expect(h.latest()[0].error).not.toBeNull();
    h.retry.next('flaky');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Back online']);
  });

  it('orders groups by priority and keeps a loading placeholder so later answers do not jump', () => {
    const late: SpotlightSource = { id: 'late', group: 'Late', priority: 0, search: () => timer(100).pipe(map(() => [item('l', 'Late item', 'Late')])) };
    const early: SpotlightSource = { id: 'early', group: 'Early', priority: 1, search: () => [item('e', 'Early item', 'Early')] };
    const h = harness([early, late]);
    h.set('x');
    expect(h.latest().map((g) => [g.id, g.loading])).toEqual([
      ['Late', true],
      ['Early', false],
    ]);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(100);
    expect(h.latest().map((g) => [g.id, g.loading])).toEqual([
      ['Late', false],
      ['Early', false],
    ]);
  });

  it('with a chip active runs only the sources feeding that group', () => {
    const pageCalls = vi.fn();
    const tracked: SpotlightSource = { ...pages, search: (q, c) => (pageCalls(q), pages.search(q, c)) };
    const commands: SpotlightSource = { id: 'cmd', group: 'Commands', local: true, search: () => [item('logout', 'Log out', 'Commands')] };
    const h = harness([tracked, commands]);
    pageCalls.mockClear();
    h.set('o', 'Commands');
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Log out']);
    expect(pageCalls).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('learns the groups of a multi-group source and passes the chip to it', () => {
    const chips: Array<string | null> = [];
    const multi: SpotlightSource = {
      id: 'multi',
      search: (_q, ctx) => {
        chips.push(ctx.chip);
        return {
          groups: [
            { id: 'customers', label: 'Customers', items: [item('c1', 'Acme', 'customers')] },
            { id: 'orders', label: 'Orders', items: [item('o1', 'Order 1', 'orders')] },
          ],
        };
      },
    };
    const h = harness([multi]);
    h.set('a');
    h.set('a', 'orders');
    expect(h.latest().map((g) => [g.id, g.label])).toEqual([['orders', 'Orders']]);
    expect(h.titles()).toEqual(['Order 1']);
    expect(chips.at(-1)).toBe('orders');
  });
});
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/engine.ts`:

````ts
import type { Injector } from '@angular/core';
import {
  catchError,
  combineLatest,
  filter,
  from,
  isObservable,
  map,
  merge,
  Observable,
  of,
  shareReplay,
  startWith,
  switchMap,
  timeout,
  timer,
  withLatestFrom,
  type Subscription,
} from 'rxjs';
import { normalizeResult, type NormalizedGroup } from './normalize';
import { rankItems } from './rank';
import type { SpotlightItem, SpotlightResult, SpotlightSource } from './types';

export const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000;

export interface SpotlightRegisteredSource {
  source: SpotlightSource;
  injector: Injector;
  order: number;
}

export interface SpotlightQueryState {
  query: string;
  chip: string | null;
}

export interface SpotlightGroupState {
  /** `<sourceId>::<groupId>`: unique across sources. */
  key: string;
  id: string;
  label: string;
  total?: number;
  sourceId: string;
  items: SpotlightItem[];
  loading: boolean;
  /** null when the source answered; the error otherwise. */
  error: unknown;
}

export interface SpotlightEngineOptions {
  sources: readonly SpotlightRegisteredSource[];
  input$: Observable<SpotlightQueryState>;
  retry$: Observable<string>;
  recentIds: () => ReadonlySet<string>;
  onError: (error: unknown, source: SpotlightSource) => void;
}

export interface SpotlightQueryPlan {
  query: string;
  only: SpotlightSource | null;
}

interface SourceState {
  groups: NormalizedGroup[];
  loading: boolean;
  error: unknown;
}

const IDLE: SourceState = { groups: [], loading: false, error: null };

/** sourceId → (groupId → label), learned from answers so chips can target multi-group sources. */
type LearnedGroups = Map<string, Map<string, string>>;

export function planQuery(sources: readonly SpotlightRegisteredSource[], raw: string): SpotlightQueryPlan {
  const trimmed = raw.trim();
  const prefixed = sources
    .filter((reg) => typeof reg.source.prefix === 'string' && reg.source.prefix !== '')
    .sort((a, b) => (b.source.prefix as string).length - (a.source.prefix as string).length);
  for (const reg of prefixed) {
    const prefix = reg.source.prefix as string;
    if (trimmed.startsWith(prefix)) return { query: trimmed.slice(prefix.length).trim(), only: reg.source };
  }
  return { query: trimmed, only: null };
}

export function sortSources(sources: readonly SpotlightRegisteredSource[]): SpotlightRegisteredSource[] {
  return [...sources].sort(
    (a, b) => (a.source.priority ?? a.order) - (b.source.priority ?? b.order) || a.order - b.order,
  );
}

export function toObservableResult(result: ReturnType<SpotlightSource['search']>): Observable<SpotlightResult> {
  if (isObservable(result)) return result;
  if (typeof (result as { then?: unknown }).then === 'function') return from(result as PromiseLike<SpotlightResult>);
  return of(result as SpotlightResult);
}

function knownGroups(learned: LearnedGroups, source: SpotlightSource): Map<string, string> {
  let known = learned.get(source.id);
  if (!known) {
    known = new Map<string, string>(source.group ? [[source.group, source.group]] : []);
    learned.set(source.id, known);
  }
  return known;
}

/** Calls search() with a fresh AbortSignal that is aborted if the call is superseded. */
function callSource(reg: SpotlightRegisteredSource, query: string, chip: string | null): Observable<SpotlightResult> {
  return new Observable<SpotlightResult>((subscriber) => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    let finished = false;
    let inner: Subscription | undefined;
    try {
      inner = toObservableResult(reg.source.search(query, { signal: controller.signal, chip, injector: reg.injector })).subscribe({
        next: (value) => subscriber.next(value),
        error: (error: unknown) => {
          finished = true;
          subscriber.error(error);
        },
        complete: () => {
          finished = true;
          subscriber.complete();
        },
      });
    } catch (error) {
      finished = true;
      subscriber.error(error);
    }
    return () => {
      if (!finished) controller.abort();
      inner?.unsubscribe();
    };
  });
}

function runSource(
  reg: SpotlightRegisteredSource,
  all: readonly SpotlightRegisteredSource[],
  input$: Observable<SpotlightQueryState>,
  options: SpotlightEngineOptions,
  learned: LearnedGroups,
): Observable<SourceState> {
  const source = reg.source;
  let last: NormalizedGroup[] = [];
  const retried$ = options.retry$.pipe(
    filter((id) => id === source.id),
    withLatestFrom(input$),
    map(([, state]) => state),
  );
  return merge(input$, retried$).pipe(
    switchMap((state): Observable<SourceState> => {
      const plan = planQuery(all, state.query);
      const known = knownGroups(learned, source);
      const eligible =
        (plan.only === null || plan.only === source) &&
        (state.chip === null || known.has(state.chip)) &&
        plan.query.length >= (source.minLength ?? 0);
      if (!eligible) {
        last = [];
        return of(IDLE);
      }
      const browseAll = plan.only !== null;
      const result$ = callSource(reg, plan.query, state.chip).pipe(
        timeout({ first: source.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS }),
        map((result): SourceState => {
          let groups = normalizeResult(result, source.group ?? source.id);
          for (const g of groups) known.set(g.id, g.label);
          if (state.chip !== null) groups = groups.filter((g) => g.id === state.chip);
          if (source.local === true && plan.query !== '') {
            const recent = options.recentIds();
            groups = groups.map((g) => ({ ...g, items: rankItems(g.items, plan.query, recent) }));
          }
          if (plan.query === '' && !browseAll) {
            groups = groups.map((g) => ({ ...g, items: g.items.filter((i) => i.suggested === true) }));
          }
          groups = groups.filter((g) => g.items.length > 0);
          last = groups;
          return { groups, loading: false, error: null };
        }),
        catchError((error: unknown) => {
          last = [];
          options.onError(error, source);
          return of<SourceState>({ groups: [], loading: false, error });
        }),
      );
      const debounceMs = source.debounceMs ?? 0;
      const delayed$ = debounceMs > 0 ? timer(debounceMs).pipe(switchMap(() => result$)) : result$;
      return delayed$.pipe(startWith<SourceState>({ groups: last, loading: true, error: null }));
    }),
  );
}

function toGroupStates(reg: SpotlightRegisteredSource, state: SourceState, learned: LearnedGroups): SpotlightGroupState[] {
  const source = reg.source;
  if (state.groups.length > 0) {
    return state.groups.map((g) => ({
      key: `${source.id}::${g.id}`,
      id: g.id,
      label: g.label,
      ...(g.total !== undefined ? { total: g.total } : {}),
      sourceId: source.id,
      items: g.items,
      loading: state.loading,
      error: null,
    }));
  }
  if (!state.loading && state.error === null) return [];
  // Placeholder header (spinner or error row) in the source's usual group.
  const known = learned.get(source.id);
  const id = source.group ?? (known ? [...known.keys()][0] : undefined);
  if (id === undefined) return [];
  return [
    {
      key: `${source.id}::${id}`,
      id,
      label: known?.get(id) ?? id,
      sourceId: source.id,
      items: [],
      loading: state.loading,
      error: state.error,
    },
  ];
}

/** Streams the grouped results for every query emitted on input$ (spec §5). */
export function createSearchEngine(options: SpotlightEngineOptions): Observable<SpotlightGroupState[]> {
  const sources = sortSources(options.sources);
  if (sources.length === 0) return of([]);
  const learned: LearnedGroups = new Map();
  const input$ = options.input$.pipe(shareReplay({ bufferSize: 1, refCount: true }));
  return combineLatest(
    sources.map((reg) =>
      runSource(reg, sources, input$, options, learned).pipe(map((state) => toGroupStates(reg, state, learned))),
    ),
  ).pipe(map((lists) => lists.flat()));
}
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/testing.spec-util.ts`:

````ts
import type { SpotlightRecentEntry, SpotlightRecentStorage } from './types';

/** In-memory Recent storage that records every save. */
export function memoryRecent(initial: SpotlightRecentEntry[] = []): SpotlightRecentStorage & { saved: SpotlightRecentEntry[][] } {
  let current = [...initial];
  const saved: SpotlightRecentEntry[][] = [];
  return {
    saved,
    load: () => Promise.resolve([...current]),
    save: (items) => {
      current = [...items];
      saved.push([...items]);
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
  };
}

/** Lets pending promises and zero-delay timers run. */
export const flush = (): Promise<void> => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 0));
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/spotlight.service.spec.ts`:

````ts
import { createEnvironmentInjector, EnvironmentInjector, inject, InjectionToken, provideZonelessChangeDetection } from '@angular/core';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { provideRouter, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { firstValueFrom, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { provideSpotlight } from './provide-spotlight';
import { staticSource } from './sources/static-source';
import { SpotlightService, type SpotlightOption } from './spotlight.service';
import { flush, memoryRecent } from './testing.spec-util';
import type { SpotlightConfig, SpotlightRecentEntry, SpotlightRecentStorage, SpotlightSource } from './types';

let ran: string[] = [];

const commands = staticSource({
  group: 'Commands',
  items: [
    { id: 'dash', title: 'Dashboard', suggested: true, action: { route: '/dashboard' } },
    { id: 'docs', title: 'Documentation', action: { url: 'https://example.com/docs' } },
    { id: 'evil', title: 'Evil link', action: { url: 'java\nscript:alert(1)' } },
    { id: 'hello', title: 'Say hello', action: { run: () => void ran.push('hello') } },
  ],
});

function setup(config: SpotlightConfig = {}) {
  const errors: Array<{ error: unknown; source: string }> = [];
  const storage = memoryRecent();
  TestBed.configureTestingModule({
    providers: [
      provideZonelessChangeDetection(),
      provideRouter([]),
      provideSpotlight({
        recent: { storage },
        onError: (error, source) => errors.push({ error, source: source.id }),
        sources: [commands],
        ...config,
      }),
    ],
  });
  const svc = TestBed.inject(SpotlightService);
  const navigate = vi.spyOn(TestBed.inject(Router), 'navigateByUrl').mockResolvedValue(true);
  return { svc, errors, storage, navigate };
}

const titles = (svc: SpotlightService) => svc.options().map((o) => o.item?.title ?? `retry:${o.sourceId}`);

function opt(svc: SpotlightService, title: string): SpotlightOption {
  const found = svc.options().find((o) => o.item?.title === title);
  if (!found) throw new Error(`no option "${title}" in ${JSON.stringify(titles(svc))}`);
  return found;
}

beforeEach(() => {
  ran = [];
});
afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

describe('SpotlightService', () => {
  it('starts closed and opens with the suggested items', async () => {
    const { svc } = setup();
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(false);
    svc.open();
    await flush();
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(true);
    expect(titles(svc)).toEqual(['Dashboard']);
  });

  it('filters in the same tick as the query changes and pre-selects the first option', () => {
    const { svc } = setup();
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('doc');
    expect(titles(svc)).toEqual(['Documentation']);
    expect(svc.activeIndex()).toBe(0);
    expect(svc.activeOption()?.item?.id).toBe('docs');
  });

  it('open(query) while open only updates the query', () => {
    const { svc } = setup();
    svc.open();
    svc.open('dash');
    expect(svc.query()).toBe('dash');
    expect(titles(svc)).toEqual(['Dashboard']);
  });

  it('moves the active option with wrap-around and resets it when the query changes', () => {
    const { svc } = setup();
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('a');
    expect(titles(svc)).toEqual(['Dashboard', 'Documentation', 'Say hello']);
    svc.moveActive(1);
    expect(svc.activeIndex()).toBe(1);
    svc.moveActive(-2);
    expect(svc.activeIndex()).toBe(2);
    svc.moveActive(1);
    expect(svc.activeIndex()).toBe(0);
    svc.setActive(svc.options()[2].key);
    svc.setQuery('as');
    expect(svc.activeIndex()).toBe(0);
  });

  it('navigates a route item, stores it in Recent, closes and emits selected', async () => {
    const { svc, navigate, storage } = setup();
    const selected = firstValueFrom(svc.selected);
    svc.open();
    await flush();
    svc.setQuery('dash');
    await svc.select(opt(svc, 'Dashboard'));
    await flush();
    expect(navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/dashboard');
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(false);
    expect((await selected).id).toBe('dash');
    expect(storage.saved.at(-1)).toEqual([
      { id: 'dash', title: 'Dashboard', group: 'Commands', sourceId: 'static:Commands', action: { route: '/dashboard' } },
    ]);
    svc.open();
    await flush();
    expect(svc.groups().map((g) => [g.id, g.label, g.items.map((i) => i.title)])).toEqual([['__recent', 'Recent', ['Dashboard']]]);
  });

  it('opens url items in a new tab with noopener when asked', async () => {
    const open = vi.spyOn(window, 'open').mockReturnValue(null);
    const { svc } = setup();
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('docu');
    await svc.select(opt(svc, 'Documentation'), { newTab: true });
    expect(open).toHaveBeenCalledWith('https://example.com/docs', '_blank', 'noopener');
  });

  it('blocks unsafe url schemes instead of navigating', async () => {
    const open = vi.spyOn(window, 'open').mockReturnValue(null);
    const { svc, errors, storage } = setup();
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('evil');
    await svc.select(opt(svc, 'Evil link'), { newTab: true });
    await flush();
    expect(open).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(errors).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(String(errors[0].error)).toContain('unsafe scheme');
    expect(errors[0].source).toBe('static:Commands');
    expect(storage.saved).toEqual([]);
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('awaits run() before closing and ignores a second select meanwhile', async () => {
    let resolveRun: () => void = () => undefined;
    let runs = 0;
    const slow = staticSource({
      id: 'slow',
      group: 'Slow',
      items: [
        {
          id: 'wait',
          title: 'Wait for it',
          action: {
            run: () => {
              runs += 1;
              return new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
                resolveRun = resolve;
              });
            },
          },
        },
      ],
    });
    const { svc } = setup({ sources: [slow] });
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('wait');
    const option = opt(svc, 'Wait for it');
    const done = svc.select(option);
    await flush();
    void svc.select(option);
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(true);
    resolveRun();
    await done;
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(false);
    expect(runs).toBe(1);
  });

  it('reports a rejecting run() and keeps it out of Recent', async () => {
    const failing = staticSource({
      id: 'failing',
      group: 'Failing',
      items: [{ id: 'boom', title: 'Explode', action: { run: () => Promise.reject(new Error('kaboom')) } }],
    });
    const { svc, errors, storage } = setup({ sources: [failing] });
    let emitted = false;
    svc.selected.subscribe(() => (emitted = true));
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('expl');
    await svc.select(opt(svc, 'Explode'));
    await flush();
    expect(errors).toEqual([{ error: new Error('kaboom'), source: 'failing' }]);
    expect(storage.saved).toEqual([]);
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(false);
    expect(emitted).toBe(false);
  });

  it('stores run() items by id and resolves them through their source on the next open', async () => {
    const storage = memoryRecent([
      { id: 'hello', title: 'Say hello', group: 'Commands', sourceId: 'static:Commands' },
      { id: 'ghost', title: 'Ghost', group: 'Gone', sourceId: 'removed-source' },
    ]);
    const { svc } = setup({ recent: { storage } });
    svc.open();
    await flush();
    expect(svc.groups()[0].items.map((i) => i.title)).toEqual(['Say hello']);
    await svc.select(svc.options()[0]);
    expect(ran).toEqual(['hello']);
  });

  it('survives a recent storage that rejects, throws or returns garbage', async () => {
    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const broken: SpotlightRecentStorage = {
      load: () => Promise.reject(new Error('blocked')),
      save: () => {
        throw new Error('quota');
      },
    };
    const { svc, navigate } = setup({ recent: { storage: broken } });
    svc.open();
    await flush();
    expect(titles(svc)).toEqual(['Dashboard']);
    svc.setQuery('dash');
    await svc.select(opt(svc, 'Dashboard'));
    await flush();
    expect(navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/dashboard');
    expect(warn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);

    TestBed.resetTestingModule();
    const garbage: SpotlightRecentStorage = {
      load: () => Promise.resolve([{ foo: 1 }, null, 'x'] as unknown as SpotlightRecentEntry[]),
      save: () => Promise.resolve(),
    };
    const second = setup({ recent: { storage: garbage } });
    second.svc.open();
    await flush();
    expect(second.svc.groups().map((g) => g.id)).toEqual(['Commands']);
  });

  it('shows a retry option for a failing source and re-runs it', async () => {
    let fail = true;
    const remote: SpotlightSource = {
      id: 'remote',
      group: 'Remote',
      search: () => (fail ? throwError(() => new Error('503')) : [{ id: 'r1', title: 'Recovered', group: 'Remote', action: { route: '/r1' } }]),
    };
    const { svc, errors } = setup({ sources: [remote] });
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('rec');
    expect(titles(svc)).toEqual(['retry:remote']);
    expect(errors.map((e) => e.source)).toContain('remote');
    fail = false;
    await svc.select(svc.options()[0]);
    expect(titles(svc)).toEqual(['Recovered']);
  });

  it('cycles chips and narrows the results to the chip group', () => {
    const pages = staticSource({ group: 'Pages', items: [{ id: 'p-docs', title: 'Docs home', action: { route: '/docs' } }] });
    const { svc } = setup({ sources: [commands, pages] });
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('do');
    expect(svc.chips().map((c) => c.label)).toEqual(['All', 'Commands', 'Pages']);
    svc.cycleChip(1);
    expect(svc.chip()).toBe('Commands');
    expect(new Set(svc.options().map((o) => o.groupKey))).toEqual(new Set(['static:Commands::Commands']));
    expect(svc.chips().map((c) => c.label)).toEqual(['All', 'Commands', 'Pages']);
    svc.cycleChip(-1);
    expect(svc.chip()).toBeNull();
    svc.cycleChip(-1);
    expect(svc.chip()).toBe('Pages');
  });

  it('announces the number of results', () => {
    const { svc } = setup();
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('do');
    expect(svc.announcement()).toBe('2 results');
  });

  it('adds sources from a child injector and removes them when it is destroyed', () => {
    const { svc } = setup();
    const child = createEnvironmentInjector(
      [provideSpotlight({ sources: [staticSource({ group: 'Admin', items: [{ id: 'users', title: 'Users', action: { route: '/admin/users' } }] })] })],
      TestBed.inject(EnvironmentInjector),
    );
    expect(child.get(SpotlightService)).toBe(svc);
    svc.open();
    svc.setQuery('users');
    expect(titles(svc)).toContain('Users');
    child.destroy();
    expect(titles(svc)).not.toContain('Users');
  });

  it('creates a separate instance when isolated', () => {
    const { svc } = setup();
    const child = createEnvironmentInjector([provideSpotlight({ isolated: true })], TestBed.inject(EnvironmentInjector));
    expect(child.get(SpotlightService)).not.toBe(svc);
    child.destroy();
  });

  it('accepts a config factory that runs in an injection context', () => {
    const PLACEHOLDER = new InjectionToken<string>('PLACEHOLDER', { factory: () => 'Find anything' });
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      providers: [provideZonelessChangeDetection(), provideRouter([]), provideSpotlight(() => ({ texts: { placeholder: inject(PLACEHOLDER) } }))],
    });
    expect(TestBed.inject(SpotlightService).texts.placeholder()).toBe('Find anything');
  });

  it('rejects an invalid hotkey at configuration time', () => {
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      providers: [provideZonelessChangeDetection(), provideRouter([]), provideSpotlight({ hotkeys: ['hyper.k'] })],
    });
    expect(() => TestBed.inject(SpotlightService)).toThrow(/unknown modifier "hyper"/);
  });

  it('close() resets the query, chip and results', () => {
    const { svc } = setup();
    svc.open('dash');
    svc.close();
    expect(svc.query()).toBe('');
    expect(svc.chip()).toBeNull();
    expect(svc.options()).toEqual([]);
  });
});
````

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the service**

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/spotlight.service.ts`:

````ts
import { DOCUMENT } from '@angular/common';
import { computed, DestroyRef, inject, Injectable, Injector, signal, type EnvironmentInjector } from '@angular/core';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { BehaviorSubject, firstValueFrom, Subject, timeout, type Observable, type Subscription } from 'rxjs';
import {
  createSearchEngine,
  DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS,
  toObservableResult,
  type SpotlightGroupState,
  type SpotlightQueryState,
  type SpotlightRegisteredSource,
} from './engine';
import { DEFAULT_HOTKEYS, detectMac, parseHotkey, type ParsedHotkey } from './hotkeys';
import { normalizeResult } from './normalize';
import { entryToItem, isRecentEntry, localStorageRecent, pushRecent, toRecentEntry } from './recent';
import { resolveTexts, type ResolvedSpotlightTexts } from './texts';
import type {
  SpotlightAction,
  SpotlightConfig,
  SpotlightItem,
  SpotlightRecentEntry,
  SpotlightRecentStorage,
  SpotlightSource,
} from './types';
import { isSafeUrl } from './url';

export const RECENT_GROUP_ID = '__recent';

export interface SpotlightOption {
  /** `<groupKey>::<itemId>`, or `retry::<sourceId>` for an error row. */
  key: string;
  /** DOM id of the role="option" element (target of aria-activedescendant). */
  domId: string;
  kind: 'item' | 'retry';
  item: SpotlightItem | null;
  sourceId: string;
  groupKey: string;
}

export interface SpotlightChip {
  /** Group id, or null for "All". */
  id: string | null;
  label: string;
}

export interface SpotlightSettings {
  hotkeys: readonly ParsedHotkey[];
  theme: 'default' | 'none';
  recentMax: number;
  storage: SpotlightRecentStorage;
  onError: (error: unknown, source: SpotlightSource) => void;
}

let nextInstanceId = 0;

const defaultOnError = (error: unknown, source: SpotlightSource): void => {
  console.error(`[ng-spotlight] source "${source.id}" failed`, error);
};

function parseHotkeys(specs: readonly string[]): ParsedHotkey[] {
  const isMac = detectMac();
  return specs.map((spec) => parseHotkey(spec, isMac));
}

function chipsFrom(groups: readonly SpotlightGroupState[]): SpotlightChip[] {
  const seen = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const group of groups) {
    if (group.items.length > 0 && !seen.has(group.id)) seen.set(group.id, group.label);
  }
  return [...seen].map(([id, label]) => ({ id, label }));
}

/**
 * State and behaviour of one palette. Provided by provideSpotlight(); never providedIn root.
 * Deliberately has no ngOnDestroy: a child injector re-provides the parent's instance and
 * must not destroy it. Cleanup runs through the creating injector's DestroyRef.
 */
@Injectable()
export class SpotlightService {
  private readonly document = inject(DOCUMENT);
  private readonly router = inject(Router, { optional: true });
  private readonly regs = signal<readonly SpotlightRegisteredSource[]>([]);
  private registrationOrder = 0;
  private configured = false;
  private selecting = false;

  /** Prefix for this instance's DOM ids. */
  readonly idPrefix = `sp-${nextInstanceId++}`;

  private _settings: SpotlightSettings = {
    hotkeys: parseHotkeys(DEFAULT_HOTKEYS),
    theme: 'default',
    recentMax: 8,
    storage: localStorageRecent('default'),
    onError: defaultOnError,
  };
  private readonly _texts = signal<ResolvedSpotlightTexts>(resolveTexts(undefined, inject(Injector)));

  private readonly _isOpen = signal(false);
  readonly isOpen = this._isOpen.asReadonly();
  private readonly _query = signal('');
  readonly query = this._query.asReadonly();
  private readonly _chip = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly chip = this._chip.asReadonly();
  private readonly _results = signal<readonly SpotlightGroupState[]>([]);
  private readonly _recent = signal<readonly SpotlightRecentEntry[]>([]);
  private readonly _resolvedRuns = signal<ReadonlyMap<string, SpotlightItem>>(new Map());
  private readonly _activeKey = signal<string | null>(null);
  private readonly _chipGroups = signal<readonly SpotlightChip[]>([]);
  private readonly selectedSubject = new Subject<SpotlightItem>();
  readonly selected: Observable<SpotlightItem> = this.selectedSubject.asObservable();

  private input$ = new BehaviorSubject<SpotlightQueryState>({ query: '', chip: null });
  private readonly retry$ = new Subject<string>();
  private engine: Subscription | null = null;

  private readonly recentItems = computed(() => {
    const runs = this._resolvedRuns();
    const out: Array<{ item: SpotlightItem; sourceId: string }> = [];
    for (const entry of this._recent()) {
      const item = entryToItem(entry) ?? runs.get(`${entry.sourceId}::${entry.id}`) ?? null;
      if (item) out.push({ item, sourceId: entry.sourceId });
    }
    return out;
  });

  /** Visible groups: Recent first on the empty query, then the sources' groups. */
  readonly groups = computed<readonly SpotlightGroupState[]>(() => {
    const results = this._results();
    if (this._query().trim() !== '' || this._chip() !== null) return results;
    const recent = this.recentItems();
    if (recent.length === 0) return results;
    const recentKeys = new Set(recent.map((r) => `${r.sourceId}::${r.item.id}`));
    const rest = results
      .map((g) => ({ ...g, items: g.items.filter((i) => !recentKeys.has(`${g.sourceId}::${i.id}`)) }))
      .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0 || g.loading || g.error !== null);
    const recentGroup: SpotlightGroupState = {
      key: RECENT_GROUP_ID,
      id: RECENT_GROUP_ID,
      label: this.texts.recent(),
      sourceId: RECENT_GROUP_ID,
      items: recent.map((r) => r.item),
      loading: false,
      error: null,
    };
    return [recentGroup, ...rest];
  });

  /** Every selectable row in display order, including one retry row per failed source. */
  readonly options = computed<readonly SpotlightOption[]>(() => {
    const out: SpotlightOption[] = [];
    const recent = this.recentItems();
    for (const group of this.groups()) {
      if (group.error !== null) {
        out.push({
          key: `retry::${group.sourceId}`,
          domId: `${this.idPrefix}-o${out.length}`,
          kind: 'retry',
          item: null,
          sourceId: group.sourceId,
          groupKey: group.key,
        });
        continue;
      }
      group.items.forEach((item, index) => {
        out.push({
          key: `${group.key}::${item.id}`,
          domId: `${this.idPrefix}-o${out.length}`,
          kind: 'item',
          item,
          sourceId: group.key === RECENT_GROUP_ID ? recent[index].sourceId : group.sourceId,
          groupKey: group.key,
        });
      });
    }
    return out;
  });

  readonly activeIndex = computed(() => {
    const options = this.options();
    if (options.length === 0) return -1;
    const key = this._activeKey();
    if (key === null) return 0;
    const index = options.findIndex((o) => o.key === key);
    return index === -1 ? 0 : index;
  });

  readonly activeOption = computed<SpotlightOption | null>(() => {
    const index = this.activeIndex();
    return index === -1 ? null : this.options()[index];
  });

  readonly chips = computed<readonly SpotlightChip[]>(() => [{ id: null, label: this.texts.all() }, ...this._chipGroups()]);

  readonly loading = computed(() => this._results().some((g) => g.loading));

  readonly announcement = computed(() =>
    this.texts.resultsAnnouncement(this.options().filter((o) => o.kind === 'item').length),
  );

  constructor() {
    inject(DestroyRef).onDestroy(() => {
      this.close();
      this.selectedSubject.complete();
    });
  }

  get texts(): ResolvedSpotlightTexts {
    return this._texts();
  }

  get settings(): Readonly<SpotlightSettings> {
    return this._settings;
  }

  /** @internal Called by provideSpotlight(). The first call owns the settings; every call adds its sources. */
  configure(config: SpotlightConfig, injector: EnvironmentInjector): void {
    if (!this.configured) {
      this.configured = true;
      this._settings = {
        hotkeys: parseHotkeys(config.hotkeys ?? DEFAULT_HOTKEYS),
        theme: config.theme ?? 'default',
        recentMax: config.recent?.max ?? 8,
        storage: config.recent?.storage ?? localStorageRecent('default'),
        onError: config.onError ?? defaultOnError,
      };
      this._texts.set(resolveTexts(config.texts, injector));
    }
    const added: SpotlightRegisteredSource[] = (config.sources ?? []).map((source) => ({
      source,
      injector,
      order: this.registrationOrder++,
    }));
    if (added.length === 0) return;
    this.regs.update((regs) => [...regs, ...added]);
    injector.onDestroy(() => {
      this.regs.update((regs) => regs.filter((reg) => !added.includes(reg)));
      this.restartEngine();
    });
    this.restartEngine();
  }

  open(query?: string): void {
    if (this._isOpen()) {
      if (query !== undefined) this.setQuery(query);
      return;
    }
    this._query.set(query ?? '');
    this._chip.set(null);
    this._activeKey.set(null);
    this._chipGroups.set([]);
    this._isOpen.set(true);
    void this.loadRecent();
    this.restartEngine();
  }

  close(): void {
    if (!this._isOpen()) return;
    this._isOpen.set(false);
    this.engine?.unsubscribe();
    this.engine = null;
    this._results.set([]);
    this._query.set('');
    this._chip.set(null);
    this._activeKey.set(null);
  }

  toggle(): void {
    if (this._isOpen()) this.close();
    else this.open();
  }

  setQuery(query: string): void {
    this._query.set(query);
    this._activeKey.set(null);
    this.input$.next({ query, chip: this._chip() });
  }

  setChip(chip: string | null): void {
    this._chip.set(chip);
    this._activeKey.set(null);
    this.input$.next({ query: this._query(), chip });
  }

  cycleChip(direction: 1 | -1): void {
    const chips = this.chips();
    if (chips.length < 2) return;
    const index = chips.findIndex((c) => c.id === this._chip());
    this.setChip(chips[(index + direction + chips.length) % chips.length].id);
  }

  moveActive(delta: number): void {
    const options = this.options();
    if (options.length === 0) return;
    const n = options.length;
    const next = (((this.activeIndex() + delta) % n) + n) % n;
    this._activeKey.set(options[next].key);
  }

  setActive(key: string): void {
    this._activeKey.set(key);
  }

  retry(sourceId: string): void {
    this.retry$.next(sourceId);
  }

  /** Runs the action, records Recent, closes and emits `selected` (spec §5.9). */
  async select(option: SpotlightOption, modifiers: { newTab?: boolean } = {}): Promise<void> {
    if (option.kind === 'retry' || option.item === null) {
      this.retry(option.sourceId);
      return;
    }
    if (this.selecting) return;
    this.selecting = true;
    const item = option.item;
    const source: SpotlightSource = this.regs().find((reg) => reg.source.id === option.sourceId)?.source ?? {
      id: option.sourceId,
      search: () => [],
    };
    try {
      await this.runAction(item.action, modifiers.newTab === true, source);
    } catch (error) {
      this.report(error, source);
      this.close();
      return;
    } finally {
      this.selecting = false;
    }
    this.remember(item, option.sourceId);
    this.close();
    this.selectedSubject.next(item);
  }

  private async runAction(action: SpotlightAction, newTab: boolean, source: SpotlightSource): Promise<void> {
    if ('run' in action) {
      await action.run();
      return;
    }
    if ('route' in action) {
      const router = this.router;
      if (!router) throw new Error('[ng-spotlight] a route action needs provideRouter()');
      const navigation =
        typeof action.route === 'string' ? router.navigateByUrl(action.route) : router.navigate(action.route);
      void navigation.catch((error: unknown) => this.report(error, source));
      return;
    }
    if (!isSafeUrl(action.url)) {
      throw new Error(`[ng-spotlight] blocked a url with an unsafe scheme: ${JSON.stringify(action.url)}`);
    }
    const view = this.document.defaultView;
    if (!view) return;
    if (action.newTab === true || newTab) view.open(action.url, '_blank', 'noopener');
    else view.location.assign(action.url);
  }

  private remember(item: SpotlightItem, sourceId: string): void {
    if ('run' in item.action) {
      const key = `${sourceId}::${item.id}`;
      this._resolvedRuns.update((runs) => new Map(runs).set(key, item));
    }
    const next = pushRecent(this._recent(), toRecentEntry(item, sourceId), this._settings.recentMax);
    this._recent.set(next);
    const storage = this._settings.storage;
    void Promise.resolve()
      .then(() => storage.save(next))
      .catch((error: unknown) => console.warn('[ng-spotlight] could not save recent items', error));
  }

  private async loadRecent(): Promise<void> {
    let loaded: unknown;
    try {
      loaded = await this._settings.storage.load();
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('[ng-spotlight] could not load recent items', error);
      loaded = [];
    }
    const entries = Array.isArray(loaded)
      ? (loaded as unknown[]).filter(isRecentEntry).slice(0, this._settings.recentMax)
      : [];
    this._recent.set(entries);
    await this.resolveRunEntries(entries);
  }

  /** run() entries are stored without an action; look them up in their source by id. */
  private async resolveRunEntries(entries: readonly SpotlightRecentEntry[]): Promise<void> {
    const pending = entries.filter((e) => e.action === undefined && !this._resolvedRuns().has(`${e.sourceId}::${e.id}`));
    for (const sourceId of new Set(pending.map((e) => e.sourceId))) {
      const reg = this.regs().find((r) => r.source.id === sourceId);
      if (!reg) continue;
      try {
        const result = await firstValueFrom(
          toObservableResult(
            reg.source.search('', { signal: new AbortController().signal, chip: null, injector: reg.injector }),
          ).pipe(timeout(reg.source.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS)),
        );
        const items = normalizeResult(result, reg.source.group ?? reg.source.id).flatMap((g) => g.items);
        this._resolvedRuns.update((runs) => {
          const next = new Map(runs);
          for (const item of items) next.set(`${sourceId}::${item.id}`, item);
          return next;
        });
      } catch {
        // An unresolvable run() entry stays hidden.
      }
    }
  }

  private restartEngine(): void {
    if (!this._isOpen()) return;
    this.engine?.unsubscribe();
    this.input$ = new BehaviorSubject<SpotlightQueryState>({ query: this._query(), chip: this._chip() });
    this.engine = createSearchEngine({
      sources: this.regs(),
      input$: this.input$,
      retry$: this.retry$,
      recentIds: () => new Set(this._recent().map((entry) => entry.id)),
      onError: (error, source) => this.report(error, source),
    }).subscribe((groups) => {
      this._results.set(groups);
      if (this._chip() === null) this._chipGroups.set(chipsFrom(groups));
    });
  }

  private report(error: unknown, source: SpotlightSource): void {
    try {
      this._settings.onError(error, source);
    } catch (handlerError) {
      console.error('[ng-spotlight] onError threw', handlerError);
    }
  }
}
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `provideSpotlight`**

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/provide-spotlight.ts`:

````ts
import {
  EnvironmentInjector,
  inject,
  InjectionToken,
  makeEnvironmentProviders,
  provideEnvironmentInitializer,
  type EnvironmentProviders,
} from '@angular/core';
import { SpotlightService } from './spotlight.service';
import { SPOTLIGHT_HTTP_DEFAULTS } from './tokens';
import type { SpotlightConfig } from './types';

const SPOTLIGHT_LOCAL_CONFIG = new InjectionToken<SpotlightConfig>('SPOTLIGHT_LOCAL_CONFIG');

/**
 * Sets up a palette. Call it once in the app config; call it again in a child injector (for
 * example a lazy route's `providers`) to add sources there. Pass a factory to build the config
 * in an injection context, e.g. `provideSpotlight(() => ({ texts: { placeholder: inject(I18n).t$('Search…') } }))`.
 */
export function provideSpotlight(config: SpotlightConfig | (() => SpotlightConfig) = {}): EnvironmentProviders {
  return makeEnvironmentProviders([
    { provide: SPOTLIGHT_LOCAL_CONFIG, useFactory: typeof config === 'function' ? config : () => config },
    {
      provide: SpotlightService,
      useFactory: (): SpotlightService => {
        const local = inject(SPOTLIGHT_LOCAL_CONFIG);
        const parent = local.isolated === true ? null : inject(SpotlightService, { skipSelf: true, optional: true });
        return parent ?? new SpotlightService();
      },
    },
    {
      provide: SPOTLIGHT_HTTP_DEFAULTS,
      useFactory: () =>
        inject(SPOTLIGHT_LOCAL_CONFIG).http ?? inject(SPOTLIGHT_HTTP_DEFAULTS, { skipSelf: true, optional: true }) ?? {},
    },
    provideEnvironmentInitializer(() => {
      inject(SpotlightService).configure(inject(SPOTLIGHT_LOCAL_CONFIG), inject(EnvironmentInjector));
    }),
  ]);
}
````

### Task 8: Renderer contract, default parts, default theme

**Files:**
- Create: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/renderer.ts`, `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/parts/default-parts.ts`, `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/parts/default-theme.ts`
- Modify: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/public-api.ts`
- Test: `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/parts/default-parts.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SpotlightItem`, `SpotlightBadge` (Task 2).
- Produces (all public):
  - Part contexts: `SpotlightInputContext { id: string; value: string; placeholder: string; label: string; expanded: boolean; controls: string; activeDescendant: string | null; onInput: (value: string) => void; onKeydown: (event: KeyboardEvent) => void }`, `SpotlightChipContext { label: string; active: boolean }`, `SpotlightGroupHeaderContext { label: string; total: number | undefined; loading: boolean; loadingText: string }`, `SpotlightRowContext { item: SpotlightItem; active: boolean; query: string; badge: Type<unknown> }`, `SpotlightBadgeContext { badge: SpotlightBadge }`
  - `interface SpotlightRenderer { dialog?: { panelClass: string; backdropClass?: string }; input?: Type<unknown>; chips?: Type<unknown>; groupHeader?: Type<unknown>; row?: Type<unknown>; badge?: Type<unknown> }`
  - `SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER: InjectionToken<SpotlightRenderer>`
  - Part contract: every part component declares `readonly ctx = input.required<XContext>()` and renders markup only. The host owns roles, ids and every ARIA attribute except those on the input element, which the `input` part must copy from its context (`role="combobox"`, `aria-autocomplete="list"`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`, `aria-activedescendant`, `aria-label`).
  - Core parts: `SpotlightDefaultInput` (`spotlight-default-input`), `SpotlightDefaultChip` (`spotlight-default-chip`), `SpotlightDefaultGroupHeader` (`spotlight-default-group-header`), `SpotlightDefaultRow` (`spotlight-default-row`), `SpotlightDefaultBadge` (`spotlight-default-badge`)
  - Internal: `interface SpotlightResolvedParts { panelClass: string | null; backdropClass: string | null; input; chips; groupHeader; row; badge: Type<unknown> }`, `resolveParts(renderer: SpotlightRenderer | null): SpotlightResolvedParts`
  - Internal: `SPOTLIGHT_DEFAULT_CSS: string`, `SpotlightDefaultTheme` (`spotlight-theme-default`, `ViewEncapsulation.None`). Its rules are scoped under `.sp-theme-default`, a class the host puts on the overlay pane and backdrop only when `theme: 'default'`, so `theme: 'none'` palettes stay unstyled even if another palette on the page loaded the CSS. Variables are declared on `:where(:root)` (zero specificity), so an app's own `:root { --sp-accent: … }` wins.

````ts
import { Component, provideZonelessChangeDetection, type Type } from '@angular/core';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import {
  resolveParts,
  SpotlightDefaultBadge,
  SpotlightDefaultChip,
  SpotlightDefaultGroupHeader,
  SpotlightDefaultInput,
  SpotlightDefaultRow,
} from './default-parts';
import { SPOTLIGHT_DEFAULT_CSS, SpotlightDefaultTheme } from './default-theme';
import type { SpotlightInputContext } from '../renderer';

async function render(type: Type<unknown>, ctx: unknown): Promise<HTMLElement> {
  TestBed.configureTestingModule({ providers: [provideZonelessChangeDetection()] });
  const fixture = TestBed.createComponent(type);
  fixture.componentRef.setInput('ctx', ctx);
  await fixture.whenStable();
  return fixture.nativeElement as HTMLElement;
}

describe('default parts', () => {
  it('input: renders the combobox with every ARIA attribute from its context and forwards events', async () => {
    const onInput = vi.fn();
    const onKeydown = vi.fn();
    const ctx: SpotlightInputContext = {
      id: 'sp-x-input',
      value: 'ord',
      placeholder: 'Search…',
      label: 'Search…',
      expanded: true,
      controls: 'sp-x-listbox',
      activeDescendant: 'sp-x-o0',
      onInput,
      onKeydown,
    };
    const el = await render(SpotlightDefaultInput, ctx);
    const input = el.querySelector('input')!;
    expect(input.getAttribute('role')).toBe('combobox');
    expect(input.getAttribute('aria-autocomplete')).toBe('list');
    expect(input.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('true');
    expect(input.getAttribute('aria-controls')).toBe('sp-x-listbox');
    expect(input.getAttribute('aria-activedescendant')).toBe('sp-x-o0');
    expect(input.getAttribute('aria-label')).toBe('Search…');
    expect(input.placeholder).toBe('Search…');
    expect(input.id).toBe('sp-x-input');
    expect(input.value).toBe('ord');
    input.value = 'orde';
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
    expect(onInput).toHaveBeenCalledWith('orde');
    const key = new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'ArrowDown' });
    input.dispatchEvent(key);
    expect(onKeydown).toHaveBeenCalledWith(key);
  });

  it('row: title, subtitle, class icon and badges through the badge part', async () => {
    const el = await render(SpotlightDefaultRow, {
      item: {
        id: 'c',
        title: 'Customers',
        subtitle: '/customers',
        icon: 'pi pi-users',
        group: 'Pages',
        badges: [{ label: 'new', tone: 'success' }, { label: 'beta' }],
        action: { route: '/customers' },
      },
      active: false,
      query: '',
      badge: SpotlightDefaultBadge,
    });
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-title')!.textContent).toBe('Customers');
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-subtitle')!.textContent).toBe('/customers');
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-icon i')!.className).toBe('pi pi-users');
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-icon')!.getAttribute('aria-hidden')).toBe('true');
    expect(Array.from(el.querySelectorAll('.sp-badge')).map((b) => [b.textContent, b.getAttribute('data-tone')])).toEqual([
      ['new', 'success'],
      ['beta', 'info'],
    ]);
  });

  it('row: image icons render as decorative images', async () => {
    const el = await render(SpotlightDefaultRow, {
      item: { id: 'a', title: 'Acme', icon: 'https://cdn.example.com/acme.png', group: 'g', action: { route: '/a' } },
      active: true,
      query: 'ac',
      badge: SpotlightDefaultBadge,
    });
    const img = el.querySelector('.sp-icon img')!;
    expect(img.getAttribute('src')).toBe('https://cdn.example.com/acme.png');
    expect(img.getAttribute('alt')).toBe('');
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-subtitle')).toBeNull();
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-badges')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('group header: label, total and a spinner with hidden loading text', async () => {
    const el = await render(SpotlightDefaultGroupHeader, { label: 'Customers', total: 42, loading: true, loadingText: 'Loading…' });
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-group-label')!.textContent).toBe('Customers');
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-group-total')!.textContent).toBe('42');
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-spinner')!.getAttribute('aria-hidden')).toBe('true');
    expect(el.textContent).toContain('Loading…');
  });

  it('chip: marks the active chip', async () => {
    const el = await render(SpotlightDefaultChip, { label: 'Orders', active: true });
    expect(el.querySelector('.sp-chip')!.classList).toContain('sp-chip-active');
    expect(el.textContent!.trim()).toBe('Orders');
  });

  it('resolveParts: core parts by default, renderer parts when given', () => {
    @Component({ selector: 'spotlight-test-row', template: '' })
    class CustomRow {}
    expect(resolveParts(null)).toEqual({
      panelClass: null,
      backdropClass: null,
      input: SpotlightDefaultInput,
      chips: SpotlightDefaultChip,
      groupHeader: SpotlightDefaultGroupHeader,
      row: SpotlightDefaultRow,
      badge: SpotlightDefaultBadge,
    });
    expect(resolveParts({ row: CustomRow, dialog: { panelClass: 'x', backdropClass: 'y' } })).toMatchObject({
      row: CustomRow,
      input: SpotlightDefaultInput,
      panelClass: 'x',
      backdropClass: 'y',
    });
  });
});

describe('default theme', () => {
  it('uses the spec CSS variables, has a dark variant and is scoped to .sp-theme-default', () => {
    for (const name of ['--sp-bg', '--sp-fg', '--sp-muted', '--sp-accent', '--sp-radius', '--sp-font', '--sp-shadow']) {
      expect(SPOTLIGHT_DEFAULT_CSS).toContain(`${name}:`);
    }
    expect(SPOTLIGHT_DEFAULT_CSS).toContain('@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
    expect(SPOTLIGHT_DEFAULT_CSS).toContain(':where(.sp-dark)');
    expect(SPOTLIGHT_DEFAULT_CSS).toContain('.sp-theme-default .sp-dialog');
    expect(SPOTLIGHT_DEFAULT_CSS).not.toMatch(/(^|\n)\.sp-dialog/);
  });

  it('adds the stylesheet to the document when rendered', async () => {
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({ providers: [provideZonelessChangeDetection()] });
    const fixture = TestBed.createComponent(SpotlightDefaultTheme);
    await fixture.whenStable();
    const css = Array.from(document.head.querySelectorAll('style')).map((s) => s.textContent ?? '').join('\n');
    expect(css).toContain('.sp-theme-default .sp-dialog');
  });
});
````

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the renderer contract**

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/renderer.ts`:

````ts
import { InjectionToken, type Type } from '@angular/core';
import type { SpotlightBadge, SpotlightItem } from './types';

/** Context of the `input` part. The part must copy every ARIA field onto its <input>. */
export interface SpotlightInputContext {
  id: string;
  value: string;
  placeholder: string;
  label: string;
  expanded: boolean;
  controls: string;
  activeDescendant: string | null;
  onInput: (value: string) => void;
  onKeydown: (event: KeyboardEvent) => void;
}

/** Context of the `chips` part (rendered once per chip, inside a host-owned <button>). */
export interface SpotlightChipContext {
  label: string;
  active: boolean;
}

export interface SpotlightGroupHeaderContext {
  label: string;
  total: number | undefined;
  loading: boolean;
  loadingText: string;
}

/** Context of the `row` part (rendered inside a host-owned role="option" element). */
export interface SpotlightRowContext {
  item: SpotlightItem;
  active: boolean;
  query: string;
  /** The badge part to render badges with (NgComponentOutlet, input `ctx: SpotlightBadgeContext`). */
  badge: Type<unknown>;
}

export interface SpotlightBadgeContext {
  badge: SpotlightBadge;
}

/**
 * Markup for each part of the palette. Every component takes one `ctx` input with the
 * part's context. Parts left out fall back to the core parts. `dialog` adds classes to the
 * overlay pane and backdrop (the core always owns the CDK overlay itself).
 */
export interface SpotlightRenderer {
  dialog?: { panelClass: string; backdropClass?: string };
  input?: Type<unknown>;
  chips?: Type<unknown>;
  groupHeader?: Type<unknown>;
  row?: Type<unknown>;
  badge?: Type<unknown>;
}

export const SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER = new InjectionToken<SpotlightRenderer>('SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER');
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the core parts and the theme**

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/parts/default-parts.ts`:

````ts
import { NgComponentOutlet } from '@angular/common';
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, input, type Type } from '@angular/core';
import type {
  SpotlightBadgeContext,
  SpotlightChipContext,
  SpotlightGroupHeaderContext,
  SpotlightInputContext,
  SpotlightRenderer,
  SpotlightRowContext,
} from '../renderer';

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-default-input',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <input
      class="sp-input-el"
      type="text"
      role="combobox"
      autocomplete="off"
      spellcheck="false"
      aria-autocomplete="list"
      [id]="ctx().id"
      [value]="ctx().value"
      [attr.placeholder]="ctx().placeholder"
      [attr.aria-label]="ctx().label"
      [attr.aria-expanded]="ctx().expanded"
      [attr.aria-controls]="ctx().controls"
      [attr.aria-activedescendant]="ctx().activeDescendant"
      (input)="onInput($event)"
      (keydown)="ctx().onKeydown($event)"
    />
  `,
})
export class SpotlightDefaultInput {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightInputContext>();

  protected onInput(event: Event): void {
    this.ctx().onInput((event.target as HTMLInputElement).value);
  }
}

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-default-chip',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `<span class="sp-chip" [class.sp-chip-active]="ctx().active">{{ ctx().label }}</span>`,
})
export class SpotlightDefaultChip {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightChipContext>();
}

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-default-group-header',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <span class="sp-group-label">{{ ctx().label }}</span>
    @if (ctx().total !== undefined) {
      <span class="sp-group-total">{{ ctx().total }}</span>
    }
    @if (ctx().loading) {
      <span class="sp-spinner" aria-hidden="true"></span>
      <span
        style="position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;margin:-1px;padding:0;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0 0 0 0);white-space:nowrap;border:0"
        >{{ ctx().loadingText }}</span
      >
    }
  `,
})
export class SpotlightDefaultGroupHeader {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightGroupHeaderContext>();
}

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-default-badge',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `<span class="sp-badge" [attr.data-tone]="ctx().badge.tone ?? 'info'">{{ ctx().badge.label }}</span>`,
})
export class SpotlightDefaultBadge {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightBadgeContext>();
}

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-default-row',
  imports: [NgComponentOutlet],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    @let item = ctx().item;
    @if (item.icon) {
      <span class="sp-icon" aria-hidden="true">
        @if (isImage(item.icon)) {
          <img [src]="item.icon" alt="" />
        } @else {
          <i [class]="item.icon"></i>
        }
      </span>
    }
    <span class="sp-text">
      <span class="sp-title">{{ item.title }}</span>
      @if (item.subtitle) {
        <span class="sp-subtitle">{{ item.subtitle }}</span>
      }
    </span>
    @if (item.badges?.length) {
      <span class="sp-badges">
        @for (badge of item.badges ?? []; track $index) {
          <ng-container *ngComponentOutlet="ctx().badge; inputs: { ctx: { badge: badge } }" />
        }
      </span>
    }
  `,
})
export class SpotlightDefaultRow {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightRowContext>();

  protected isImage(icon: string): boolean {
    return /^(https?:|data:image\/|\/|\.)/.test(icon);
  }
}

export interface SpotlightResolvedParts {
  panelClass: string | null;
  backdropClass: string | null;
  input: Type<unknown>;
  chips: Type<unknown>;
  groupHeader: Type<unknown>;
  row: Type<unknown>;
  badge: Type<unknown>;
}

export function resolveParts(renderer: SpotlightRenderer | null): SpotlightResolvedParts {
  return {
    panelClass: renderer?.dialog?.panelClass ?? null,
    backdropClass: renderer?.dialog?.backdropClass ?? null,
    input: renderer?.input ?? SpotlightDefaultInput,
    chips: renderer?.chips ?? SpotlightDefaultChip,
    groupHeader: renderer?.groupHeader ?? SpotlightDefaultGroupHeader,
    row: renderer?.row ?? SpotlightDefaultRow,
    badge: renderer?.badge ?? SpotlightDefaultBadge,
  };
}
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/parts/default-theme.ts`:

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, ViewEncapsulation } from '@angular/core';

/**
 * The default theme: CSS variables only, light and dark. Variables sit on :where(:root)
 * (zero specificity) so apps override them with a plain :root or pane-class rule. Every
 * other rule is scoped under .sp-theme-default (set on the pane/backdrop by the host).
 */
export const SPOTLIGHT_DEFAULT_CSS = `
:where(:root) {
  --sp-bg: #ffffff;
  --sp-fg: #1f2328;
  --sp-muted: #59636e;
  --sp-accent: #0969da;
  --sp-accent-fg: #ffffff;
  --sp-active-bg: #ddf4ff;
  --sp-border: #d1d9e0;
  --sp-backdrop: rgba(31, 35, 40, 0.4);
  --sp-radius: 12px;
  --sp-font: system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
  --sp-shadow: 0 16px 48px rgba(31, 35, 40, 0.24);
  --sp-info: #0969da;
  --sp-success: #1a7f37;
  --sp-warn: #9a6700;
  --sp-danger: #d1242f;
}
@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {
  :where(:root:not(.sp-light)) {
    --sp-bg: #151b23;
    --sp-fg: #f0f6fc;
    --sp-muted: #9198a1;
    --sp-accent: #4493f8;
    --sp-accent-fg: #0d1117;
    --sp-active-bg: #1d2d44;
    --sp-border: #3d444d;
    --sp-backdrop: rgba(1, 4, 9, 0.6);
    --sp-shadow: 0 16px 48px rgba(1, 4, 9, 0.6);
    --sp-info: #4493f8;
    --sp-success: #3fb950;
    --sp-warn: #d29922;
    --sp-danger: #f85149;
  }
}
:where(.sp-dark) {
  --sp-bg: #151b23;
  --sp-fg: #f0f6fc;
  --sp-muted: #9198a1;
  --sp-accent: #4493f8;
  --sp-accent-fg: #0d1117;
  --sp-active-bg: #1d2d44;
  --sp-border: #3d444d;
  --sp-backdrop: rgba(1, 4, 9, 0.6);
  --sp-shadow: 0 16px 48px rgba(1, 4, 9, 0.6);
  --sp-info: #4493f8;
  --sp-success: #3fb950;
  --sp-warn: #d29922;
  --sp-danger: #f85149;
}
.sp-backdrop.sp-theme-default { background: var(--sp-backdrop); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-dialog {
  box-sizing: border-box; display: flex; flex-direction: column; width: 100%; max-height: min(70vh, 640px);
  background: var(--sp-bg); color: var(--sp-fg); font-family: var(--sp-font); font-size: 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--sp-border); border-radius: var(--sp-radius); box-shadow: var(--sp-shadow); overflow: hidden;
}
.sp-theme-default.sp-sheet .sp-dialog { height: 100%; max-height: none; border: 0; border-radius: 0; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-input { display: flex; align-items: center; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--sp-border); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-input:focus-within { box-shadow: inset 0 -2px 0 var(--sp-accent); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-input-el {
  flex: 1; min-width: 0; padding: 16px; border: 0; outline: none; background: transparent; color: inherit; font: inherit; font-size: 16px;
}
.sp-theme-default .sp-input-el::placeholder { color: var(--sp-muted); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-chips { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 6px; padding: 8px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--sp-border); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-chip-button { padding: 0; border: 0; background: none; color: inherit; font: inherit; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 999px; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-chip { display: inline-block; padding: 2px 10px; border: 1px solid var(--sp-border); border-radius: 999px; color: var(--sp-muted); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-chip-active { background: var(--sp-accent); border-color: var(--sp-accent); color: var(--sp-accent-fg); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-list { flex: 1; overflow-y: auto; padding: 4px 0; outline: none; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-group + .sp-group { border-top: 1px solid var(--sp-border); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-group-header {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 8px 16px 4px; color: var(--sp-muted);
  font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.04em;
}
.sp-theme-default .sp-group-total { font-weight: 400; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-option { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; padding: 8px 16px; cursor: pointer; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-option[aria-selected='true'] { background: var(--sp-active-bg); box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--sp-accent); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-icon { display: inline-flex; justify-content: center; width: 20px; color: var(--sp-muted); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-icon img { width: 20px; height: 20px; object-fit: contain; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-text { display: flex; flex: 1; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-title, .sp-theme-default .sp-subtitle { overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-subtitle { color: var(--sp-muted); font-size: 12px; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-badges { display: inline-flex; gap: 4px; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-badge { padding: 0 6px; border: 1px solid currentColor; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 11px; line-height: 18px; color: var(--sp-info); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-badge[data-tone='success'] { color: var(--sp-success); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-badge[data-tone='warn'] { color: var(--sp-warn); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-badge[data-tone='danger'] { color: var(--sp-danger); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-error { flex: 1; color: var(--sp-muted); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-retry { color: var(--sp-accent); text-decoration: underline; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-empty { padding: 24px 16px; text-align: center; color: var(--sp-muted); }
.sp-theme-default .sp-footer {
  display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 16px; padding: 8px 16px; border-top: 1px solid var(--sp-border); color: var(--sp-muted); font-size: 12px;
}
.sp-theme-default .sp-footer kbd { padding: 0 4px; border: 1px solid var(--sp-border); border-radius: 4px; font-family: inherit; }
.sp-theme-default .sp-spinner {
  width: 10px; height: 10px; border: 2px solid var(--sp-border); border-top-color: var(--sp-accent); border-radius: 50%;
  animation: sp-spin 0.8s linear infinite;
}
@keyframes sp-spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .sp-theme-default .sp-spinner { animation: none; } }
`;

/** Rendered by <spotlight-host> only when theme is 'default'; theme 'none' never loads this CSS. */
@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-theme-default',
  template: '',
  styles: [SPOTLIGHT_DEFAULT_CSS],
  encapsulation: ViewEncapsulation.None,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  host: { style: 'display:none' },
})
export class SpotlightDefaultTheme {}
````

````ts
import { BreakpointObserver } from '@angular/cdk/layout';
import { Component, provideZonelessChangeDetection, type Provider, type Type } from '@angular/core';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';

@Component({

      </ng-template>
      <ng-template spotlightItem let-item><span class="tpl-fallback">{{ item.title }}</span></ng-template>
      <ng-template spotlightEmpty let-query><span class="tpl-empty">Nothing for "{{ query }}"</span></ng-template>
      <ng-template spotlightFooter><span class="tpl-footer">Custom footer</span></ng-template>

})

const pages = staticSource({
  group: 'Pages',
  items: [
    { id: 'dash', title: 'Dashboard', suggested: true, icon: 'pi pi-home', action: { route: '/dashboard' } },
    { id: 'cust', title: 'Customers', badges: [{ label: 'new', tone: 'success' }], action: { route: '/customers' } },
  ],
});
const orders = staticSource({ group: 'Orders', prefix: '#', items: [{ id: '1001', title: 'Order 1001', action: { route: '/orders/1001' } }] });

async function setup(config: SpotlightConfig = {}, wrapper: Type<unknown> = PlainWrapper, providers: Provider[] = []) {
  TestBed.configureTestingModule({
    providers: [
      provideZonelessChangeDetection(),
````
