````ts
  return { fixture, svc, navigate, stable };
}

const q = <T extends HTMLElement = HTMLElement>(selector: string) => document.querySelector<T>(selector);
const qa = (selector: string) => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(selector));
const combobox = () => q<HTMLInputElement>('[role="combobox"]')!;
const rowTitles = () => qa('[role="option"] .sp-title').map((e) => e.textContent);

function typeInto(text: string): void {
  const input = combobox();
  input.value = text;
  input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
}

function press(target: EventTarget, init: KeyboardEventInit): void {
  target.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, ...init }));
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  document.documentElement.classList.remove('sp-dark');
});

describe('SpotlightHost', () => {
  it('renders the combobox pattern and focuses the input when opened', async () => {
    const { svc, stable } = await setup();
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    const input = combobox();
    const listbox = q('[role="listbox"]')!;
    const options = qa('[role="option"]');
    expect(q('[role="dialog"]')!.getAttribute('aria-modal')).toBe('true');
    expect(input.getAttribute('aria-controls')).toBe(listbox.id);
    expect(input.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('true');
    expect(rowTitles()).toEqual(['Dashboard']);
    expect(input.getAttribute('aria-activedescendant')).toBe(options[0].id);
    expect(options[0].getAttribute('aria-selected')).toBe('true');
    expect(q('[role="group"]')!.getAttribute('aria-labelledby')).toBe(q('.sp-group-header')!.id);
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(input);
  });

  it('navigates with the arrow keys, selects with Enter and restores focus', async () => {
    const { navigate, stable } = await setup();
    const trigger = q<HTMLButtonElement>('#trigger')!;
    trigger.focus();
    trigger.click();
    await stable();
    typeInto('o');
    await stable();
    expect(rowTitles()).toEqual(['Dashboard', 'Customers', 'Order 1001']);
    press(combobox(), { key: 'ArrowDown' });
    await stable();
    const active = document.getElementById(combobox().getAttribute('aria-activedescendant')!)!;
    expect(active.querySelector('.sp-title')!.textContent).toBe('Customers');
    press(combobox(), { key: 'ArrowUp' });
    press(combobox(), { key: 'ArrowUp' });
    await stable();
    expect(document.getElementById(combobox().getAttribute('aria-activedescendant')!)!.textContent).toContain('Order 1001');
    press(combobox(), { key: 'Enter' });
    await stable();
    expect(navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/orders/1001');
    expect(q('[role="dialog"]')).toBeNull();
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(trigger);
  });

  it('selects with a mouse click', async () => {
    const { svc, navigate, stable } = await setup();
    svc.open('cust');
    await stable();
    qa('[role="option"]')[0].click();
    await stable();
    expect(navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/customers');
  });

  it('closes on Escape and on a backdrop click', async () => {
    const { svc, stable } = await setup();
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    press(combobox(), { key: 'Escape' });
    await stable();
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(false);
    expect(q('[role="dialog"]')).toBeNull();
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    q('.cdk-overlay-backdrop')!.click();
    await stable();
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('toggles on the hotkeys and ignores IME composition', async () => {
    const { svc, stable } = await setup();
    press(document.body, { key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true });
    await stable();
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(true);
    press(document.body, { key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true });
    await stable();
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(false);
    press(document.body, { key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true, isComposing: true });
    await stable();
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(false);
    press(document.body, { key: 'k', code: 'KeyK', ctrlKey: true });
    await stable();
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(true);
  });

  it('keeps a single overlay under key repeat and repeated open()', async () => {
    const { svc, stable } = await setup();
    const trigger = q<HTMLButtonElement>('#trigger')!;
    trigger.focus();
    press(document.body, { key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true });
    await stable();
    press(document.body, { key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true, repeat: true });
    press(document.body, { key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true, repeat: true });
    await stable();
    expect(svc.isOpen()).toBe(true);
    svc.open();
    svc.open('dash');
    await stable();
    expect(qa('.sp-pane')).toHaveLength(1);
    press(document.body, { key: ' ', code: 'Space', ctrlKey: true });
    await stable();
    expect(qa('.sp-pane')).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(trigger);
    svc.open();
    svc.close();
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    expect(qa('.sp-pane')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('opens with the trigger value as the query', async () => {
    const { stable } = await setup();
    q<HTMLButtonElement>('#hash')!.click();
    await stable();
    expect(combobox().value).toBe('#');
    expect(rowTitles()).toEqual(['Order 1001']);
    expect(q('#hash')!.getAttribute('aria-haspopup')).toBe('dialog');
  });

  it('cycles chips with Tab and Shift+Tab', async () => {
    const { svc, stable } = await setup();
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    typeInto('o');
    await stable();
    const chips = () => qa('.sp-chip-button');
    expect(chips().map((c) => c.textContent?.trim())).toEqual(['All', 'Pages', 'Orders']);
    expect(chips()[0].getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    press(combobox(), { key: 'Tab' });
    await stable();
    expect(chips()[1].getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expect(rowTitles()).toEqual(['Dashboard', 'Customers']);
    press(combobox(), { key: 'Tab', shiftKey: true });
    await stable();
    expect(chips()[0].getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    chips()[2].click();
    await stable();
    expect(rowTitles()).toEqual(['Order 1001']);
  });

  it('shows an error row with Retry that re-runs the source on Enter', async () => {
    let fail = true;
    const flaky: SpotlightSource = {
      id: 'flaky',
      group: 'Flaky',
      search: () =>
        fail ? throwError(() => new Error('down')) : [{ id: 'up', title: 'Back up', group: 'Flaky', suggested: true, action: { route: '/up' } }],
    };
    const { svc, stable } = await setup({ sources: [flaky], onError: () => undefined });
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    const row = qa('[role="option"]')[0];
    expect(row.textContent).toContain('Something went wrong');
    expect(row.textContent).toContain('Retry');
    fail = false;
    press(combobox(), { key: 'Enter' });
    await stable();
    expect(rowTitles()).toEqual(['Back up']);
  });

  it('uses per-group, fallback, empty and footer templates in that precedence', async () => {
    const { svc, stable } = await setup({}, TemplatedWrapper);
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    typeInto('o');
    await stable();
    expect(qa('.tpl-orders').map((e) => e.textContent)).toEqual(['#Order 1001']);
    expect(qa('.tpl-fallback').map((e) => e.textContent)).toEqual(['Dashboard', 'Customers']);
    expect(q('.tpl-fallback')!.closest('[role="option"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(q('.tpl-footer')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(q('.sp-footer kbd')).toBeNull();
    typeInto('zzz');
    await stable();
    expect(q('.tpl-empty')!.textContent).toBe('Nothing for "zzz"');
  });

  it('shows the default empty state, footer hints and live announcement', async () => {
    const { svc, stable } = await setup();
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    typeInto('o');
    await stable();
    expect(q('[role="status"]')!.textContent!.trim()).toBe('3 results');
    expect(qa('.sp-footer kbd').map((k) => k.textContent)).toEqual(['↑', '↓', '↵', 'esc']);
    typeInto('zzz');
    await stable();
    expect(q('.sp-empty')!.textContent!.trim()).toBe('No results');
    expect(q('[role="status"]')!.textContent!.trim()).toBe('No results');
  });

  it('becomes a full-screen sheet at phone width', async () => {
    const { svc, stable } = await setup({}, PlainWrapper, [{ provide: BreakpointObserver, useValue: { isMatched: () => true } }]);
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    expect(q('.sp-pane')!.classList).toContain('sp-sheet');
  });

  it("loads the default theme, and no theme for theme: 'none'", async () => {
    const first = await setup();
    first.svc.open();
    await first.stable();
    expect(q('.sp-pane')!.classList).toContain('sp-theme-default');
    expect(q('.cdk-overlay-backdrop')!.classList).toContain('sp-theme-default');
    expect(q('spotlight-theme-default')).not.toBeNull();
    first.fixture.destroy();
    TestBed.resetTestingModule();

    const second = await setup({ theme: 'none' });
    second.svc.open();
    await second.stable();
    expect(q('.sp-pane')!.classList).not.toContain('sp-theme-default');
    expect(q('spotlight-theme-default')).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each(['light', 'dark'])('has no axe violations with the open overlay (%s)', async (mode) => {
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle('sp-dark', mode === 'dark');
    const { svc, stable } = await setup();
    svc.open();
    await stable();
    typeInto('o');
    await stable();
    // The CDK focus-trap anchors are aria-hidden tab stops by design; they are tested upstream.
    const results = await axe.run(
      { exclude: ['.cdk-focus-trap-anchor'] },
      { runOnly: { type: 'tag', values: ['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21a', 'wcag21aa', 'wcag22aa'] } },
    );
    expect(results.violations.map((v) => `${v.id}: ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(' ')).join(', ')}`)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
````

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the template directives and the trigger**

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/templates.ts`:

````ts
import { Directive, inject, input, TemplateRef } from '@angular/core';
import { SpotlightService } from './spotlight.service';
import type { SpotlightItem } from './types';

export interface SpotlightItemTemplateContext {
  $implicit: SpotlightItem;
  active: boolean;
  query: string;
}

export interface SpotlightEmptyTemplateContext {
  /** The current query. */
  $implicit: string;
}

/** `<ng-template spotlightItem="customers" let-item>`: row for one group; without a value: for every group. */
@Directive({ selector: '[spotlightItem]' })
export class SpotlightItemTemplate {
  readonly spotlightItem = input('');
  readonly template = inject<TemplateRef<SpotlightItemTemplateContext>>(TemplateRef);

  static ngTemplateContextGuard(_dir: SpotlightItemTemplate, ctx: unknown): ctx is SpotlightItemTemplateContext {
    return true;
  }
}

/** `<ng-template spotlightEmpty let-query>`: shown when a non-empty query has no results. */
@Directive({ selector: '[spotlightEmpty]' })
export class SpotlightEmptyTemplate {
  readonly template = inject<TemplateRef<SpotlightEmptyTemplateContext>>(TemplateRef);

  static ngTemplateContextGuard(_dir: SpotlightEmptyTemplate, ctx: unknown): ctx is SpotlightEmptyTemplateContext {
    return true;
  }
}

/** `<ng-template spotlightFooter>`: replaces the default key hints. */
@Directive({ selector: '[spotlightFooter]' })
export class SpotlightFooterTemplate {
  readonly template = inject<TemplateRef<void>>(TemplateRef);
}

/** Opens the palette on click. `spotlightTrigger="#"` opens it with an initial query. */
@Directive({
  selector: '[spotlightTrigger]',
  host: { '(click)': 'open()', 'aria-haspopup': 'dialog' },
})
export class SpotlightTrigger {
  private readonly svc = inject(SpotlightService);
  readonly spotlightTrigger = input('');

  protected open(): void {
    this.svc.open(this.spotlightTrigger() || undefined);
  }
}
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the host**

`projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/spotlight-host.ts`:

````ts
import { FocusTrapFactory, type FocusTrap } from '@angular/cdk/a11y';
import { BreakpointObserver } from '@angular/cdk/layout';
import { Overlay, type OverlayRef } from '@angular/cdk/overlay';
import { TemplatePortal } from '@angular/cdk/portal';
import { DOCUMENT, NgComponentOutlet, NgTemplateOutlet } from '@angular/common';
import {
  afterNextRender,
  afterRenderEffect,
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  computed,
  contentChild,
  contentChildren,
  DestroyRef,
  effect,
  inject,
  Injector,
  TemplateRef,
  viewChild,
  ViewContainerRef,
} from '@angular/core';
import { matchesHotkey } from './hotkeys';
import { resolveParts } from './parts/default-parts';
import { SpotlightDefaultTheme } from './parts/default-theme';
import { SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER, type SpotlightInputContext } from './renderer';
import { SpotlightService, type SpotlightOption } from './spotlight.service';
import {
  SpotlightEmptyTemplate,
  SpotlightFooterTemplate,
  SpotlightItemTemplate,
  type SpotlightItemTemplateContext,
} from './templates';

const PHONE_QUERY = '(max-width: 600px)';

/** The palette. Place it once in the app shell. */
@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-host',
  imports: [NgComponentOutlet, NgTemplateOutlet, SpotlightDefaultTheme],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  host: { '(document:keydown)': 'onDocumentKeydown($event)' },
  template: `
    @if (themeDefault) {
      <spotlight-theme-default />
    }
    <ng-template #panel>
      <div class="sp-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" [attr.aria-label]="texts.placeholder()">
        <div class="sp-input">
          <ng-container *ngComponentOutlet="parts.input; inputs: { ctx: inputCtx() }" />
        </div>
        @if (svc.chips().length > 1) {
          <div class="sp-chips">
            @for (chip of svc.chips(); track chip.id ?? '') {
              <button
                type="button"
                class="sp-chip-button"
                tabindex="-1"
                [attr.aria-pressed]="chip.id === svc.chip()"
                [attr.aria-label]="chip.label"
                (mousedown)="$event.preventDefault()"
                (click)="svc.setChip(chip.id)"
              >
                <ng-container
                  *ngComponentOutlet="parts.chips; inputs: { ctx: { label: chip.label, active: chip.id === svc.chip() } }"
                />
              </button>
            }
          </div>
        }
        <div class="sp-list" role="listbox" tabindex="-1" [id]="ids.listbox" [attr.aria-label]="texts.placeholder()">
          @for (group of svc.groups(); track group.key; let g = $index) {
            <div class="sp-group" role="group" [attr.aria-labelledby]="ids.prefix + '-g' + g">
              <div class="sp-group-header" [id]="ids.prefix + '-g' + g">
                <ng-container
                  *ngComponentOutlet="
                    parts.groupHeader;
                    inputs: { ctx: { label: group.label, total: group.total, loading: group.loading, loadingText: texts.loading() } }
                  "
                />
              </div>
              @for (opt of optionsByGroup().get(group.key) ?? []; track opt.key) {
                <div
                  class="sp-option"
                  role="option"
                  [id]="opt.domId"
                  [attr.aria-selected]="opt.domId === activeId()"
                  (mousedown)="$event.preventDefault()"
                  (mousemove)="svc.setActive(opt.key)"
                  (click)="choose(opt, $event)"
                >
                  @if (opt.item; as item) {
                    @let rowTemplate = templateFor(item.group);
                    @if (rowTemplate) {
                      <ng-container
                        *ngTemplateOutlet="rowTemplate; context: { $implicit: item, active: opt.domId === activeId(), query: svc.query() }"
                      />
                    } @else {
                      <ng-container
                        *ngComponentOutlet="
                          parts.row;
                          inputs: { ctx: { item: item, active: opt.domId === activeId(), query: svc.query(), badge: parts.badge } }
                        "
                      />
                    }
                  } @else {
                    <span class="sp-error">{{ texts.error() }}</span>
                    <span class="sp-retry">{{ texts.retry() }}</span>
                  }
                </div>
              }
            </div>
          }
        </div>
        @if (showEmpty()) {
          <div class="sp-empty">
            @if (emptyTemplate(); as empty) {
              <ng-container *ngTemplateOutlet="empty.template; context: { $implicit: svc.query() }" />
            } @else {
              {{ texts.noResults() }}
            }
          </div>
        }
        <div class="sp-footer">
          @if (footerTemplate(); as footer) {
            <ng-container *ngTemplateOutlet="footer.template" />
          } @else {
            <span><kbd>↑</kbd><kbd>↓</kbd> {{ texts.hintNavigate() }}</span>
            <span><kbd>↵</kbd> {{ texts.hintOpen() }}</span>
            <span><kbd>esc</kbd> {{ texts.hintClose() }}</span>
          }
        </div>
        <div
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
          style="position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;margin:-1px;padding:0;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0 0 0 0);white-space:nowrap;border:0"
        >
          {{ svc.announcement() }}
        </div>
      </div>
    </ng-template>
  `,
})
export class SpotlightHost {
  protected readonly svc = inject(SpotlightService);
  private readonly overlay = inject(Overlay);
  private readonly focusTrapFactory = inject(FocusTrapFactory);
  private readonly breakpoints = inject(BreakpointObserver);
  private readonly viewContainerRef = inject(ViewContainerRef);
  private readonly injector = inject(Injector);
  private readonly document = inject(DOCUMENT);

  protected readonly parts = resolveParts(inject(SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER, { optional: true }));
  protected readonly themeDefault = this.svc.settings.theme === 'default';
  protected readonly ids = {
    prefix: this.svc.idPrefix,
    input: `${this.svc.idPrefix}-input`,
    listbox: `${this.svc.idPrefix}-listbox`,
  };

  private readonly panel = viewChild.required<TemplateRef<unknown>>('panel');
  private readonly itemTemplates = contentChildren(SpotlightItemTemplate);
  protected readonly emptyTemplate = contentChild(SpotlightEmptyTemplate);
  protected readonly footerTemplate = contentChild(SpotlightFooterTemplate);

  private overlayRef: OverlayRef | null = null;
  private focusTrap: FocusTrap | null = null;
  private restoreFocus: HTMLElement | null = null;

  protected readonly activeId = computed(() => this.svc.activeOption()?.domId ?? null);

  protected readonly optionsByGroup = computed(() => {
    const byGroup = new Map<string, SpotlightOption[]>();
    for (const option of this.svc.options()) {
      const list = byGroup.get(option.groupKey) ?? [];
      list.push(option);
      byGroup.set(option.groupKey, list);
    }
    return byGroup;
  });

  protected readonly showEmpty = computed(
    () => this.svc.query().trim() !== '' && this.svc.options().length === 0 && !this.svc.loading(),
  );

  private readonly handleInput = (value: string): void => this.svc.setQuery(value);
  private readonly handleKeydown = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => this.onInputKeydown(event);

  protected readonly inputCtx = computed<SpotlightInputContext>(() => ({
    id: this.ids.input,
    value: this.svc.query(),
    placeholder: this.texts.placeholder(),
    label: this.texts.placeholder(),
    expanded: this.svc.options().length > 0,
    controls: this.ids.listbox,
    activeDescendant: this.activeId(),
    onInput: this.handleInput,
    onKeydown: this.handleKeydown,
  }));

  constructor() {
    effect(() => {
      if (this.svc.isOpen()) this.attach();
      else this.detach();
    });
    afterRenderEffect(() => {
      const id = this.activeId();
      if (id === null || this.overlayRef === null) return;
      this.document.getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'nearest' });
    });
    inject(DestroyRef).onDestroy(() => {
      this.svc.close();
      this.detach();
    });
  }

  protected get texts() {
    return this.svc.texts;
  }

  protected templateFor(groupId: string): TemplateRef<SpotlightItemTemplateContext> | null {
    const templates = this.itemTemplates();
    const match =
      templates.find((t) => t.spotlightItem() === groupId) ?? templates.find((t) => t.spotlightItem() === '');
    return match?.template ?? null;
  }

  protected choose(option: SpotlightOption, event: MouseEvent): void {
    void this.svc.select(option, { newTab: event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey });
  }

  protected onDocumentKeydown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if (!this.svc.settings.hotkeys.some((hotkey) => matchesHotkey(event, hotkey))) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    if (event.repeat) return;
    this.svc.toggle();
  }

  private onInputKeydown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if (event.isComposing) return;
    switch (event.key) {
      case 'ArrowDown':
        event.preventDefault();
        this.svc.moveActive(1);
        break;
      case 'ArrowUp':
        event.preventDefault();
        this.svc.moveActive(-1);
        break;
      case 'Enter': {
        event.preventDefault();
        const option = this.svc.activeOption();
        if (option) void this.svc.select(option, { newTab: event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey });
        break;
      }
      case 'Tab':
        if (this.svc.chips().length > 1) {
          event.preventDefault();
          this.svc.cycleChip(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
        }
        break;
    }
  }

  private attach(): void {
    if (this.overlayRef) return;
    const active = this.document.activeElement;
    this.restoreFocus = active instanceof HTMLElement ? active : null;
    const sheet = this.breakpoints.isMatched(PHONE_QUERY);
    const theme = this.themeDefault ? ['sp-theme-default'] : [];
    const position = this.overlay.position().global();
    const ref = this.overlay.create({
      hasBackdrop: true,
      backdropClass: ['sp-backdrop', ...theme, ...(this.parts.backdropClass ? [this.parts.backdropClass] : [])],
      panelClass: ['sp-pane', ...theme, ...(sheet ? ['sp-sheet'] : []), ...(this.parts.panelClass ? [this.parts.panelClass] : [])],
      positionStrategy: sheet ? position.top('0').left('0') : position.centerHorizontally().top('12vh'),
      scrollStrategy: this.overlay.scrollStrategies.block(),
      width: sheet ? '100vw' : 'min(640px, calc(100vw - 32px))',
      height: sheet ? '100dvh' : undefined,
    });
    ref.attach(new TemplatePortal(this.panel(), this.viewContainerRef));
    ref.backdropClick().subscribe(() => this.svc.close());
    ref.keydownEvents().subscribe((event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        event.preventDefault();
        this.svc.close();
      }
    });
    this.focusTrap = this.focusTrapFactory.create(ref.overlayElement);
    this.overlayRef = ref;
    afterNextRender(() => this.overlayRef?.overlayElement.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[role="combobox"]')?.focus(), {
      injector: this.injector,
    });
  }

  private detach(): void {
    if (!this.overlayRef) return;
    this.focusTrap?.destroy();
    this.focusTrap = null;
    this.overlayRef.dispose();
    this.overlayRef = null;
    const target = this.restoreFocus;
    this.restoreFocus = null;
    if (target?.isConnected) target.focus();
  }
}
````

  - `SPOTLIGHT_PRIMENG_CSS`: maps `--sp-*` onto PrimeNG design tokens under `.sp-primeng` / `.sp-primeng-backdrop` (decision D-P1). Apps keep `theme: 'default'` for layout; the PrimeNG tokens supply the colours, so white-label presets apply.

````ts
import { Component, provideZonelessChangeDetection } from '@angular/core';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { provideRouter } from '@angular/router';
import { provideSpotlight, SpotlightHost, SpotlightService, staticSource } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import axe from 'axe-core';
import { providePrimeNG } from 'primeng/config';
import { memoryRecent } from '../../src/lib/testing.spec-util';
import { provideSpotlightPrimeNG, toneToSeverity } from './public-api';

@Component({ imports: [SpotlightHost], template: '<spotlight-host />' })
class Wrapper {}

const pages = staticSource({
  group: 'Pages',
  items: [
    { id: 'dash', title: 'Dashboard', suggested: true, icon: 'pi pi-home', action: { route: '/dashboard' } },
    { id: 'cust', title: 'Customers', icon: 'pi pi-users', badges: [{ label: 'new', tone: 'success' }], action: { route: '/customers' } },
  ],
});
const orders = staticSource({ group: 'Orders', items: [{ id: '1001', title: 'Order 1001', action: { route: '/orders/1001' } }] });

beforeAll(() => {
  // PrimeNG may consult matchMedia; jsdom does not implement it.
  if (!('matchMedia' in window)) {
    Object.defineProperty(window, 'matchMedia', {
      configurable: true,
      value: (query: string) => ({
        matches: false,
        media: query,
        onchange: null,
        addListener: () => undefined,
        removeListener: () => undefined,
        addEventListener: () => undefined,
        removeEventListener: () => undefined,
        dispatchEvent: () => false,
      }),
    });
  }
});

async function openWith(primeng: boolean, query = 'o') {
  TestBed.resetTestingModule();
  TestBed.configureTestingModule({
    providers: [
      provideZonelessChangeDetection(),
      provideRouter([]),
      provideSpotlight({ recent: { storage: memoryRecent() }, sources: [pages, orders] }),
      ...(primeng ? [provideSpotlightPrimeNG(), providePrimeNG({})] : []),
    ],
  });
  const fixture = TestBed.createComponent(Wrapper);
  await fixture.whenStable();
  const svc = TestBed.inject(SpotlightService);
  svc.open();
  await fixture.whenStable();
  svc.setQuery(query);
  await fixture.whenStable();
  return { fixture, svc };
}

/** Roles and ARIA attributes of every non-hidden element, ids normalised to first-seen order. */
function a11yTree(root: Element): string[] {
  const ids = new Map<string, string>();
  const norm = (value: string) =>
    value
      .split(/\s+/)
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((id) => {
        if (!ids.has(id)) ids.set(id, `#${ids.size}`);
        return ids.get(id)!;
      })
      .join(' ');
  const idRefs = new Set(['aria-controls', 'aria-activedescendant', 'aria-labelledby']);
  const out: string[] = [];
  const walk = (el: Element, depth: number) => {
    if (el.getAttribute('aria-hidden') === 'true') return;
    const role = el.getAttribute('role');
    const aria = Array.from(el.attributes)
      .filter((a) => a.name.startsWith('aria-'))
      .map((a) => `${a.name}=${idRefs.has(a.name) ? norm(a.value) : a.value}`)
      .sort();
    if (role || aria.length > 0) {
      const id = el.id ? norm(el.id) : '';
      out.push(`${'  '.repeat(depth)}${el.tagName.toLowerCase()}[${role ?? ''}]${id} ${aria.join(' ')}`.trimEnd());
    }
    for (const child of Array.from(el.children)) walk(child, role ? depth + 1 : depth);
  };
  walk(root, 0);
  return out;
}

describe('@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/primeng', () => {
  it('maps badge tones to p-tag severities', () => {
    expect(toneToSeverity('info')).toBe('info');
    expect(toneToSeverity('success')).toBe('success');
    expect(toneToSeverity('warn')).toBe('warn');
    expect(toneToSeverity('danger')).toBe('danger');
    expect(toneToSeverity(undefined)).toBe('secondary');
  });

  it('renders PrimeNG components inside the core overlay', async () => {
    const { fixture } = await openWith(true);
    expect(document.querySelector('.sp-pane')!.classList).toContain('sp-primeng');
    expect(document.querySelector('.cdk-overlay-backdrop')!.classList).toContain('sp-primeng-backdrop');
    expect(document.querySelector('p-iconfield input[pinputtext][role="combobox"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(document.querySelectorAll('p-chip')).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(document.querySelector('p-tag')!.textContent).toContain('new');
    expect(document.querySelector('[role="option"] i.pi-users')).not.toBeNull();
    fixture.destroy();
  });

  it('keeps the core keyboard behaviour through the PrimeNG input', async () => {
    const { fixture, svc } = await openWith(true);
    const input = document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('[role="combobox"]')!;
    input.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'ArrowDown', bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    await fixture.whenStable();
    expect(svc.activeIndex()).toBe(1);
    input.value = 'cust';
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
    await fixture.whenStable();
    expect(svc.query()).toBe('cust');
    fixture.destroy();
  });

  it('produces the same accessibility tree as the core renderer', async () => {
    const core = await openWith(false);
    const coreTree = a11yTree(document.querySelector('.cdk-overlay-container')!);
    core.fixture.destroy();
    const prime = await openWith(true);
    const primeTree = a11yTree(document.querySelector('.cdk-overlay-container')!);
    prime.fixture.destroy();
    expect(coreTree.length).toBeGreaterThan(5);
    expect(primeTree).toEqual(coreTree);
  });

  it('has no axe violations with the open PrimeNG overlay', async () => {
    const { fixture } = await openWith(true);
    const results = await axe.run(
      { exclude: ['.cdk-focus-trap-anchor'] },
      { runOnly: { type: 'tag', values: ['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21a', 'wcag21aa', 'wcag22aa'] } },
    );
    expect(results.violations.map((v) => `${v.id}: ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(' ')).join(', ')}`)).toEqual([]);
    fixture.destroy();
  });
});
````

````ts
import { NgComponentOutlet } from '@angular/common';
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, input, ViewEncapsulation } from '@angular/core';
import type {
  SpotlightBadgeContext,
  SpotlightBadgeTone,
  SpotlightChipContext,
  SpotlightGroupHeaderContext,
  SpotlightInputContext,
  SpotlightRowContext,
} from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { Chip } from 'primeng/chip';
import { IconField } from 'primeng/iconfield';
import { InputIcon } from 'primeng/inputicon';
import { InputText } from 'primeng/inputtext';
import { Tag } from 'primeng/tag';

export type SpotlightPrimeTagSeverity = 'success' | 'secondary' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'danger' | 'contrast';

export function toneToSeverity(tone: SpotlightBadgeTone | undefined): SpotlightPrimeTagSeverity {
  switch (tone) {
    case 'info':
      return 'info';
    case 'success':
      return 'success';
    case 'warn':
      return 'warn';
    case 'danger':
      return 'danger';
    default:
      return 'secondary';
  }
}

/** Maps the core's --sp-* variables to PrimeNG design tokens, so presets and white-label themes apply. */
export const SPOTLIGHT_PRIMENG_CSS = `
.sp-primeng {
  --sp-bg: var(--p-content-background, #ffffff);
  --sp-fg: var(--p-text-color, #334155);
  --sp-muted: var(--p-text-muted-color, #64748b);
  --sp-accent: var(--p-primary-color, #10b981);
  --sp-accent-fg: var(--p-primary-contrast-color, #ffffff);
  --sp-active-bg: var(--p-content-hover-background, #f1f5f9);
  --sp-border: var(--p-content-border-color, #e2e8f0);
  --sp-radius: var(--p-content-border-radius, 6px);
  --sp-shadow: var(--p-overlay-modal-shadow, 0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1));
  --sp-font: inherit;
}
.sp-primeng-backdrop { --sp-backdrop: var(--p-mask-background, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4)); background: var(--sp-backdrop); }
.sp-primeng .sp-input { padding: 12px; }
.sp-primeng .sp-primeng-field { display: block; flex: 1; }
.sp-primeng .sp-primeng-field input { width: 100%; box-sizing: border-box; }
.sp-primeng .sp-chip-button .p-chip.sp-primeng-chip-active { background: var(--p-primary-color); color: var(--p-primary-contrast-color); }
`;

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-primeng-input',
  imports: [IconField, InputIcon, InputText],
  encapsulation: ViewEncapsulation.None,
  styles: [SPOTLIGHT_PRIMENG_CSS],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <p-iconfield class="sp-primeng-field">
      <p-inputicon><i class="pi pi-search" aria-hidden="true"></i></p-inputicon>
      <input
        pInputText
        type="text"
        role="combobox"
        autocomplete="off"
        spellcheck="false"
        aria-autocomplete="list"
        [id]="ctx().id"
        [value]="ctx().value"
        [attr.placeholder]="ctx().placeholder"
        [attr.aria-label]="ctx().label"
        [attr.aria-expanded]="ctx().expanded"
        [attr.aria-controls]="ctx().controls"
        [attr.aria-activedescendant]="ctx().activeDescendant"
        (input)="onInput($event)"
        (keydown)="ctx().onKeydown($event)"
      />
    </p-iconfield>
  `,
})
export class SpotlightPrimeNgInput {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightInputContext>();

  protected onInput(event: Event): void {
    this.ctx().onInput((event.target as HTMLInputElement).value);
  }
}

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-primeng-chip',
  imports: [Chip],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `<span aria-hidden="true"><p-chip [label]="ctx().label" [styleClass]="ctx().active ? 'sp-primeng-chip-active' : ''" /></span>`,
})
export class SpotlightPrimeNgChip {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightChipContext>();
}

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-primeng-group-header',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <span class="sp-group-label">{{ ctx().label }}</span>
    @if (ctx().total !== undefined) {
      <span class="sp-group-total">{{ ctx().total }}</span>
    }
    @if (ctx().loading) {
      <i class="pi pi-spin pi-spinner" aria-hidden="true"></i>
      <span
        style="position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;margin:-1px;padding:0;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0 0 0 0);white-space:nowrap;border:0"
        >{{ ctx().loadingText }}</span
      >
    }
  `,
})
export class SpotlightPrimeNgGroupHeader {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightGroupHeaderContext>();
}

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-primeng-badge',
  imports: [Tag],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `<p-tag [value]="ctx().badge.label" [severity]="severity()" />`,
})
export class SpotlightPrimeNgBadge {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightBadgeContext>();
  protected readonly severity = computed(() => toneToSeverity(this.ctx().badge.tone));
}

@Component({
  selector: 'spotlight-primeng-row',
  imports: [NgComponentOutlet],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    @let item = ctx().item;
    @if (item.icon) {
      <span class="sp-icon" aria-hidden="true"><i [class]="item.icon"></i></span>
    }
    <span class="sp-text">
      <span class="sp-title">{{ item.title }}</span>
      @if (item.subtitle) {
        <span class="sp-subtitle">{{ item.subtitle }}</span>
      }
    </span>
    @if (item.badges?.length) {
      <span class="sp-badges">
        @for (badge of item.badges ?? []; track $index) {
          <ng-container *ngComponentOutlet="ctx().badge; inputs: { ctx: { badge: badge } }" />
        }
      </span>
    }
  `,
})
export class SpotlightPrimeNgRow {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightRowContext>();
}
````

`projects/ng-spotlight/primeng/src/provide-spotlight-primeng.ts`:

````ts
import { makeEnvironmentProviders, type EnvironmentProviders } from '@angular/core';
import { SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER, type SpotlightRenderer } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import {
  SpotlightPrimeNgBadge,
  SpotlightPrimeNgChip,
  SpotlightPrimeNgGroupHeader,
  SpotlightPrimeNgInput,
  SpotlightPrimeNgRow,
} from './primeng-parts';

export const SPOTLIGHT_PRIMENG_RENDERER: SpotlightRenderer = {
  dialog: { panelClass: 'sp-primeng', backdropClass: 'sp-primeng-backdrop' },
  input: SpotlightPrimeNgInput,
  chips: SpotlightPrimeNgChip,
  groupHeader: SpotlightPrimeNgGroupHeader,
  row: SpotlightPrimeNgRow,
  badge: SpotlightPrimeNgBadge,
};

/** Renders the palette with PrimeNG components. Use next to provideSpotlight() and providePrimeNG(). */
export function provideSpotlightPrimeNG(): EnvironmentProviders {
  return makeEnvironmentProviders([{ provide: SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER, useValue: SPOTLIGHT_PRIMENG_RENDERER }]);
}
````

- Create: `projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/index.ts`, `getting-started.example.ts`, `route-source.example.ts`
- Create: `projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/content/getting-started.content.ts`, `route-source.content.ts`, `api.content.ts`
- Test: `projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/kitchen-sink.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the whole public API of `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight` and `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/primeng` (Tasks 2–10).
- Produces:
  - `interface PageSection { id: string; title: string; markdown: string }`, `interface PageExample { key: string; title: string }`, `interface PageContent { title: string; sections: PageSection[]; examples: PageExample[] }`
  - `interface ExampleDef { component: Type<unknown>; providers: Array<Provider | EnvironmentProviders> }`, `EXAMPLES: Record<string, ExampleDef>` (key = file name without `.example.ts`)
  - `SOURCES: Record<string, string>` in the generated, gitignored `examples/sources.generated.ts`
  - `ExampleCard` (`app-example-card`, inputs `key`, `title`): renders the example in its own `EnvironmentInjector` (so every example's `provideSpotlight({ isolated: true, hotkeys: [] })` is independent) and reveals its Prism-highlighted source
  - `Page` (`app-page`, input `content: PageContent`, bound from route `data.content` via `withComponentInputBinding()`), `Markdown` (`app-markdown`, input `source`), `ThemeToggle` (`app-theme-toggle`; toggles `sp-dark`/`sp-light` on `<html>`)
  - `routes: Routes` (every page route has a string `title`), `NAV_PAGES: NavPage[]` (`{ path: string; title: string }`)
  - npm scripts `check:docs`, and `prestart`/`prebuild:demo`/`pretest`/`pretest:coverage`/`pretest:demo` hooks that run the source generator
  - Convention for every example file `<key>.example.ts`: export one component and `<camelKey>Providers`, a provider array that starts with `provideSpotlight({ isolated: true, hotkeys: [], … })` (only the site's own palette owns Ctrl+Space / ⌘K).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the docs tooling scripts and hooks**

`scripts/generate-example-sources.mjs`:

````js
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Reads every projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/<key>.example.ts and writes
 * sources.generated.ts (key -> file text) for the example cards' "Show source" pane.
 * The output is gitignored and regenerated by the prestart, prebuild:demo and pretest hooks.
 */
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const root = new URL('..', import.meta.url).pathname;
const examplesDir = join(root, 'projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples');
const outputPath = join(examplesDir, 'sources.generated.ts');

if (!existsSync(examplesDir)) {
  console.warn(`[generate-example-sources] ${examplesDir} not found; nothing to generate.`);
  process.exit(0);
}

/** @type {Record<string, string>} */
const sources = {};
for (const file of readdirSync(examplesDir).sort()) {
  if (!file.endsWith('.example.ts')) continue;
  sources[file.slice(0, -'.example.ts'.length)] = readFileSync(join(examplesDir, file), 'utf8');
}

writeFileSync(
  outputPath,
  '/* AUTO-GENERATED by scripts/generate-example-sources.mjs. DO NOT EDIT BY HAND. */\n' +
    `export const SOURCES: Record<string, string> = ${JSON.stringify(sources, null, 2)};\n`,
);
console.log(`[generate-example-sources] wrote ${Object.keys(sources).length} example source(s)`);
````

`scripts/check-docs.mjs`:

````js
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Drift guard for the kitchen sink: every export of both entry points
 * (projects/ng-spotlight/src/public-api.ts and projects/ng-spotlight/primeng/src/public-api.ts)
 * must be mentioned in at least one projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/content/*.content.ts file.
 */
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const root = new URL('..', import.meta.url).pathname;
const entryPoints = ['projects/ng-spotlight/src/public-api.ts', 'projects/ng-spotlight/primeng/src/public-api.ts'];
const contentDir = join(root, 'projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/content');

function extractExports(source) {
  const names = new Set();
  const stripped = source.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '').replace(/(^|[^:])\/\/.*$/gm, '$1');
  const braceRe = /export\s+(?:type\s+)?\{([^}]+)\}/g;
  let match;
  while ((match = braceRe.exec(stripped)) !== null) {
    for (const raw of match[1].split(',')) {
      let name = raw.trim();
      if (!name) continue;
      if (name.startsWith('type ')) name = name.slice(5).trim();
      const asIndex = name.indexOf(' as ');
      if (asIndex > 0) name = name.slice(asIndex + 4).trim();
      if (name) names.add(name);
    }
  }
  const declRe = /export\s+(?:declare\s+)?(?:abstract\s+)?(?:class|function|const|let|var|interface|type|enum)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)/g;
  while ((match = declRe.exec(stripped)) !== null) names.add(match[1]);
  return [...names];
}

if (!existsSync(contentDir)) {
  console.error(`[check-docs] FAIL: ${contentDir} does not exist`);
  process.exit(1);
}
const contents = readdirSync(contentDir)
  .filter((f) => f.endsWith('.content.ts'))
  .map((f) => readFileSync(join(contentDir, f), 'utf8'));
if (contents.length === 0) {
  console.error('[check-docs] FAIL: no *.content.ts files');
  process.exit(1);
}

let failures = 0;
let checked = 0;
for (const entry of entryPoints) {
  const path = join(root, entry);
  if (!existsSync(path)) {
    console.error(`[check-docs] FAIL: ${entry} not found`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  for (const name of extractExports(readFileSync(path, 'utf8'))) {
    checked++;
    if (contents.some((c) => c.includes(name))) {
      console.log(`  PASS  ${name}`);
    } else {
      console.log(`  FAIL  ${name} (${entry}) is not mentioned in any content/*.content.ts`);
      failures++;
    }
  }
}
console.log(`\n[check-docs] ${checked - failures} passed, ${failures} failed`);
process.exit(failures === 0 ? 0 : 1);
````

````ts
import { provideHttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';

import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';

const pageExamples = routes.flatMap((r) => (r.data?.['content'] as PageContent | undefined)?.examples.map((e) => e.key) ?? []);

describe('kitchen sink', () => {
  it('labels every route (dogfoods spotlightRouteAudit)', () => {
    expect(spotlightRouteAudit(routes)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('references only existing examples, each with its generated source, and uses every example', () => {
    expect(pageExamples.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const key of pageExamples) {
      expect(EXAMPLES[key], key).toBeDefined();
      expect(SOURCES[key], key).toContain('provideSpotlight');
    }
    expect(new Set(pageExamples)).toEqual(new Set(Object.keys(EXAMPLES)));
  });

  it.each(Object.keys(EXAMPLES))('renders example "%s" with its own palette', async (key) => {
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      providers: [
        provideZonelessChangeDetection(),
        provideRouter(routes),
        provideHttpClient(),
        providePrimeNG({}),
        provideSpotlight({ hotkeys: [], recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('kitchen-sink-test') } }),
      ],
    });
    const fixture = TestBed.createComponent(ExampleCard);
    fixture.componentRef.setInput('key', key);
    fixture.componentRef.setInput('title', key);
    await fixture.whenStable();
    const host = fixture.debugElement.query(By.directive(SpotlightHost));
    expect(host, `${key} renders <spotlight-host>`).not.toBeNull();
    const svc = host.injector.get(SpotlightService);
    expect(svc).not.toBe(TestBed.inject(SpotlightService));
    svc.open();
    await fixture.whenStable();
    expect(document.querySelector('[role="combobox"]')).not.toBeNull();
    svc.close();
    await fixture.whenStable();
    fixture.destroy();
  });
});
````

````ts
/** One documentation page, rendered by Page from a route's data.content. */
export interface PageContent {
  title: string;
  sections: PageSection[];
  examples: PageExample[];
}

export interface PageSection {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  markdown: string;
}

export interface PageExample {
  /** Key into EXAMPLES and SOURCES: the example file name without `.example.ts`. */
  key: string;
  title: string;
}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/shared/markdown.ts`:

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, inject, input } from '@angular/core';
import { DomSanitizer, type SafeHtml } from '@angular/platform-browser';
import { marked } from 'marked';
import Prism from 'prismjs';
import 'prismjs/components/prism-typescript';
import 'prismjs/components/prism-json';
import 'prismjs/components/prism-bash';

/**
 * Renders hand-written markdown from content/*.content.ts with Prism-highlighted code fences.
 * The content is authored in this repo, so it is trusted (no DOMPurify), like lingui-angular.
 */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-markdown',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `<div class="prose" [innerHTML]="html()"></div>`,
})
export class Markdown {
  readonly source = input.required<string>();
  private readonly sanitizer = inject(DomSanitizer);

  protected readonly html = computed<SafeHtml>(() => {
    const raw = marked.parse(this.source(), { async: false, gfm: true, breaks: false });
    return this.sanitizer.bypassSecurityTrustHtml(highlight(raw));
  });
}

function highlight(html: string): string {
  return html.replace(/<code class="language-([a-z0-9-]+)">([\s\S]*?)<\/code>/g, (_match, lang: string, code: string) => {
    const grammar = Prism.languages[lang];
    if (!grammar) return `<code class="language-${lang}">${code}</code>`;
    const decoded = code
      .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
      .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
      .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
      .replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
      .replace(/&amp;/g, '&');
    return `<code class="language-${lang}">${Prism.highlight(decoded, grammar, lang)}</code>`;
  });
}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/shared/example-card.ts`:

````ts
import { NgComponentOutlet } from '@angular/common';
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  computed,
  createEnvironmentInjector,
  DestroyRef,
  EnvironmentInjector,
  inject,
  input,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import Prism from 'prismjs';
import 'prismjs/components/prism-typescript';
import { EXAMPLES } from '../examples';
import { SOURCES } from '../examples/sources.generated';

/** A live example plus its own source, read from the real example file at build time. */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-example-card',
  imports: [NgComponentOutlet],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <section class="example" [id]="key()">
      <header class="example-header">
        <h3>{{ title() }}</h3>
        <button type="button" class="link-button" [attr.aria-expanded]="showSource()" (click)="showSource.set(!showSource())">
          {{ showSource() ? 'Hide source' : 'Show source' }}
        </button>
      </header>
      <div class="example-demo">
        <ng-container *ngComponentOutlet="example().component; environmentInjector: environment()" />
      </div>
      @if (showSource()) {
        <pre class="example-source"><code class="language-typescript" [innerHTML]="highlighted()"></code></pre>
      }
    </section>
  `,
})
export class ExampleCard {
  readonly key = input.required<string>();
  readonly title = input.required<string>();

  private readonly parent = inject(EnvironmentInjector);
  private readonly created: EnvironmentInjector[] = [];
  protected readonly showSource = signal(false);

  protected readonly example = computed(() => {
    const example = EXAMPLES[this.key()];
    if (!example) throw new Error(`[kitchen-sink] unknown example "${this.key()}"`);
    return example;
  });

  /** Each example gets its own environment injector, so its provideSpotlight() is independent. */
  protected readonly environment = computed(() => {
    const env = createEnvironmentInjector(this.example().providers, this.parent, `example:${this.key()}`);
    this.created.push(env);
    return env;
  });

  protected readonly highlighted = computed(() =>
    Prism.highlight(SOURCES[this.key()] ?? '', Prism.languages['typescript'], 'typescript'),
  );

  constructor() {
    inject(DestroyRef).onDestroy(() => this.created.forEach((env) => env.destroy()));
  }
}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/shared/page.ts`:

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, input } from '@angular/core';
import { ExampleCard } from './example-card';
import { Markdown } from './markdown';
import type { PageContent } from './page-content.types';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-page',
  imports: [ExampleCard, Markdown],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <article>
      <h1>{{ content().title }}</h1>
      @for (section of content().sections; track section.id) {
        <section [id]="section.id">
          <h2>{{ section.title }}</h2>
          <app-markdown [source]="section.markdown" />
        </section>
      }
      @if (content().examples.length > 0) {
        <h2 id="examples">Examples</h2>
        @for (example of content().examples; track example.key) {
          <app-example-card [key]="example.key" [title]="example.title" />
        }
      }
    </article>
  `,
})
export class Page {
  readonly content = input.required<PageContent>();
}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/shared/theme-toggle.ts`:

````ts
import { DOCUMENT } from '@angular/common';
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-theme-toggle',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <button
      type="button"
      class="icon-button"
      [attr.aria-label]="dark() ? 'Switch to light theme' : 'Switch to dark theme'"
      (click)="toggle()"
    >
      <i [class]="dark() ? 'pi pi-sun' : 'pi pi-moon'" aria-hidden="true"></i>
    </button>
  `,
})
export class ThemeToggle {
  private readonly root = inject(DOCUMENT).documentElement;
  protected readonly dark = signal(this.root.classList.contains('sp-dark'));

  protected toggle(): void {
    const dark = !this.dark();
    this.dark.set(dark);
    this.root.classList.toggle('sp-dark', dark);
    this.root.classList.toggle('sp-light', !dark);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('kitchen-sink:theme', dark ? 'dark' : 'light');
    } catch {
      // Storage blocked: the choice lasts until reload.
    }
  }
}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/getting-started.example.ts`:

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { localStorageRecent, provideSpotlight, routeSource, SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger, staticSource } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';

const greetings = signal(0);

// In an app this is the whole setup: provideSpotlight() in app.config.ts and <spotlight-host /> in the shell.
// `isolated` and `hotkeys: []` are only here because the kitchen sink runs several palettes side by side
// and the site's own palette already owns Ctrl+Space / ⌘K.
export const gettingStartedProviders = [
  provideSpotlight({
    isolated: true,
    hotkeys: [],
    recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('example-getting-started') },
    sources: [
      routeSource({ group: 'Pages' }),
      staticSource({
        group: 'Commands',
        items: [
          { id: 'hello', title: 'Say hello', icon: 'pi pi-comment', suggested: true, action: { run: () => greetings.update((n) => n + 1) } },
        ],
      }),
    ],
  }),
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-getting-started-example',
  imports: [SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger><i class="pi pi-search" aria-hidden="true"></i> Open the palette</button>
    <p>"Say hello" has run {{ greetings() }} time(s). Every kitchen-sink page is listed under Pages.</p>
    <spotlight-host />
  `,
})
export class GettingStartedExample {
  protected readonly greetings = greetings;
}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/route-source.example.ts`:

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component } from '@angular/core';
import type { Routes } from '@angular/router';
import {
  localStorageRecent,
  provideSpotlight,
  routeSource,
  SpotlightHost,
  spotlightRouteAudit,
  SpotlightTrigger,
} from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';

@Component({ template: '' })
class DemoPage {}

/** A route config like an app's, to show what the audit reports. */
const DEMO_ROUTES: Routes = [
  { path: 'customers', component: DemoPage, title: 'Customers', data: { spotlight: { keywords: ['clients', 'accounts'] } } },
  { path: 'customers/:id', component: DemoPage, title: 'Customer' }, // skipped: has a :param segment
  { path: 'reports', component: DemoPage, data: { breadcrumb: 'Reports' } }, // label from data.breadcrumb
  { path: 'internal', component: DemoPage, data: { spotlight: false } }, // opted out
  { path: 'settings', component: DemoPage }, // no label: the audit reports it
];

export const routeSourceProviders = [
  provideSpotlight({
    isolated: true,
    hotkeys: [],
    recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('example-route-source') },
    // The kitchen sink's own routes; the filter hides one page, like an app hiding pages by role.
    sources: [routeSource({ group: 'Kitchen sink pages', filter: (_route, url) => url !== '/api' })],
  }),
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-route-source-example',
  imports: [SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger>Search the kitchen-sink routes</button>
    <p><code>spotlightRouteAudit(DEMO_ROUTES)</code> returns:</p>
    <pre>{{ audit }}</pre>
    <spotlight-host />
  `,
})
export class RouteSourceExample {
  protected readonly audit = JSON.stringify(spotlightRouteAudit(DEMO_ROUTES), null, 2);
}
````

````ts
import type { EnvironmentProviders, Provider, Type } from '@angular/core';
import { GettingStartedExample, gettingStartedProviders } from './getting-started.example';
import { RouteSourceExample, routeSourceProviders } from './route-source.example';

export interface ExampleDef {
  component: Type<unknown>;
  providers: Array<Provider | EnvironmentProviders>;
}

/** Key = example file name without `.example.ts` (also the key into SOURCES). */
export const EXAMPLES: Record<string, ExampleDef> = {
  'getting-started': { component: GettingStartedExample, providers: gettingStartedProviders },
  'route-source': { component: RouteSourceExample, providers: routeSourceProviders },
};
````

````ts
import type { PageContent } from '../shared/page-content.types';

export const GETTING_STARTED_CONTENT: PageContent = {
  title: 'Getting started',
  sections: [
    {
      id: 'install',
      title: 'Install',
      markdown: `
\`\`\`bash
npm i @tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight
\`\`\`

Peer dependencies: \`@angular/core\`, \`@angular/common\`, \`@angular/router\`, \`@angular/cdk\` (21.x) and \`rxjs\`.
Versions follow Angular majors: \`21.x\` supports Angular 21. \`VERSION\` exports the installed version.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'setup',
      title: 'Three-line setup',
      markdown: `
\`\`\`ts
// app.config.ts
providers: [provideRouter(routes), provideSpotlight({ sources: [routeSource()] })]
\`\`\`

\`\`\`html
<!-- app shell, once -->
<spotlight-host />
<button spotlightTrigger>Search</button>
\`\`\`

Press **Ctrl+Space** or **⌘K / Ctrl+K**. Every labelled route is searchable. Try it on this site: the
palette in the top bar is configured exactly like this, plus a few links.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'service',
      title: 'Open it from code',
      markdown: `
\`SpotlightService\` exposes \`open(query?)\`, \`close()\`, \`isOpen\` (a signal) and \`selected\` (an Observable of the
chosen \`SpotlightItem\`). \`SpotlightTrigger\` (\`spotlightTrigger\`, optionally \`spotlightTrigger="#"\`) opens it on click.

Call \`provideSpotlight()\` again in a lazy route's \`providers\` to add sources there; they disappear when that
injector is destroyed. Use \`provideSpotlight(() => ({ … }))\` when the config needs \`inject()\`.
      `,
    },
  ],
  examples: [{ key: 'getting-started', title: 'A palette with route and static sources' }],
};
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/content/route-source.content.ts`:

````ts
import type { PageContent } from '../shared/page-content.types';

export const ROUTE_SOURCE_CONTENT: PageContent = {
  title: 'Route source',
  sections: [
    {
      id: 'labels',
      title: 'Labels',
      markdown: `
\`routeSource(options?)\` walks the Router config, including lazy children once they are loaded.
The label comes from the route \`title\` (string), then \`data.spotlight.label\`, then \`data.breadcrumb\`.
Routes with a \`:param\` segment are skipped. Each item's subtitle is its URL.

\`\`\`ts
{ path: 'customers', title: 'Customers', data: { spotlight: { keywords: ['clients'], suggested: true } } }
{ path: 'internal', component: Internal, data: { spotlight: false } }   // opt out
\`\`\`

\`SpotlightRouteData\` types \`data.spotlight\`. \`RouteSourceOptions\`: \`id\`, \`group\` (default "Pages"),
\`prefix\`, \`priority\` and \`filter(route, url)\` for the app's own access rules.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'audit',
      title: 'Route audit',
      markdown: `
\`spotlightRouteAudit(routes, options?)\` returns \`SpotlightRouteAuditEntry[]\` (\`{ path, reason }\`) for static pages
without a label that are not opted out. It is synchronous, so a unit test can fail on unlabelled routes:

\`\`\`ts
it('labels every admin route', () => {
  expect(spotlightRouteAudit(adminRoutes)).toEqual([]);
});
\`\`\`

\`SpotlightRouteAuditOptions.ignore(route, url)\` skips routes. \`loadChildren\` is not followed: pass lazy route
arrays to the audit directly. This kitchen sink runs the audit on its own routes in CI.
      `,
    },
  ],
  examples: [{ key: 'route-source', title: 'Kitchen-sink routes and an audit report' }],
};
````

````ts
import type { PageContent } from '../shared/page-content.types';

/** Every public export, so scripts/check-docs.mjs can enforce that the docs keep up. */
export const API_CONTENT: PageContent = {
  title: 'API reference',
  sections: [
    {
      id: 'setup',
      title: 'Setup',
      markdown: `
| Export | What it is |
|---|---|
| \`provideSpotlight(config \\| () => config)\` | Environment providers. \`SpotlightConfig\`: \`hotkeys\`, \`texts\`, \`recent\`, \`http\`, \`theme\`, \`onError\`, \`sources\`, \`isolated\` |
| \`SpotlightService\` | \`open(query?)\`, \`close()\`, \`isOpen\`, \`selected\`, plus the state used by renderers (\`groups\`, \`options\`, \`chips\`, …) |
| \`SpotlightHost\` | \`<spotlight-host>\`, place once in the shell |
| \`SpotlightTrigger\` | \`spotlightTrigger\` directive: opens the palette on click |
| \`SpotlightItemTemplate\`, \`SpotlightItemTemplateContext\` | \`<ng-template spotlightItem="group" let-item>\` row templates |
| \`SpotlightEmptyTemplate\`, \`SpotlightEmptyTemplateContext\` | \`<ng-template spotlightEmpty let-query>\` |
| \`SpotlightFooterTemplate\` | \`<ng-template spotlightFooter>\` |
| \`VERSION\` | The library version |
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'data',
      title: 'Data types',
      markdown: `
| Export | What it is |
|---|---|
| \`SpotlightItem\`, \`SpotlightItemInput\` | A result; the input form lets \`group\` default to the source's group |
| \`SpotlightAction\`, \`SpotlightStoredAction\` | \`{ route }\`, \`{ url, newTab? }\` or \`{ run }\`; the stored form drops \`run\` |
| \`SpotlightBadge\`, \`SpotlightBadgeTone\` | \`{ label, tone?: 'info' \\| 'success' \\| 'warn' \\| 'danger' }\` |
| \`SpotlightSource\`, \`SpotlightContext\` | A source and the context its \`search(query, ctx)\` receives (\`signal\`, \`chip\`, \`injector\`) |
| \`SpotlightResult\`, \`SpotlightResponse\`, \`SpotlightGroup\` | \`SpotlightItem[]\` or \`{ groups: [{ id, label, total?, items }] }\` |
| \`SpotlightGroupState\`, \`SpotlightOption\`, \`SpotlightChip\` | Rendered state: groups with loading/error, selectable rows, chips |
| \`SpotlightConfig\` | The \`provideSpotlight\` options |
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'sources',
      title: 'Sources',
      markdown: `
| Export | What it is |
|---|---|
| \`routeSource\`, \`RouteSourceOptions\`, \`SpotlightRouteData\` | Labelled routes |
| \`staticSource\`, \`StaticSourceOptions\` | Fixed commands |
| \`httpSource\`, \`HttpSourceOptions\`, \`SpotlightHttpOptions\`, \`SpotlightHttpRequest\` | Remote results with \`request\`/\`map\` overrides |
| \`spotlightRouteAudit\`, \`SpotlightRouteAuditEntry\`, \`SpotlightRouteAuditOptions\` | Finds unlabelled routes |
| \`schema/spotlight-response.schema.json\` | JSON Schema of the HTTP response format |
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'texts-recent',
      title: 'Texts and Recent',
      markdown: `
| Export | What it is |
|---|---|
| \`SpotlightTexts\`, \`SpotlightTextValue\`, \`DEFAULT_SPOTLIGHT_TEXTS\` | Every UI string; a value is a string, Signal or Observable |
| \`SpotlightRecentStorage\`, \`SpotlightRecentEntry\`, \`localStorageRecent(key)\` | Where Recent items live |
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'rendering',
      title: 'Rendering',
      markdown: `
| Export | What it is |
|---|---|
| \`SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER\`, \`SpotlightRenderer\` | Token and shape of a renderer adapter: \`dialog\`, \`input\`, \`chips\`, \`groupHeader\`, \`row\`, \`badge\` |
| \`SpotlightInputContext\`, \`SpotlightChipContext\`, \`SpotlightGroupHeaderContext\`, \`SpotlightRowContext\`, \`SpotlightBadgeContext\` | The \`ctx\` input of each part |
| \`SpotlightDefaultInput\`, \`SpotlightDefaultChip\`, \`SpotlightDefaultGroupHeader\`, \`SpotlightDefaultRow\`, \`SpotlightDefaultBadge\` | The core parts, reusable in your own adapter |
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'primeng',
      title: '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/primeng',
      markdown: `
| Export | What it is |
|---|---|
| \`provideSpotlightPrimeNG()\`, \`SPOTLIGHT_PRIMENG_RENDERER\` | Renders the palette with PrimeNG components |
| \`SpotlightPrimeNgInput\`, \`SpotlightPrimeNgChip\`, \`SpotlightPrimeNgGroupHeader\`, \`SpotlightPrimeNgRow\`, \`SpotlightPrimeNgBadge\` | The PrimeNG parts |
| \`toneToSeverity\`, \`SpotlightPrimeTagSeverity\` | Badge tone → \`p-tag\` severity |
| \`SPOTLIGHT_PRIMENG_CSS\` | The \`--sp-*\` → PrimeNG token mapping |
      `,
    },
  ],
  examples: [],
};
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/app.routes.ts`:

````ts
import type { Routes } from '@angular/router';
import { API_CONTENT } from './content/api.content';
import { GETTING_STARTED_CONTENT } from './content/getting-started.content';
import { ROUTE_SOURCE_CONTENT } from './content/route-source.content';

const page = () => import('./shared/page').then((m) => m.Page);

export const routes: Routes = [
  { path: '', pathMatch: 'full', redirectTo: 'getting-started' },
  {
    path: 'getting-started',
    title: 'Getting started',
    loadComponent: page,
    data: { content: GETTING_STARTED_CONTENT, spotlight: { keywords: ['install', 'setup', 'provideSpotlight'], suggested: true } },
  },
  {
    path: 'route-source',
    title: 'Route source',
    loadComponent: page,
    data: { content: ROUTE_SOURCE_CONTENT, spotlight: { keywords: ['routeSource', 'spotlightRouteAudit', 'audit'] } },
  },
  {
    path: 'api',
    title: 'API reference',
    loadComponent: page,
    data: { content: API_CONTENT, spotlight: { keywords: ['exports', 'reference', 'types'] } },
  },
  { path: '**', redirectTo: 'getting-started' },
];

export interface NavPage {
  path: string;
  title: string;
}

export const NAV_PAGES: NavPage[] = routes.flatMap((r) =>
  typeof r.path === 'string' && typeof r.title === 'string' ? [{ path: r.path, title: r.title }] : [],
);
````

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component } from '@angular/core';
import { RouterLink, RouterLinkActive, RouterOutlet } from '@angular/router';
import { SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { NAV_PAGES } from './app.routes';
import { ThemeToggle } from './shared/theme-toggle';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-root',
  imports: [RouterLink, RouterLinkActive, RouterOutlet, SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger, ThemeToggle],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
````

````ts
import { provideHttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { provideZonelessChangeDetection } from '@angular/core';
import { bootstrapApplication } from '@angular/platform-browser';
import { provideRouter, withComponentInputBinding } from '@angular/router';
import Aura from '@primeuix/themes/aura';
import { localStorageRecent, provideSpotlight, routeSource, staticSource } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { providePrimeNG } from 'primeng/config';
import { App } from './app/app';
import { routes } from './app/app.routes';

bootstrapApplication(App, {
  providers: [
    provideZonelessChangeDetection(),
    provideRouter(routes, withComponentInputBinding()),
    provideHttpClient(),
    providePrimeNG({ theme: { preset: Aura, options: { darkModeSelector: '.sp-dark' } } }),
    // The site's own palette (Ctrl+Space / ⌘K): every documented page, plus links.
    provideSpotlight({
      recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('kitchen-sink') },
      sources: [
        routeSource({ group: 'Pages' }),
        staticSource({
          group: 'Links',
          items: [
            {
              id: 'github',
              title: 'GitHub repository',
              icon: 'pi pi-github',

              action: { url: 'https://www.npmjs.com/package/@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight', newTab: true },
            },
          ],
        }),
      ],
    }),
  ],
}).catch((err: unknown) => console.error(err));
````

````css
.content { max-width: 960px; padding: 24px; }
@media (max-width: 760px) {
  .layout { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); }
  .sidenav { flex-direction: row; flex-wrap: wrap; border-right: 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ks-border); }

.prose code { font-family: ui-monospace, monospace; font-size: 13px; }
.prose table { border-collapse: collapse; }
.prose th, .prose td { padding: 4px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--ks-border); text-align: left; vertical-align: top; }
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/shared/mock-backend.ts`:

````ts
import { HttpErrorResponse, HttpResponse, type HttpInterceptorFn } from '@angular/common/http';
import type { SpotlightGroup, SpotlightItem, SpotlightResponse } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { delay, of, switchMap, throwError, timer } from 'rxjs';

const CUSTOMERS = ['Acme A/S', 'Nordlys ApS', 'Fjord Media', 'Bølge Consulting', 'Havn & Co', 'Skov Retail', 'Acme Nordic'];
const ORDERS = [1001, 1002, 1017, 1042, 1100, 1101, 2001];
const LEGACY = [
  { key: 'L-17', name: 'Legacy campaign 17' },
  { key: 'L-42', name: 'Legacy campaign 42' },
  { key: 'L-99', name: 'Archived campaign 99' },
];

function searchResponse(query: string, chip: string | null): SpotlightResponse {
  const customers: SpotlightItem[] = CUSTOMERS.flatMap((name, i) =>
    name.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      ? [
          {
            id: `customer-${i}`,
            title: name,
            subtitle: 'Customer',
            icon: 'pi pi-building',
            group: 'customers',
            ...(i % 3 === 0 ? { badges: [{ label: 'VIP', tone: 'success' as const }] } : {}),
            action: { url: `https://example.com/customers/${i}`, newTab: true },
          },
        ]
      : [],
  );
  const orders: SpotlightItem[] = ORDERS.flatMap((n) =>
    String(n).includes(query) || `order ${n}`.includes(query)
      ? [
          {
            id: `order-${n}`,
            title: `Order #${n}`,
            icon: 'pi pi-shopping-cart',
            group: 'orders',
            badges: [n > 1500 ? { label: 'open', tone: 'warn' as const } : { label: 'paid', tone: 'info' as const }],
            action: { url: `https://example.com/orders/${n}`, newTab: true },
          },
        ]
      : [],
  );
  const groups: SpotlightGroup[] = [
    { id: 'customers', label: 'Customers', total: customers.length, items: customers.slice(0, 5) },
    { id: 'orders', label: 'Orders', total: orders.length, items: orders.slice(0, 5) },
  ];
  return { groups: groups.filter((g) => (chip === null || g.id === chip) && g.items.length > 0) };
}

let flakyCalls = 0;

/** An in-browser backend for the HTTP examples. Real apps call their own API instead. */
export const mockBackendInterceptor: HttpInterceptorFn = (req, next) => {
  const query = (req.params.get('q') ?? req.params.get('term') ?? '').toLowerCase();
  switch (req.url) {
    case '/api/search':
      return of(new HttpResponse({ status: 200, body: searchResponse(query, req.params.get('chip')) })).pipe(delay(300));
    case '/api/legacy-search':
      return of(new HttpResponse({ status: 200, body: { hits: LEGACY.filter((h) => h.name.toLowerCase().includes(query)) } })).pipe(
        delay(200),
      );
    case '/api/flaky':
      flakyCalls += 1;
      if (flakyCalls % 2 === 1) {
        return timer(200).pipe(
          switchMap(() => throwError(() => new HttpErrorResponse({ status: 503, statusText: 'Service Unavailable', url: req.url }))),
        );
      }
      return of(
        new HttpResponse({
          status: 200,
          body: [{ id: 'flaky-ok', title: `Flaky service answered "${query}"`, group: 'Flaky service', action: { route: '/http-source' } }],
        }),
      ).pipe(delay(200));
    default:
      return next(req);
  }
};
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the twelve examples**

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/http-source.example.ts`:

````ts
import { provideHttpClient, withInterceptors } from '@angular/common/http';
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component } from '@angular/core';
import { httpSource, localStorageRecent, provideSpotlight, SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { mockBackendInterceptor } from '../shared/mock-backend';

interface LegacyResponse {
  hits: Array<{ key: string; name: string }>;
}

export const httpSourceProviders = [
  // The interceptor stands in for a server; GET /api/search returns the default SpotlightResponse.
  provideHttpClient(withInterceptors([mockBackendInterceptor])),
  provideSpotlight({
    isolated: true,
    hotkeys: [],
    recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('example-http-source') },
    // Global override: every httpSource registered here sends this request shape.
    http: {
      request: (query, ctx) => ({
        params: ctx.chip !== null ? { q: query, chip: ctx.chip } : { q: query },
        headers: { 'x-demo-tenant': 'kitchen-sink' },
      }),
    },
    sources: [
      // 1. Defaults: GET /api/search?q=…[&chip=…], grouped response, server order kept.
      httpSource({ id: 'search', url: '/api/search' }),
      // 2. Per-source override: a legacy API with its own request and response shape.
      httpSource({
        id: 'legacy',
        url: '/api/legacy-search',
        group: 'Legacy',
        request: (query) => ({ params: { term: query } }),
        map: (response) =>
          (response as LegacyResponse).hits.map((hit) => ({
            id: hit.key,
            title: hit.name,
            subtitle: 'Legacy system',
            group: 'Legacy',
            action: { url: `https://example.com/legacy/${hit.key}`, newTab: true },
          })),
      }),
      // 3. A failing service: its group shows an error row with Retry; the others are unaffected.
      httpSource({ id: 'flaky', url: '/api/flaky', group: 'Flaky service', minLength: 1 }),
    ],
  }),
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-http-source-example',
  imports: [SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger>Search the mock backend</button>
    <p>Try "acme", "10" or "campaign". Tab moves between the chips; the chip is sent as <code>chip=</code>.</p>
    <spotlight-host />
  `,
})
export class HttpSourceExample {}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/custom-templates.example.ts`:

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component } from '@angular/core';
import {
  localStorageRecent,
  provideSpotlight,
  SpotlightEmptyTemplate,
  SpotlightFooterTemplate,
  SpotlightHost,
  SpotlightItemTemplate,
  SpotlightTrigger,
  staticSource,
  type SpotlightItem,
} from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';

export const customTemplatesProviders = [
  provideSpotlight({
    isolated: true,
    hotkeys: [],
    recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('example-custom-templates') },
    sources: [
      staticSource({
        group: 'Customers',
        items: [
          {
            id: 'acme',
            title: 'Acme A/S',
            subtitle: 'Copenhagen',
            suggested: true,
            badges: [{ label: 'VIP', tone: 'success' }],
            data: { revenue: 1_200_000 },
            action: { route: '/custom-templates' },
          },
          {
            id: 'nordlys',
            title: 'Nordlys ApS',
            subtitle: 'Aarhus',
            badges: [{ label: 'overdue', tone: 'danger' }],
            data: { revenue: 340_000 },
            action: { route: '/custom-templates' },
          },
        ],
      }),
      staticSource({
        group: 'Commands',
        items: [{ id: 'new', title: 'New campaign', icon: 'pi pi-plus', suggested: true, action: { run: () => undefined } }],
      }),
    ],
  }),
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-custom-templates-example',
  imports: [SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger, SpotlightItemTemplate, SpotlightEmptyTemplate, SpotlightFooterTemplate],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  styles: `
    .customer-row { display: flex; flex-direction: column; flex: 1; }
    .customer-row.is-active strong { text-decoration: underline; }
    .customer-meta { color: var(--sp-muted); font-size: 12px; }
    .customer-badge { margin-left: 6px; font-size: 11px; text-transform: uppercase; }
    .customer-badge[data-tone='danger'] { color: var(--sp-danger); }
    .customer-badge[data-tone='success'] { color: var(--sp-success); }
  `,
  template: `
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger>Open with custom rows</button>
    <spotlight-host>
      <!-- Per-group row: only the Customers group uses it. -->
      <ng-template spotlightItem="Customers" let-item let-active="active">
        <span class="customer-row" [class.is-active]="active">
          <strong>
            {{ item.title }}
            @for (badge of item.badges ?? []; track badge.label) {
              <span class="customer-badge" [attr.data-tone]="badge.tone">{{ badge.label }}</span>
            }
          </strong>
          <span class="customer-meta">{{ item.subtitle }} · revenue {{ revenue(item) }} DKK</span>
        </span>
      </ng-template>
      <!-- No fallback template here, so Commands keep the renderer's row. -->
      <ng-template spotlightEmpty let-query>No match for "{{ query }}". Try "acme".</ng-template>
      <ng-template spotlightFooter><span>Custom footer: Enter opens, Esc closes.</span></ng-template>
    </spotlight-host>
  `,
})
export class CustomTemplatesExample {
  protected revenue(item: SpotlightItem): string {
    return ((item.data as { revenue: number }).revenue).toLocaleString('en-US');
  }
}
````

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, ViewEncapsulation } from '@angular/core';
import {
  localStorageRecent,
  provideSpotlight,
  SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER,
  SpotlightHost,
  SpotlightTrigger,
  type SpotlightRenderer,
} from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { demoSources } from '../shared/demo-data';

// theme: 'none' ships no CSS. A partial renderer adds a class to the pane so the app can style it.
export const themeNoneProviders = [
  provideSpotlight({ isolated: true, hotkeys: [], theme: 'none', recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('example-theme-none') }, sources: demoSources() }),
  { provide: SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER, useValue: { dialog: { panelClass: 'demo-bare', backdropClass: 'demo-bare-backdrop' } } satisfies SpotlightRenderer },
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-theme-none-example',
  imports: [SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  encapsulation: ViewEncapsulation.None,
  styles: `
    .demo-bare-backdrop { background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5); }
    .demo-bare .sp-dialog { display: flex; flex-direction: column; max-height: 70vh; background: #fffbe6; color: #222; border: 2px solid #222; font-family: Georgia, serif; }
    .demo-bare .sp-input-el { width: 100%; padding: 12px; border: 0; border-bottom: 2px solid #222; background: transparent; color: inherit; font: inherit; }
    .demo-bare .sp-list { overflow: auto; }
    .demo-bare .sp-group-header { padding: 6px 12px; font-weight: bold; }
    .demo-bare .sp-option { display: flex; gap: 8px; padding: 6px 12px; cursor: pointer; }
    .demo-bare .sp-option[aria-selected='true'] { background: #222; color: #fffbe6; }
    .demo-bare .sp-chips, .demo-bare .sp-footer { display: flex; gap: 8px; padding: 6px 12px; font-size: 12px; }
  `,
  template: `
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger>theme: 'none' + your own CSS</button>
    <spotlight-host />
  `,
})
export class ThemeNoneExample {}
````

````ts
import { demoSources } from '../shared/demo-data';

export const hotkeysPrefixesProviders = [
  provideSpotlight({
    isolated: true,
    // A custom shortcut. 'mod' would mean Cmd on macOS and Ctrl elsewhere, e.g. 'mod.shift.p'.
    hotkeys: ['alt.k'],
    recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('example-hotkeys-prefixes') },
    sources: [
      // '#1042' searches only orders; '#' alone lists them all.
      staticSource({
        group: 'Orders',
        prefix: '#',
        items: [1001, 1002, 1017, 1042].map((n) => ({
          id: `order-${n}`,
          title: `Order ${n}`,
          icon: 'pi pi-shopping-cart',
          action: { route: '/hotkeys-and-prefixes' },

      staticSource({
        group: 'People',

        items: ['Anna Holm', 'Bo Lund', 'Carla Berg', 'Dennis Krog'].map((name) => ({
          id: `person-${name}`,
          title: name,
          icon: 'pi pi-user',
          action: { route: '/hotkeys-and-prefixes' },

];
````

````ts
import { localStorageRecent, provideSpotlight, SpotlightHost, SpotlightService, SpotlightTrigger } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { map, scan } from 'rxjs';
import { demoSources } from '../shared/demo-data';

  template: `
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger>Try it with the keyboard only</button>
    <p>Selected so far (from <code>SpotlightService.selected</code>): {{ log() }}</p>
    <spotlight-host />
  `,
})
export class AccessibilityExample {
  protected readonly log = toSignal(
    inject(SpotlightService).selected.pipe(
      scan((titles: string[], item) => [...titles, item.title], []),
      map((titles) => titles.join(', ')),
    ),
    { initialValue: 'nothing yet' },
  );
}
````
