`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/byo-source.example.ts`:

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, signal } from '@angular/core';
import {
  localStorageRecent,
  provideSpotlight,
  SpotlightHost,
  SpotlightTrigger,
  type SpotlightItem,
  type SpotlightSource,
} from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';

const COLORS = ['crimson', 'coral', 'gold', 'olive', 'teal', 'navy', 'indigo', 'orchid', 'salmon', 'tomato'];
const picked = signal<string | null>(null);

/** A source from scratch: async, cancellable through ctx.signal, results in our own order. */
const colorSource: SpotlightSource = {
  id: 'colors',
  group: 'Colors',
  minLength: 1,
  debounceMs: 100,
  search: (query, { signal: abort }) =>
    new Promise<SpotlightItem[]>((resolve, reject) => {
      const timerId = setTimeout(() => {
        resolve(
          COLORS.filter((c) => c.includes(query.toLowerCase())).map((c) => ({
            id: c,
            title: c,
            group: 'Colors',
            icon: 'pi pi-palette',
            action: { run: () => picked.set(c) },
          })),
        );
      }, 250);
      abort.addEventListener('abort', () => {
        clearTimeout(timerId);
        reject(new DOMException('Superseded by a newer query', 'AbortError'));
      });
    }),
};

export const byoSourceProviders = [
  provideSpotlight({ isolated: true, hotkeys: [], recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('example-byo-source') }, sources: [colorSource] }),
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-byo-source-example',
  imports: [SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger>Search colours</button>
    <p [style.color]="picked() ?? 'inherit'">Picked: {{ picked() ?? 'none' }}</p>
    <spotlight-host />
  `,
})
export class ByoSourceExample {
  protected readonly picked = picked;
}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/byo-lingui.example.ts`:

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { LinguiService, provideLingui, type LinguiCatalog } from '@tocdk/lingui-angular';
import { localStorageRecent, provideSpotlight, SpotlightFooterTemplate, SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { demoSources } from '../shared/demo-data';

// Explicit ids keep the catalogs readable; a real app extracts them with `lingui extract`.
const EN: LinguiCatalog = { messages: {} };
const DA: LinguiCatalog = {
  messages: {
    'spotlight.placeholder': 'Søg…',
    'spotlight.all': 'Alle',
    'spotlight.recent': 'Seneste',
    'spotlight.noResults': 'Ingen resultater',
    'spotlight.loading': 'Indlæser…',
    'spotlight.error': 'Noget gik galt',
    'spotlight.retry': 'Prøv igen',
    'spotlight.hintNavigate': 'for at navigere',
    'spotlight.hintOpen': 'for at åbne',
    'spotlight.hintClose': 'for at lukke',
    'spotlight.result': 'resultat',
    'spotlight.results': 'resultater',
  },
};

export const byoLinguiProviders = [
  provideLingui({ sourceLocale: 'en', locales: ['en', 'da'], loader: (locale) => Promise.resolve(locale === 'da' ? DA : EN) }),
  // The factory form runs in an injection context, so the texts can be LinguiService signals.
  provideSpotlight(() => {
    const lingui = inject(LinguiService);
    const t = (key: string, message: string) => lingui.t$({ id: `spotlight.${key}`, message });
    return {
      isolated: true,
      hotkeys: [],
      recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('example-byo-lingui') },
      sources: demoSources(),
      texts: {
        placeholder: t('placeholder', 'Search…'),
        all: t('all', 'All'),
        recent: t('recent', 'Recent'),
        noResults: t('noResults', 'No results'),
        loading: t('loading', 'Loading…'),
        error: t('error', 'Something went wrong'),
        retry: t('retry', 'Retry'),
        hintNavigate: t('hintNavigate', 'to navigate'),
        hintOpen: t('hintOpen', 'to open'),
        hintClose: t('hintClose', 'to close'),
        resultsAnnouncement: (count) => {
          lingui.locale(); // read the locale signal so the announcement follows a switch
          const word = count === 1 ? lingui.t({ id: 'spotlight.result', message: 'result' }) : lingui.t({ id: 'spotlight.results', message: 'results' });
          return `${count} ${word}`;
        },
      },
    };
  }),
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-byo-lingui-example',
  imports: [SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger, SpotlightFooterTemplate],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <p>Open the palette and switch the language with the buttons in its footer: every text updates while it stays open.</p>
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger>Open the palette</button>
    <spotlight-host>
      <ng-template spotlightFooter>
        <span role="group" aria-label="Language">
          @for (locale of lingui.locales; track locale) {
            <button type="button" class="demo-button" [attr.aria-pressed]="lingui.locale() === locale" (click)="switchTo(locale)">
              {{ locale.toUpperCase() }}
            </button>
          }
        </span>
      </ng-template>
    </spotlight-host>
  `,
})
export class ByoLinguiExample {
  protected readonly lingui = inject(LinguiService);

  protected switchTo(locale: string): void {
    void this.lingui.activate(locale);
  }
}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/byo-recent.example.ts`:

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component } from '@angular/core';
import {
  provideSpotlight,
  SpotlightHost,
  SpotlightTrigger,
  type SpotlightRecentEntry,
  type SpotlightRecentStorage,
} from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { demoSources } from '../shared/demo-data';

/** Recent items in sessionStorage instead of localStorage: forgotten when the tab closes. */
const sessionRecent: SpotlightRecentStorage = {
  load: () => {
    try {
      return Promise.resolve(JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('demo-recent') ?? '[]') as SpotlightRecentEntry[]);
    } catch {
      return Promise.resolve([]);
    }
  },
  save: (items) => {
    try {
      sessionStorage.setItem('demo-recent', JSON.stringify(items));
    } catch {
      // Storage blocked: Recent lasts until the palette instance goes away.
    }
    return Promise.resolve();
  },
};

export const byoRecentProviders = [
  provideSpotlight({ isolated: true, hotkeys: [], recent: { max: 3, storage: sessionRecent }, sources: demoSources() }),
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-byo-recent-example',
  imports: [SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger>Pick something, then reopen</button>
    <p>Recent keeps the last 3 picks in sessionStorage.</p>
    <spotlight-host />
  `,
})
export class ByoRecentExample {}
````

````ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, input } from '@angular/core';
import {
  localStorageRecent,
  provideSpotlight,
  SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER,
  SpotlightHost,
  SpotlightTrigger,
  type SpotlightBadgeContext,
  type SpotlightRenderer,
  type SpotlightRowContext,
} from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';
import { demoSources } from '../shared/demo-data';

/** A row part: markup only. The host already provides role="option", ids and aria-selected. */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-compact-row',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  styles: `
    :host { display: flex; flex: 1; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px; }
    .group { color: var(--sp-muted); font-size: 12px; }
  `,
  template: `<span>{{ ctx().item.title }}</span><span class="group">{{ ctx().item.group }}</span>`,
})
export class CompactRow {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightRowContext>();
}

/** A badge part. Rows that keep the core row render badges through it. */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-dot-badge',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `● {{ ctx().badge.label }}`,
})
export class DotBadge {
  readonly ctx = input.required<SpotlightBadgeContext>();
}

// A starter adapter: replace any subset of dialog/input/chips/groupHeader/row/badge.
const compactRenderer: SpotlightRenderer = { row: CompactRow, badge: DotBadge };

export const byoRendererProviders = [
  provideSpotlight({ isolated: true, hotkeys: [], recent: { storage: localStorageRecent('example-byo-renderer') }, sources: demoSources() }),
  { provide: SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER, useValue: compactRenderer },
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-byo-renderer-example',
  imports: [SpotlightHost, SpotlightTrigger],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <button type="button" class="demo-button" spotlightTrigger>Open with a custom row part</button>
    <spotlight-host />
  `,
})
export class ByoRendererExample {}
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/content/http-source.content.ts`:

````ts
import type { PageContent } from '../shared/page-content.types';

export const HTTP_SOURCE_CONTENT: PageContent = {
  title: 'HTTP source',
  sections: [
    {
      id: 'format',
      title: 'Default request and response',
      markdown: `
\`httpSource({ url })\` sends \`GET {url}?q={query}\`, plus \`chip={groupId}\` when a chip other than All is active.
It expects a \`SpotlightResponse\`; a flat \`SpotlightItem[]\` is grouped by \`item.group\`. Results keep the server's order.

\`\`\`json
{ "groups": [{ "id": "customers", "label": "Customers", "total": 12, "items": [
  { "id": "c-1", "title": "Acme A/S", "action": { "url": "/customers/c-1" } }
] }] }
\`\`\`

Backends in any language can validate against \`@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/schema/spotlight-response.schema.json\`.
Defaults: \`minLength\` 2, \`debounceMs\` 150, \`timeoutMs\` 5000. A new keystroke cancels the request in flight.
The source uses \`HttpClient\`, so the app's interceptors (auth, tenant) apply; call \`provideHttpClient()\`.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'overrides',
      title: 'Default + override',
      markdown: `
1. Nothing set: the defaults above.
2. Global: \`provideSpotlight({ http: { request, map } })\` applies to every \`httpSource\` registered there (\`SpotlightHttpOptions\`).
3. Per source: \`httpSource({ url, request, map })\` wins over the global setting (\`HttpSourceOptions\`).

\`request(query, ctx)\` returns a \`SpotlightHttpRequest\` (\`method\`, \`params\`, \`headers\`, \`body\`);
\`map(response, query)\` returns a \`SpotlightResult\`. A failing or timed-out source shows one error row with Retry in its
own group, calls \`onError\`, and leaves the other groups alone.
      `,
    },
  ],
  examples: [{ key: 'http-source', title: 'Grouped format, global and per-source overrides, a failing service' }],
};
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/content/custom-templates.content.ts`:

````ts
import type { PageContent } from '../shared/page-content.types';

export const CUSTOM_TEMPLATES_CONTENT: PageContent = {
  title: 'Custom templates',
  sections: [
    {
      id: 'templates',
      title: 'Rows, empty state and footer',
      markdown: `
\`\`\`html
<spotlight-host>
  <ng-template spotlightItem="customers" let-item let-active="active">…</ng-template>  <!-- per-group row -->
  <ng-template spotlightItem let-item>…</ng-template>                                    <!-- fallback row -->
  <ng-template spotlightEmpty let-query>…</ng-template>
  <ng-template spotlightFooter>…</ng-template>
</spotlight-host>
\`\`\`

Precedence: your per-group template, then your fallback template, then the adapter's row, then the core row.
Templates get \`SpotlightItemTemplateContext\` (\`$implicit\` item, \`active\`, \`query\`) or \`SpotlightEmptyTemplateContext\`.
Put anything you need in \`item.data\` and \`item.badges\`. The host keeps \`role="option"\` and the ARIA state, so a
template only supplies markup.
      `,
    },
  ],
  examples: [{ key: 'custom-templates', title: 'Per-group row with badges, empty state and footer' }],
};
````

````ts
import type { PageContent } from '../shared/page-content.types';

export const THEMES_CONTENT: PageContent = {
  title: 'Themes',
  sections: [
    {
      id: 'default',
      title: "theme: 'default'",
      markdown: `
One stylesheet that uses CSS variables only: \`--sp-bg\`, \`--sp-fg\`, \`--sp-muted\`, \`--sp-accent\`, \`--sp-accent-fg\`,
\`--sp-active-bg\`, \`--sp-border\`, \`--sp-backdrop\`, \`--sp-radius\`, \`--sp-font\`, \`--sp-shadow\` and the badge tones
\`--sp-info\`, \`--sp-success\`, \`--sp-warn\`, \`--sp-danger\`. It follows \`prefers-color-scheme\`; the classes \`sp-dark\`
and \`sp-light\` on any ancestor force a mode. Override a variable on \`:root\` or on a pane class.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'none',
      title: "theme: 'none'",
      markdown: `
Ships no CSS. Style \`.sp-dialog\`, \`.sp-input-el\`, \`.sp-option[aria-selected='true']\`, … yourself, scoped by a pane
class from a partial \`SpotlightRenderer\` (\`{ dialog: { panelClass } }\` via \`SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER\`).
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'primeng',
      title: 'PrimeNG',
      markdown: `
\`\`\`ts
import { provideSpotlightPrimeNG } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/primeng';
providers: [providePrimeNG({ theme: { preset: Aura } }), provideSpotlight({ … }), provideSpotlightPrimeNG()]
\`\`\`

The palette then renders with \`p-iconfield\` + \`pInputText\`, \`p-chip\`, \`p-tag\` and \`pi\` icon classes, and takes its
colours from the PrimeNG preset (\`SPOTLIGHT_PRIMENG_CSS\` maps the \`--sp-*\` variables to PrimeNG tokens), so white-label
presets apply. Apps that never import \`/primeng\` never load PrimeNG. A test keeps its accessibility tree identical to the core's.
      `,
    },
  ],
  examples: [
    { key: 'theme-default', title: 'Default theme (toggle dark mode in the top bar)' },
    { key: 'theme-none', title: "theme: 'none' with app CSS" },
    { key: 'theme-primeng', title: 'PrimeNG adapter (Aura preset)' },
    { key: 'theme-variables', title: 'CSS-variable overrides' },
  ],
};
````

````ts
import type { PageContent } from '../shared/page-content.types';

export const HOTKEYS_CONTENT: PageContent = {
  title: 'Hotkeys & prefixes',
  sections: [
    {
      id: 'hotkeys',
      title: 'Hotkeys',
      markdown: `
Default: \`['ctrl.space', 'mod.k']\`. \`mod\` is Cmd on macOS and Ctrl elsewhere. Modifiers: \`ctrl\`, \`alt\`, \`shift\`,
\`meta\`, \`mod\`; keys: letters, digits, \`space\`, \`slash\`, \`period\`, \`comma\`, \`dash\`, \`enter\`, \`escape\`.
Pressing the hotkey again closes the palette. Hotkeys are ignored during IME composition.

**macOS caveat:** the system may use Ctrl+Space to switch input source, which is why \`mod.k\` is in the default set.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'prefixes',
      title: 'Prefixes',
      markdown: `
A source with \`prefix: '#'\` runs alone when the query starts with \`#\`, and receives the query without it.
\`#\` alone lists everything that source returns. Without the prefix the source still takes part in normal searches.
      `,
    },
  ],
  examples: [{ key: 'hotkeys-prefixes', title: 'Alt+K, # for orders and @ for people' }],
};
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/content/accessibility.content.ts`:

````ts
import type { PageContent } from '../shared/page-content.types';

export const ACCESSIBILITY_CONTENT: PageContent = {
  title: 'Accessibility',
  sections: [
    {
      id: 'keyboard',
      title: 'Keyboard-only walkthrough',
      markdown: `
1. Press **Ctrl+Space** (or **⌘K / Ctrl+K**), or focus a \`spotlightTrigger\` button and press **Enter**.
2. Type. The first result is already active, so **Enter** opens it.
3. **↓ / ↑** move through results (they wrap). **Tab / Shift+Tab** switch between the group chips.
4. **Ctrl+Enter / ⌘+Enter** opens a link result in a new tab.
5. On an error row, **Enter** retries that source.
6. **Esc** closes; focus returns to where it was.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'screen-readers',
      title: 'Screen-reader notes',
      markdown: `
The palette follows the WAI-ARIA combobox pattern inside a modal dialog: the input is \`role="combobox"\` with
\`aria-expanded\`, \`aria-controls\` and \`aria-activedescendant\`; results are a \`role="listbox"\` of \`role="group"\`
sections (labelled by their headers) containing \`role="option"\` rows with \`aria-selected\`. Focus stays in the input
and is trapped in the dialog. A polite live region announces the result count (\`texts.resultsAnnouncement\`).
Chips are buttons with \`aria-pressed\`. The test suite runs axe on the open palette in light, dark and PrimeNG modes;
colour contrast is checked by hand because the test DOM cannot compute it.
      `,
    },
  ],
  examples: [{ key: 'accessibility', title: 'Keyboard-only, with the selected stream' }],
};
````

`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/content/build-your-own.content.ts`:

````ts
import type { PageContent } from '../shared/page-content.types';

export const BUILD_YOUR_OWN_CONTENT: PageContent = {
  title: 'Build your own',
  sections: [
    {
      id: 'source',
      title: 'A source from scratch',
      markdown: `
Implement \`SpotlightSource\`: \`id\`, optional \`group\`, \`prefix\`, \`minLength\`, \`debounceMs\`, \`timeoutMs\`,
\`priority\`, \`local\` (\`true\` = the library ranks your items) and \`search(query, ctx)\` returning items, a promise or an
Observable. Stop work when \`ctx.signal\` aborts: the next keystroke has replaced the query.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'i18n',
      title: 'Texts with @tocdk/lingui-angular',
      markdown: `
Every text (\`SpotlightTexts\`) can be a string, a \`Signal<string>\` or an \`Observable<string>\`, so an open palette
switches language live. The library has no i18n dependency; pass \`provideSpotlight(() => …)\` to build the texts
with \`inject(LinguiService).t$(…)\`. Defaults: \`DEFAULT_SPOTLIGHT_TEXTS\`.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'recent',
      title: 'Recent storage',
      markdown: `
\`recent: { max, storage }\` takes any \`SpotlightRecentStorage\` (\`load(): Promise<SpotlightRecentEntry[]>\`,
\`save(items)\`). The default, \`localStorageRecent(key)\`, survives blocked storage. Only what is needed to re-render and
re-run a \`route\` or \`url\` item is stored; \`run()\` items are stored by id and looked up in their source.
      `,
    },
    {
      id: 'renderer',
      title: 'A starter renderer adapter',
      markdown: `
Provide \`SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER\` with any of \`dialog\` (pane/backdrop classes), \`input\`, \`chips\`, \`groupHeader\`, \`row\`
and \`badge\`. Each part is a component with one \`ctx\` input (\`SpotlightRowContext\`, \`SpotlightBadgeContext\`, …). The
host owns behaviour, state and ARIA; an \`input\` part must copy the ARIA fields of \`SpotlightInputContext\` onto its
\`<input>\`. Start from \`SpotlightDefaultRow\` and friends.
      `,
    },
  ],
  examples: [
    { key: 'byo-source', title: 'An async, cancellable custom source' },
    { key: 'byo-lingui', title: 'Texts from @tocdk/lingui-angular with live locale switching' },
    { key: 'byo-recent', title: 'Recent items in sessionStorage' },
    { key: 'byo-renderer', title: 'A starter renderer adapter (row + badge parts)' },
  ],
};
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `README.md`**

`README.md`:

`````markdown
# @tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight

[![CI](https://github.com/tocDK/ng-spotlight/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/tocDK/ng-spotlight/actions/workflows/ci.yml)
[![npm](https://img.shields.io/npm/v/@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight.svg)](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight)
[![License: MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/license-MIT-blue.svg)](LICENSE)
[![Angular 21](https://img.shields.io/badge/angular-21-dd0031.svg)](https://angular.dev/)

A keyboard-first spotlight (command palette) for Angular. Press **Ctrl+Space** or **⌘K**, type, and jump straight to a
page, a record or a command. Headless core, a PrimeNG adapter, WAI-ARIA combobox pattern, zoneless and signal-based.

**Live demo and docs: [tocdk.github.io/ng-spotlight](https://tocdk.github.io/ng-spotlight/)** (every page shows its own source).

## Install

```bash
npm i @tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight
```

Peer dependencies: `@angular/core`, `@angular/common`, `@angular/router`, `@angular/cdk` and `rxjs`. Versions follow
Angular majors: `21.x` supports Angular 21, `22.x` will support Angular 22. For the PrimeNG adapter you also need `primeng` 21.

## Three-line setup

```ts
// app.config.ts
import { provideSpotlight, routeSource } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';

export const appConfig = {
  providers: [provideRouter(routes), provideSpotlight({ sources: [routeSource()] })],
};
```

```html
<!-- app shell, once -->
<spotlight-host />
<button spotlightTrigger>Search</button>
```

Every route with a `title` (or `data.spotlight.label`, or `data.breadcrumb`) is now searchable. Routes with a `:param`
are skipped; `data: { spotlight: false }` opts a route out.

## Sources

```ts
provideSpotlight({
  hotkeys: ['ctrl.space', 'mod.k'],                       // default; 'mod' = Cmd on macOS, Ctrl elsewhere
  texts: { placeholder: 'Search…' },                      // string | Signal<string> | Observable<string>
  recent: { max: 8, storage: localStorageRecent('app') }, // default storage: localStorage
  theme: 'default',                                       // or 'none'
  onError: (err, source) => reportToSentry(err, source.id),
  sources: [
    routeSource({ group: 'Pages', filter: (route, url) => canSee(url) }),
    staticSource({ group: 'Commands', items: [{ id: 'logout', title: 'Log out', action: { run: () => auth.logout() } }] }),
    httpSource({ url: '/api/search' }),                   // needs provideHttpClient()
  ],
});
```

- `routeSource()` walks the Router config, including lazy children once loaded. Local results, ranked by the library.
- `staticSource({ group, items })` holds fixed commands. Local results.
- `httpSource({ url })` sends `GET {url}?q={query}` (plus `chip={groupId}` when a chip is active) and expects
  `{ groups: [{ id, label, total?, items }] }` or a flat item array. Results keep the server's order. Override the
  request and response globally (`provideSpotlight({ http: { request, map } })`) or per source
  (`httpSource({ url, request, map })`). The JSON Schema ships as `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/schema/spotlight-response.schema.json`.
- Any object implementing `SpotlightSource` is a source: `search(query, ctx)` may return items, a promise or an
  Observable, and should stop when `ctx.signal` aborts.

Each item has an `action`: `{ route }` (Router), `{ url, newTab? }` (only http, https, mailto, tel and relative URLs
are allowed) or `{ run }`. A query that starts with a source's `prefix` (for example `#` or `@`) searches only that source.
Call `provideSpotlight()` again in a lazy route's `providers` to add sources for that area.

## CI check for unlabelled routes

```ts
import { spotlightRouteAudit } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight';

it('labels every admin route', () => {
  expect(spotlightRouteAudit(adminRoutes)).toEqual([]);
});
```

## Templates

```html
<spotlight-host>
  <ng-template spotlightItem="customers" let-item>…</ng-template>  <!-- per-group row -->
  <ng-template spotlightItem let-item>…</ng-template>              <!-- fallback row -->
  <ng-template spotlightEmpty let-query>…</ng-template>
  <ng-template spotlightFooter>…</ng-template>
</spotlight-host>
```

## Theming and PrimeNG

The default theme uses CSS variables only (`--sp-bg`, `--sp-fg`, `--sp-muted`, `--sp-accent`, `--sp-radius`,
`--sp-font`, `--sp-shadow`, …) and follows `prefers-color-scheme` (force a mode with the `sp-dark` / `sp-light` class).
`theme: 'none'` ships no CSS.

```ts
import { provideSpotlightPrimeNG } from '@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/primeng';

providers: [providePrimeNG({ theme: { preset: Aura } }), provideSpotlight({ … }), provideSpotlightPrimeNG()];
```

The adapter renders with `p-iconfield` + `pInputText`, `p-chip`, `p-tag` and `pi` icons and takes its colours from your
PrimeNG preset. Apps that never import `/primeng` never load PrimeNG. Other design systems: provide `SPOTLIGHT_RENDERER`
with your own part components (see "Build your own" in the docs).

## Accessibility

WAI-ARIA combobox inside a modal dialog: `role="combobox"` input with `aria-activedescendant`, a `role="listbox"` of
labelled `role="group"`s and `role="option"`s, a focus trap with focus restore, and a polite live region for the result
count. Keys: ↑/↓ move, Enter opens, Ctrl/⌘+Enter opens links in a new tab, Tab/Shift+Tab switch chips, Esc closes.
The tests run axe on the open palette with zero violations. Note: macOS may use Ctrl+Space to switch input source,
which is why ⌘K is in the default set.

## Contributing

See [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md). Changes are listed in [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md).

## License

[MIT](LICENSE) © Thomas Christensen (tocDK)
`````

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `CHANGELOG.md` and `CONTRIBUTING.md`**

`CHANGELOG.md`:

`````markdown
# Changelog

All notable changes to `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight` are documented here.

Format: [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/). Versioning: the major follows Angular's major;
minor and patch follow [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/).

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- Headless core: `provideSpotlight`, `<spotlight-host>`, `SpotlightService`, the `spotlightTrigger`, `spotlightItem`,
  `spotlightEmpty` and `spotlightFooter` directives, hotkeys (`ctrl.space`, `mod.k`), keyboard navigation, chips,
  prefixes, Recent items (`localStorageRecent`), texts as strings, signals or observables.
- Sources: `routeSource` (with lazy children), `staticSource`, `httpSource` (default grouped format with global and
  per-source `request`/`map` overrides) and the `spotlightRouteAudit` helper.
- `schema/spotlight-response.schema.json` for the HTTP response format.
- Default CSS-variable theme with a dark variant; `theme: 'none'`.
- `@tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight/primeng`: `provideSpotlightPrimeNG()` renderer adapter.
- Kitchen sink on GitHub Pages: <https://tocdk.github.io/ng-spotlight/>.

[Unreleased]: https://github.com/tocDK/ng-spotlight/commits/main
`````

`CONTRIBUTING.md`:

`````markdown
# Contributing to @tocdk-oss/ng-spotlight

PRs are welcome. Every change lands through a pull request against `main`; branch names follow
`toc/<topic>-<short-description>`. Use [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/) prefixes
(`feat:`, `fix:`, `docs:`, `chore:`, `ci:`, `test:`, `refactor:`).

## Setup

```bash
git clone https://github.com/tocDK/ng-spotlight.git
cd ng-spotlight
npm install
```

## Commands

| Command | What it does |
|---|---|
| `npm start` | Kitchen sink at http://localhost:4200 |
| `npm test` | All vitest projects (lib, demo, tooling) |
| `npm run test:lib` / `npm run test:demo` | One project |
| `npm run test:coverage` | With coverage thresholds (≥ 90 % lines/functions/statements, ≥ 85 % branches) |
| `npm run lint` | ESLint |
| `npm run build:lib` | ng-packagr → `dist/ng-spotlight/` (core + `/primeng`) |
| `npm run check:size` | Core bundle ≤ 15 kB gzipped (run after `build:lib`) |
| `npm run build:demo` | Kitchen sink with the GitHub Pages base href |
| `npm run check:docs` | Every public export is documented in a kitchen-sink content module |
| `npm run check:workflows` | Workflow hygiene (pinned SHAs, no persisted credentials, permissions, no `${{ }}` in `run:`) |

## Adding a feature

1. Write the failing test first (vitest, jsdom; every TestBed adds `provideZonelessChangeDetection()`).
2. Implement it. Keep ARIA in the host; renderer parts only render markup.
3. Add or update a kitchen-sink example (`projects/kitchen-sink/src/app/examples/<key>.example.ts`) and its content page.
4. `npm test`, `npm run lint` and `npm run check:docs` must pass.
5. Add a CHANGELOG entry under `[Unreleased]`.

## Workflow rules

Pin every action to a full commit SHA with a version comment, set `persist-credentials: false` on checkout, grant
permissions per job only, and pass values into `run:` through `env:`. `npm run check:workflows` enforces this.

## Releasing (operator only)

Publishing happens only when the operator pushes a version tag.

One-time setup:
1. npm: create an automation token for the `@tocdk` scope and save it as the repository secret `NPM_TOKEN`
   (or configure npm trusted publishing for this repository and workflow `release.yml`, then delete the secret).
2. GitHub: create the environment `npm` (Settings → Environments) with yourself as required reviewer, so every
   publish waits for approval.
3. GitHub Pages: Settings → Pages → Source: GitHub Actions.

Each release:
1. Bump `projects/ng-spotlight/package.json` and `projects/ng-spotlight/src/lib/version.ts`, move the
   `[Unreleased]` CHANGELOG entries under the new version, merge to `main`.
2. `git tag v<version> && git push origin v<version>`.
3. Approve the `npm` environment in the release run. The workflow checks the tag against the package version, runs
   the tests, builds, publishes with provenance and creates the GitHub release.
`````

- Produces:

  - `scripts/check-size.mjs`: exits 1 when the esbuild-minified, gzipped core FESM bundle (Angular/rxjs/tslib external) exceeds 15360 bytes

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing workflow-checker test**

`scripts/check-workflows.spec.mjs`:

````js
import { checkWorkflow } from './check-workflows.mjs';

const SHA = '3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1';
const EXPR = '$' + '{{ github.ref_name }}';

const good = [
  'name: ok',
  'on: push',
  'permissions: {}',
  'jobs:',
  '  build:',
  '    runs-on: ubuntu-latest',
  '    permissions:',
  '      contents: read',
  '    steps:',
  `      - uses: actions/checkout@${SHA} # v7.0.1`,
  '        with:',
  '          persist-credentials: false',
  '      - name: Use an env var',
  '        run: echo "$REF"',
  '        env:',
  `          REF: ${EXPR}`,
].join('\n');

describe('checkWorkflow', () => {
  it('accepts a hardened workflow', () => {
    expect(checkWorkflow(good, 'good.yml')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('flags actions pinned to a tag and SHAs without a version comment', () => {
    const tagPinned = good.replace(`actions/checkout@${SHA} # v7.0.1`, 'actions/checkout@v4');
    expect(checkWorkflow(tagPinned, 'w.yml')).toEqual(['w.yml:10: action not pinned to a commit SHA: actions/checkout@v4']);
    const noComment = good.replace(' # v7.0.1', '');
    expect(checkWorkflow(noComment, 'w.yml')).toEqual(['w.yml:10: add a "# vX.Y.Z" comment after the pinned SHA']);
  });

  it('flags a checkout that persists credentials', () => {
    const persisted = good.replace('          persist-credentials: false', '          fetch-depth: 0');
    expect(checkWorkflow(persisted, 'w.yml')).toEqual(['w.yml:10: actions/checkout needs "with: persist-credentials: false"']);
  });

  it('flags missing top-level and job permissions', () => {
    const noTop = good.replace('permissions: {}\n', '');
    expect(checkWorkflow(noTop, 'w.yml')).toEqual(['w.yml: missing top-level "permissions:"']);
    const noJob = good.replace('    permissions:\n      contents: read\n', '');
    expect(checkWorkflow(noJob, 'w.yml')).toEqual(['w.yml:5: job "build" has no "permissions:"']);
  });

  it('flags ${{ }} expansion inside run, inline and in a block', () => {
    const inline = good.replace('        run: echo "$REF"', `        run: echo "${EXPR}"`);
    expect(checkWorkflow(inline, 'w.yml')).toEqual(['w.yml:14: "${{ }}" expansion inside run:; pass it through env: instead']);
    const block = good.replace('        run: echo "$REF"', `        run: |\n          echo start\n          echo "${EXPR}"`);
    expect(checkWorkflow(block, 'w.yml')).toEqual(['w.yml:16: "${{ }}" expansion inside run:; pass it through env: instead']);
  });
});
````

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the checker and the size check**

`scripts/check-workflows.mjs`:

````js
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Workflow hygiene (spec §8, the zizmor findings on lingui-angular): every action pinned to a
 * commit SHA with a version comment, checkout with persist-credentials: false, a top-level
 * permissions block plus permissions on every job, and no ${{ }} expansion inside run:.
 */
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const indentOf = (line) => line.search(/\S/);

export function checkWorkflow(text, file = 'workflow.yml') {
  const problems = [];
  const lines = text.split('\n');

  lines.forEach((line, i) => {
    const m = /^\s*(?:-\s+)?uses:\s*([^\s#]+)\s*(#.*)?$/.exec(line);
    if (!m || m[1].startsWith('./')) return;
    if (!/^[\w.-]+\/[\w./-]+@[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(m[1])) {
      problems.push(`${file}:${i + 1}: action not pinned to a commit SHA: ${m[1]}`);
    } else if (!m[2]) {
      problems.push(`${file}:${i + 1}: add a "# vX.Y.Z" comment after the pinned SHA`);
    }
  });

  lines.forEach((line, i) => {
    if (!/uses:\s*actions\/checkout@/.test(line)) return;
    const stepIndent = line.trimStart().startsWith('-') ? indentOf(line) : indentOf(line) - 2;
    let ok = false;
    for (let j = i + 1; j < lines.length; j++) {
      if (lines[j].trim() === '') continue;
      if (indentOf(lines[j]) <= stepIndent) break;
      if (/^\s*persist-credentials:\s*false\s*$/.test(lines[j])) ok = true;
    }
    if (!ok) problems.push(`${file}:${i + 1}: actions/checkout needs "with: persist-credentials: false"`);
  });

  if (!lines.some((l) => /^permissions:/.test(l))) problems.push(`${file}: missing top-level "permissions:"`);
  const jobsAt = lines.findIndex((l) => /^jobs:\s*$/.test(l));
  if (jobsAt === -1) {
    problems.push(`${file}: no "jobs:" block`);
  } else {
    const starts = [];
    for (let i = jobsAt + 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
      if (/^\S/.test(lines[i])) break;
      if (/^ {2}[A-Za-z0-9_-]+:\s*$/.test(lines[i])) starts.push(i);
    }
    starts.forEach((start, k) => {
      const end = k + 1 < starts.length ? starts[k + 1] : lines.length;
      if (!lines.slice(start + 1, end).some((l) => /^ {4}permissions:/.test(l))) {
        problems.push(`${file}:${start + 1}: job "${lines[start].trim().replace(/:$/, '')}" has no "permissions:"`);
      }
    });
  }

  const expansion = '"${{ }}" expansion inside run:; pass it through env: instead';
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const m = /^\s*(?:-\s+)?run:\s*(.*)$/.exec(lines[i]);
    if (!m) continue;
    const runIndent = indentOf(lines[i]) + (lines[i].trimStart().startsWith('-') ? 2 : 0);
    if (m[1].includes('${{')) problems.push(`${file}:${i + 1}: ${expansion}`);
    if (/^[|>]/.test(m[1].trim())) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < lines.length; j++) {
        if (lines[j].trim() === '') continue;
        if (indentOf(lines[j]) <= runIndent) break;
        if (lines[j].includes('${{')) problems.push(`${file}:${j + 1}: ${expansion}`);
      }
    }
  }
  return problems;
}

if (process.argv[1] === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  const dir = join(fileURLToPath(new URL('..', import.meta.url)), '.github/workflows');
  const files = readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.yml') || f.endsWith('.yaml'));
  if (files.length === 0) {
    console.error('[check-workflows] FAIL: no workflow files');
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const problems = files.flatMap((f) => checkWorkflow(readFileSync(join(dir, f), 'utf8'), `.github/workflows/${f}`));
  for (const p of problems) console.error(`  FAIL  ${p}`);
  console.log(`[check-workflows] ${files.length} file(s), ${problems.length} problem(s)`);
  process.exit(problems.length === 0 ? 0 : 1);
}
````

`scripts/check-size.mjs`:

````js
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Spec §2: the core is at most 15 kB gzipped (minified, Angular/rxjs/tslib external). Run after build:lib. */
import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { gzipSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { build } from 'esbuild';

const LIMIT = 15 * 1024;
const entry = 'dist/ng-spotlight/fesm2022/tocdk-oss-ng-spotlight.mjs';

if (!existsSync(entry)) {
  console.error(`[check-size] ${entry} not found; run npm run build:lib first`);
  process.exit(1);
}
const result = await build({
  entryPoints: [entry],
  bundle: true,
  minify: true,
  format: 'esm',
  write: false,
  external: ['@angular/*', 'rxjs', 'rxjs/*', 'tslib'],
  logLevel: 'silent',
});
const bytes = gzipSync(result.outputFiles[0].contents, { level: 9 }).length;
console.log(`[check-size] core: ${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(2)} kB gzipped (limit ${LIMIT / 1024} kB)`);
process.exit(bytes <= LIMIT ? 0 : 1);
````

If N > 15, do not loosen the limit or drop features: report the number and the three largest contributors (`npx esbuild dist/ng-spotlight/fesm2022/tocdk-oss-ng-spotlight.mjs --bundle --minify --format=esm --external:@angular/* --external:rxjs --external:tslib --analyze --outfile=/dev/null`) to the operator.

`.github/workflows/ci.yml`:

````yaml
name: ci

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

permissions: {}

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  lint:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version-file: .nvmrc
          cache: npm
      - run: npm ci
      # The kitchen sink imports the generated (gitignored) example sources.
      - run: node scripts/generate-example-sources.mjs
      - run: npm run lint

  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version-file: .nvmrc
          cache: npm
      - run: npm ci
      - run: npm run test:coverage
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2
        if: always()
        with:
          name: coverage
          path: coverage/
          retention-days: 7

  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version-file: .nvmrc
          cache: npm
      - run: npm ci
      - run: npm run build:lib
      - run: npm run check:size
      - name: Verify the package layout
        run: |
          test -f dist/ng-spotlight/fesm2022/tocdk-oss-ng-spotlight.mjs
          test -f dist/ng-spotlight/fesm2022/tocdk-oss-ng-spotlight-primeng.mjs
          test -f dist/ng-spotlight/schema/spotlight-response.schema.json
          test -f dist/ng-spotlight/README.md
          test -f dist/ng-spotlight/LICENSE
      - run: npm run build:demo

  docs:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version-file: .nvmrc
          cache: npm
      - run: npm ci
      - run: npm run check:docs
      - run: npm run check:workflows
````

`.github/workflows/release.yml`:

````yaml
name: release

# Runs only when the operator pushes a version tag (see CONTRIBUTING.md "Releasing").
on:
  push:
    tags: ['v*']

permissions: {}

concurrency:
  group: release
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  publish:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    # Give this environment a required reviewer in the repo settings: the publish then waits for approval.
    environment: npm
    permissions:
      contents: write # gh release create
      id-token: write # npm provenance
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version-file: .nvmrc
          cache: npm
          registry-url: https://registry.npmjs.org
      - run: npm ci
      - name: The tag must match the library version
        run: |
          expected="v$(node -p "require('./projects/ng-spotlight/package.json').version")"
          if [ "$GITHUB_REF_NAME" != "$expected" ]; then
            echo "::error::tag $GITHUB_REF_NAME does not match $expected"
            exit 1
          fi
      - run: npm run test:coverage
      - run: npm run build:lib
      - run: npm run check:size
      - name: Publish to npm with provenance
        run: npm publish ./dist/ng-spotlight --provenance --access public
        env:
          NODE_AUTH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.NPM_TOKEN }}
      - name: Create the GitHub release
        run: gh release create "$GITHUB_REF_NAME" --verify-tag --generate-notes --title "$GITHUB_REF_NAME"
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
````

`.github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml`:

````yaml
name: deploy-pages

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions: {}

concurrency:
  group: pages
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version-file: .nvmrc
          cache: npm
      - run: npm ci
      # build:demo runs the example-source codegen (prebuild:demo) and sets --base-href "/ng-spotlight/".
      - run: npm run build:demo
      - name: SPA fallback so deep links survive a refresh
        run: cp dist/kitchen-sink/browser/index.html dist/kitchen-sink/browser/404.html
      - uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@fc324d3547104276b827a68afc52ff2a11cc49c9 # v5.0.0
        with:
          path: dist/kitchen-sink/browser

  deploy:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      pages: write
      id-token: write
    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment.outputs.page_url }}
    steps:
      - id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@368f82528645a54fb793d4d04e342629a3f51346 # v5.0.1
````
